# Public Rust API inventory
#
# Maintained by hand against the public items in rust/src, grouped by module:
# there is no generator, so a change that adds or retires a public name edits
# this file too. Use it to check a name before writing it into the
# documentation.

### yggdryl::arrow  [rust/src/arrow/mod.rs]
pub const IPC_DICTIONARY_IDS_KEY: &str = "YGGDRYL:ipc:dictionary-ids";
  pub enum Error
  pub fn is_source_failure(&self) -> bool  (`Arrow` and `External`, or a `Core` failure `crate::Error::is_source_failure` answers for)
  pub fn external(error: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self
  pub type BatchReader = Box<dyn arrow_array::RecordBatchReader + Send>;
  pub fn batch_reader<I>(schema: SchemaRef, batches: I) -> BatchReader where I: IntoIterator<Item = arrow_array::RecordBatch>, I::IntoIter: Send + 'static,
  pub fn combined_as(left: BatchReader, right: BatchReader, field: &Field, safe: bool) -> Result<BatchReader>
  pub fn combined(left: BatchReader, right: BatchReader) -> Result<BatchReader>
  pub fn from_reader_error(error: ArrowError) -> Error
  pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;

### yggdryl::arrow::size  [rust/src/arrow/size.rs]  (the one memory estimator every byte bound reads: a batch or an array costs the bytes its own slice reaches, a scalar what the row-to-batch builder charges it)
pub fn memory_size(batch: &RecordBatch) -> usize
pub fn array_memory_size(column: &ArrayRef) -> usize
pub fn scalar_memory_size(value: &Scalar) -> usize

### yggdryl::arrow::extension  [rust/src/arrow/extension.rs]  (arrow-rs's typed extension types for the datatypes that ride a name, every method redirecting into the recognizer Field::from_arrow_field imports through: the name over the storage the datatype lays out, a dictionary of it included, is that datatype, and any other storage or a document a parameter-free name states none of is refused)
impl ExtensionType for UuidType, VariantType, VersionType, TimezoneType, MimeTypeType, MediaTypeType, StateType, MarketDataKindType, MarketDataTypeType, SideType, TimeInForceType, CountryType, CcyType, MicType, CfiType, IsinType, CusipType, SedolType, FigiType, RicType, BbgType, UnitType, ForexType, LeiType, BicType, ElfType, DtiType, FisnType  (NAME the datatype identifier's arrow_extension_name, Metadata = (), no document)
impl ExtensionType for StringType  (NAME yggdryl.string, Metadata = Self: the leaf written whole as its extension_json document)
impl ExtensionType for BytesType  (NAME yggdryl.bytes, Metadata = Self)

pub enum Charset  (#[non_exhaustive])
  pub const ALL: [Self; 13] = [ Self::Utf8, Self::Utf16Le, Self::Utf16Be, Self::Ascii, Self::Latin1, Self::Latin2, Self::Latin9, Self::Cp1250, Self::Cp1251, Self::Cp1252, Self::Cp437, Self::Cp850, Self::MacRoman, ];
  pub const PARAMETER: &'static str = "charset";
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn is_utf8(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_unicode(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_ascii_compatible(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_single_byte(self) -> bool
  pub const fn unit_size(self) -> usize
  pub const fn bom(self) -> Option<&'static [u8]>

  pub fn from_url(value: &Url) -> Self
  pub fn from_content_type(value: &str) -> Result<Option<Self>>

  pub fn byte_of(self, scalar: char) -> Option<u8>

  pub fn decode_bytes_into(self, input: &[u8], target: &mut Vec<u8>) -> Result<()>

  pub fn encode_into(self, input: &str, target: &mut Vec<u8>) -> Result<()>
  pub const fn decoder(self) -> Decoder
  pub const fn transcriber(self) -> Decoder  (the same carry, reading each run as `transcribe` does; never refuses a byte)
  pub fn reader<'source, R: Read + 'source>(self, source: R) -> Box<dyn Read + 'source>
  pub fn writer<'target, W: Write + 'target>(self, target: W) -> Writer<'target>

### yggdryl::root::codec  [rust/src/codec.rs]
pub struct Level(u8);
  pub const NONE: Self = Self(0);
  pub const FAST: Self = Self(1);
  pub const DEFAULT: Self = Self(6);
  pub const BEST: Self = Self(9);
  pub const fn new(level: u8) -> Self
  pub const fn get(self) -> u8
  pub enum Codec
  pub const ALL: [Self; 5] = [ Self::Identity, Self::Gzip, Self::Zlib, Self::Deflate, Self::Zstd, ];
  pub const fn has_restarts(self) -> bool
  pub const fn restart_scan(self) -> RestartScan
pub struct RestartScan
  pub const fn consumed(&self) -> u64
  pub fn push(&mut self, chunk: &[u8], into: &mut Vec<u64>)
pub struct Restarts
  pub fn new(stride: u64, points: impl Into<std::sync::Arc<[u64]>>) -> Self
  pub const fn stride(&self) -> u64
  pub fn points(&self) -> &[u64]
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn before(&self, position: u64) -> (u64, u64)
pub struct Encoder
  pub const fn codec(&self) -> Codec
  pub fn restart(&mut self) -> Result<()>
  pub fn finish(self) -> Result<()>
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn extension(self) -> Option<&'static str>
  pub const fn is_identity(self) -> bool
  pub fn from_mime_type(value: &MimeType) -> Self
  pub fn from_media_type(value: &MediaType) -> Self
  pub fn from_url(value: &Url) -> Self
  pub fn load(self, input: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>>
  pub fn dump(self, input: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>>
  pub fn dump_with_level(self, input: &[u8], level: Level) -> Result<Vec<u8>>
  pub fn reader<'source, R: Read + 'source>(self, source: R) -> Box<dyn Read + 'source>
  pub fn writer<'target, W: Write + 'target>(self, target: W) -> Encoder<'target>
  pub fn writer_with_level<'target, W: Write + 'target>( self, target: W, level: Level, ) -> Encoder<'target>
  pub fn finish(self) -> Result<()>

### yggdryl::variant  [rust/src/variant.rs]  (the Apache Parquet Variant binary encoding, version 1: one metadata dictionary and one value payload)
pub const VARIANT_VERSION: u8 = 1;  (the specification version, the low nibble of the metadata header)
pub const VARIANT_EXTENSION_NAME: &str = "arrow.parquet.variant";  (the canonical Arrow extension over the struct of two binaries)
pub const VARIANT_METADATA_FIELD: &str = "metadata";  (the child holding the key dictionary)
pub const VARIANT_VALUE_FIELD: &str = "value";  (the child holding the value payload)
pub struct Variant  (Clone, Eq/Ord/Hash, Debug, Display = the JSON the value spells; the value of Scalar::Variant and what a `variant` column stores per row)
  pub fn new(metadata: impl Into<Arc<[u8]>>, value: impl Into<Arc<[u8]>>) -> Result<Self>  (bytes a medium stored; the metadata header, its dictionary and its offsets are read here, the value payload by `scalar`)
  pub fn encode(value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (the tree's object keys sorted into one dictionary, sizes the narrowest the payload allows; refuses what the standard cannot spell - a decimal past 38 digits, a clock that is not a whole microsecond, a zoned time or duration, a mapping keyed by anything but text)
  pub fn metadata(&self) -> &[u8]
  pub fn value(&self) -> &[u8]
  pub fn scalar(&self) -> Result<Scalar>  (the value the bytes hold, refused at the byte: a payload cut short, a primitive version 1 does not name, a field id past the dictionary, an object naming one key twice, bytes left over)

### yggdryl::valuestream  [rust/src/valuestream.rs]  (the crate's own byte stream: any Scalar, leaf for leaf, and back)
pub const VALUE_STREAM_VERSION: u8 = 0;  (the first byte of every stream)
pub const COMPRESS_FROM: usize = 4 * 1024;  (a payload of no fixed width past this many bytes is a zstd frame)
pub struct ValueStream  (Iterator<Item = Vec<u8>> + FusedIterator: one chunk per leaf, the children of a nested value after its header, holding what it has yet to write and nothing it wrote)

### yggdryl::root::datatype_id  [rust/src/datatype_id.rs]
pub enum DataTypeId
  pub const ALL: [Self; 97] = [all exact datatype identifiers in canonical declaration order, including Lei, Bic, Elf, Dti, Fisn at 0x6b..=0x6f, Figi, Decimal, BigDecimal, Ric at 0x7e and Forex at 0x7f, and ending State, MarketDataKind, Side, MarketDataType, TimeInForce, PluginSide at 0xc1..=0xc6];  (every discriminant is explicit; values are partitioned by DataTypeKind rather than declaration position; 0x75, 0x76 and 0x77 - side, state and timeinforce while each was a code - are retired and never reused; the code family took the text family's never-used tail 0x6a..=0x6f, so Text is 0x50..=0x69 and Code 0x6a..=0x7f)
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>  (an identifier's as_str name or one of its LEGACY_NAMES, ignoring case)
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str  (the serie family spells serie, serie_view, fixed_size_serie, large_serie, large_serie_view)
  pub const LEGACY_NAMES: [(&'static str, Self); 5]  (the names the serie family had before it took its own - list, list_view, fixed_size_list, large_list, large_list_view - read by every door that reads a datatype's name, written by none)
  pub fn from_legacy_name(value: &str) -> Option<Self>  (one of LEGACY_NAMES, ignoring case; nothing for any other word)
  pub const fn as_u8(self) -> u8  (one byte laid out by family: the family's own number first, `DataTypeKind::id`, then its leaves in the family's range; the tag the digest feed and the variant encoding write)
  pub const fn from_u8(byte: u8) -> Option<Self>  (the leaf one byte names; none for a family's own number, a placeholder in a family's range, or a byte past every family)
  pub const fn kind(self) -> DataTypeKind  (the family whose DataTypeKind::range the byte is in)
  pub const fn temporal_family(self) -> Option<&'static str>  (date, time, datetime, duration or interval: which of the five temporal families the leaf is in, the widths of one family sharing it; None outside DataTypeKind::Temporal)
  pub const fn arrow_extension_name(self) -> Option<&'static str>  (the one owner of which datatype states itself to Arrow by a name: arrow.uuid, arrow.parquet.variant, geoarrow.wkb, yggdryl.string for every string leaf but plain utf8/large_utf8/utf8_view, yggdryl.bytes for sized_binary and large_binary_view, and yggdryl.<name> for the fixed decimals, the version, URL, URN, timezone, MIME and media types, the six enum leaves and the seventeen codes; None for every datatype Arrow states alone; DataType::arrow_extension reads its name here)
  pub fn arrow_extension_names() -> impl Iterator<Item = &'static str>  (every distinct name arrow_extension_name answers, in ALL order - thirty-six; what a binding registers its runtime's extension types from)
  pub const fn is_parameterized(self) -> bool  (a leaf whose datatype carries more than its identity: the temporal units, the fixed and sized string and byte leaves, the nested, dictionary, decimal and geospatial ones; the unnumbered string and byte leaves are their identifier)
  pub const fn is_integer(self) -> bool  (DataTypeKind::Integer.contains(self))
  pub const fn is_signed_integer(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_unsigned_integer(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_floating(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_decimal(self) -> bool  (the four widths and the two fixed leaves)
  Decimal = 0x2d, BigDecimal = 0x2e  (the fixed `decimal` and `bigdecimal` leaves, the next bytes of the decimal family's range after Decimal256 = 0x2c; `as_str` `decimal` and `bigdecimal`; neither `is_parameterized`; `fixed_byte_width` 16 and 32)
  pub const fn is_temporal(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_binary(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_string(self) -> bool  (the eighteen string leaves)
  pub const fn fixed_byte_width(self) -> Option<usize>  (the numbers, a UUID; a code answers code_width instead)
  pub const fn code_width(self) -> Option<usize>  (the seventeen registered codes; the most bytes one value may be: elf 4, sedol and forex 7, ccy 8, cusip and dti 9, bic 11, lei 20, bbg, ric and unit 32, fisn 35)

### yggdryl::root::datatype_kind  [rust/src/datatype_kind.rs]
pub enum DataTypeKind
  pub const ALL: [Self; 13] = [ Self::Null, Self::Boolean, Self::Integer, Self::Floating, Self::Decimal, Self::Temporal, Self::Text, Self::Code, Self::Enum, Self::Bytes, Self::Nested, Self::Geospatial, Self::Uuid, ];  (Enum, `0xc0`-`0xcf`: a closed vocabulary stored as the unsigned code of its member, `uint8` or `uint16` by the leaf - `state`, `marketdatakind`, `side`, `marketdatatype`, `timeinforce`, `pluginside`)
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn id(self) -> u8  (the family's own number, the start of the range its leaves take and a placeholder no leaf takes - but the null family's, whose one leaf is the null itself)
  pub const fn last(self) -> u8  (the last byte of the range the family owns)
  pub const fn range(self) -> RangeInclusive<u8>  (id() ..= last(): a family is this range of DataTypeId bytes, laid out by hand and checked at compile time never to overlap)
  pub const fn contains(self, id: DataTypeId) -> bool  (whether id's byte is in range(); Scalar::is_integer, is_decimal, is_temporal, is_code and DataType::is_integer, is_code are this check)
  pub const fn of_u8(byte: u8) -> Option<Self>  (the family whose range a byte is in, or none past every family; one table lookup)
  pub const fn is_nested(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_wrapper(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_numeric(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_bytes(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_ordered(self) -> bool

### yggdryl::root::digest  [rust/src/digest.rs]
pub enum DigestAlgorithm

  pub const fn is_secretable(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_seedable(self) -> bool

  pub fn digester_with_seed(self, seed: u64) -> Digester
  pub fn digest(self, input: &[u8]) -> Digest
  pub struct Digest
  pub const fn new(algorithm: DigestAlgorithm, payload: u128) -> Self
  pub const fn algorithm(self) -> DigestAlgorithm
  pub const fn as_u32(self) -> Option<u32>

  pub const fn as_u128(self) -> Option<u128>
  pub const fn into_bytes(self) -> DigestBytes

  pub struct DigestBytes

  impl From<Xxh32>, From<Xxh64>, From<Xxh3>, From<Xxh128> for Digester  (a concrete state, fed bytes included, becomes the dispatcher)

### yggdryl::expression  [rust/src/expression/mod.rs]
pub const RECURSION_LIMIT: usize = 32
  pub const NODE_LIMIT: usize = 100_000
  pub enum Comparison
  pub const ALL: [Self; 8] = [ Self::Eq, Self::NotEq, Self::Lt, Self::LtEq, Self::Gt, Self::GtEq, Self::IsDistinctFrom, Self::IsNotDistinctFrom, ]
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str

  pub const fn flipped(self) -> Self
  pub const fn negated(self) -> Self

### yggdryl::fix::cfb  [rust/src/fix/cfb.rs]
impl FixRegistry
  pub fn from_cfb_file(handle: &dyn IOBase, dialect: Option<&str>) -> Result<(Self, Vec<Field>)>  (every field, group, component and message root the file produces is stamped a member of `dialect` in `FIX:sources` and the dialect's entry - its id and the handle's file name - held in the sources catalog, nothing standing in for an absent one and no entry held then; the root element's version and session are read past; the document decoded once in the charset its byte-order mark or declared `encoding` states, else UTF-8 - bytes that charset cannot read transcribed, an unknown charset read as UTF-8, each with a warning; `float` typed float64 and every other type word through the schema grammar, a word nothing reads typing the tag utf8; a tag the vocabulary leaves unnamed named by the one spelling its normalization bindings agree on and no other tag claims, else by its own decimal; an attribute that will not split dropped, one that will not unescape kept as spelled, a repeated one read once; everything else the reader cannot keep dropped or kept another way with one warn-level `log` record, `invalid cfb expression at byte N: line L, column C: expected X, got Y in "<element>"; <what the reader did>`, prefixed `[dialect] ` when one is named; a spelling - an alt, a normalization tag-name, an rg-name, a counter's spelling - filed under its catalog name, lower case with every run of characters outside [a-z0-9_.-] one `_` and `_` trimmed at both ends (`OTC Trade Flags` is otc_trade_flags), the spelling kept as display and one nothing of which folds leaving the tag named by its decimal; a map entry whose key or value is blank, `none` or `null` skipped as stating no code, named in one warning per code set listing up to 16 entries then `and N more`, and a map entry whose name another code claims dropped alone; a group or component a grammar declares structurally alike to one the catalog holds - the same members in order, each its tag and the field it reads, a nested group its counter over the same structure; never the member names, a `required`, an `rg-name` spelling, a description or the sources - read as that definition under its held name whatever the grammar named it, the held definition's members relaxed to the more permissive nullability where the two differ and every member the message read before the relaxation reading it again, so a name a message declares in several shapes is split `{name}_{message}`, then `{name}_{message}_2`, `_3`, in declaration order, only for a shape alike to nothing held; a second grammar-binding of one wire type folded into the first under merge_with's member rules, each member that still disagrees dropped with a warning; a message the catalog will not hold taking back the groups and components its walk wrote; only a document that is not well-formed XML or leaves an element open is refused)

### yggdryl::fix::field  [rust/src/fix/field.rs]
impl<'field> FixField<'field>
  pub fn sources(&self) -> Words<'field>  (the ids of the sources that contributed this field, folded and sorted, each naming an entry of FixRegistry::sources; empty for a field the specification alone defines, and for a stored text that is not the array the setter writes; lazy, nothing allocated)
  pub fn has_source(&self, source: &str) -> bool  (under the crate fold)
  pub fn id(&self) -> Result<Option<FixId>>  (derived from `FIX:tag` and the field's name, never stored)
  pub fn tag(&self) -> Result<Option<i32>>  (positive decimal digits only)
  pub fn tags(&self) -> Result<Vec<i32>>  (the FIX:tags array, `[1088,152]`; refuses a stored text that is not the compact array the setter writes, a tag that is not positive, or one named twice)
  pub fn names(&self) -> Words<'field>  (the FIX:names array, `["Qty","Quantity"]`, walked lazily; a stored text that is not the array the setter writes yields nothing)
  pub fn identifiers(&self) -> FixSpellings<'field>  (canonical direct scalar members in component order; borrowed)
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> FixSpellings<'field>  (the FIX:nulls spellings that mean nothing was sent, lazy; nothing where absent)
  pub fn is_null_value(&self, value: &str) -> bool  (ASCII case-insensitive against the trimmed text)
  pub fn description(&self) -> Option<&'field str>
  pub fn component(&self) -> Option<&'field str>
  pub fn field_ref(&self) -> Option<&'field str>
  pub fn deprecated(&self) -> Option<&'field str>  (the FIX:deprecated version at which FIX removed this field; the dictionary keeps it so an old message resolves, and a parse restates it under what replaced it and nulls the source)
  pub fn group(&self) -> Option<&'field str>
  pub fn msgtype(&self) -> Option<&'field str>
  pub fn msgcat(&self) -> Option<&'field str>  (the symbolic four-character `FIX:msgcat` business-category metadata a message definition declares; `MarketDataKind::from_name` resolves it, and the fixed row stores the member's `int32` code)
  pub fn counter(&self) -> Result<Option<i32>>  (positive decimal digits only)
  pub fn codeset(&self) -> Option<&'field str>  (the name of the FIX:codeset set this field's values are drawn from; the dictionary holds the members, and FixRegistry::codeset_of answers them)
  pub fn directions(&self) -> FixDirections<'field>  (tag 385's FIX:directions rules in document order; nothing where absent, which reads by the defaults)
  pub fn idmap(&self) -> FixIdSources<'field>  (the FIX:idmap entries in document order: the identifier map, key, follow flag and PartyID(448) role this field's value states; nothing where absent)
  pub fn parents(&self) -> Words<'field>  (the FIX:parents array of identifier types - folded words, nearest first - holding the parents of the identifier this field states, `["origclordid"]` on `ClOrdID(11)`; borrowed, nothing where absent; a registry answers it, or the name's, through `parents_of`)
  pub fn is_transient(&self) -> Result<bool>  (the FIX:transient flag, true where absent: whether this column travels from one message of a chain to the next, so a column settled to one message says false)
  impl FixFieldMut<'_>
  pub fn set_sources<I, S>(&mut self, sources: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>,  (each a non-empty id without a quote, a backslash or a control character - the id grammar of fix/source.rs - lowercased, deduplicated under the fold, stored sorted as a JSON array; empty removes; a refusal names FIX:sources and leaves the field unchanged)
  pub fn add_source(&mut self, source: &str) -> Result<()>  (idempotent under the fold)
  pub fn set_tag(&mut self, tag: i32) -> Result<()>  (any positive tag; zero is unresolved arrival provenance only)
  pub fn set_tags(&mut self, tags: &[i32]) -> Result<()>  (positive, distinct; stored as the compact JSON array; empty removes)
  pub fn set_names<I, S>(&mut self, names: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>,  (each non-empty and free of a quote, a backslash or a control character, distinct with ASCII case folded; stored as the compact JSON array; empty removes)
  pub fn set_identifiers<I, S>(&mut self, identifiers: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (resolve names, aliases or tags once against direct scalar members; atomic, empty removes)
  pub fn set_parents<I, S>(&mut self, parents: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (each spelling folded as an `IdType` folds it; a spelling no type folds from or a type listed twice refused, leaving the field unchanged; stored as the compact JSON array; empty removes)
  pub fn remove_parents(&mut self) -> Option<String>  (removes FIX:parents, answering what it held)
  pub fn set_nulls<I, S>(&mut self, spellings: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (comma-separated; a spelling holding the separator or repeating another under ASCII case folding is refused; an empty spelling is admitted)
  pub fn set_description(&mut self, value: impl Into<String>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn set_component(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_component(&mut self) -> Option<String>
  pub fn set_field_ref(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_field_ref(&mut self) -> Option<String>
  pub fn set_deprecated(&mut self, version: Option<&str>) -> Result<()>  (None removes FIX:deprecated; an empty version is InvalidMetadataValue)
  pub fn set_group(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_group(&mut self) -> Option<String>
  pub fn set_msgtype(&mut self, value: &str) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_msgtype(&mut self) -> Option<String>
  pub fn set_counter(&mut self, tag: i32) -> Result<()>  (positive tag)
  pub fn remove_counter(&mut self) -> Result<Option<i32>>
  pub fn set_codeset(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<()>  (names the set this field reads its values by; a name no store can file is InvalidRecord; empty removes; the members are stated with FixRegistry::set_codeset)
  pub fn remove_codeset(&mut self) -> Option<String>  (answers the name it held)
  pub fn set_directions(&mut self, directions: &[FixDirection]) -> Result<()>  (empty removes; refuses a code named twice under a folded spelling, an empty code, no pattern, an empty pattern, or a pattern regex::bytes refuses; the field is unchanged. Whether a code is one of the set is the dictionary's question: MsgDirection::from_registry resolves each against the set in force and drops one outside it with this setter's own refusal as the warning)
  pub fn remove_directions(&mut self) -> Result<Option<Vec<FixDirection>>>
  pub fn set_idmap(&mut self, sources: &[FixIdSource]) -> Result<()>  (empty removes; refuses a role that is not ASCII letters and digits, or one map and key twice - Error::Parse, the field unchanged; the stored key is the folded word an `IdType` spells, an upper-case key or an alias of a member refused when a document is read; a registry refuses a role on any tag but 448)
  pub fn marketdatatypes(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&'field str, MarketDataType)>  (the `FIX:marketdatatype` entries in document order: the wire value and the `MarketDataType` member this field's value types a message as; nothing where absent)
  pub fn set_marketdatatypes(&mut self, types: &[(&str, MarketDataType)]) -> Result<()>  (empty removes; refuses an empty wire value, one holding `=` or a character a word cannot, or one stated twice - Error::Parse, the field unchanged)
  pub fn timeinforces(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&'field str, TimeInForce)>  (the `FIX:timeinforce` entries in document order: the wire value and the `TimeInForce` member this field's value reads as; a word naming no member is passed over)
  pub fn set_timeinforces(&mut self, values: &[(&str, TimeInForce)]) -> Result<()>  (empty removes; refuses an empty wire value, one holding `=` or a character a word cannot, or one stated twice - Error::Parse, the field unchanged)
  pub fn set_transient(&mut self, transient: bool) -> Result<()>  (writes FIX:transient; true is the default and is stored as stated)
  pub fn merge_with(&mut self, other: &FixField<'_>) -> Result<()>  (the same identity's metadata folded in under the per-key rules; a disagreeing tag is a typed conflict naming both sides, and any refusal leaves the field unchanged)
  pub struct FixSpellings<'field>  (the comma-separated properties: identifiers and nulls)
  impl<'field> Iterator for FixSpellings<'field>  (Item = &'field str; also DoubleEndedIterator and FusedIterator)

  impl Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash for FixIdSource

  impl Iterator for FixIdSources<'_>  (Item = Result<FixIdSource>; fused after the first refusal)

### yggdryl::fix  [rust/src/fix/mod.rs]
pub struct FixId(i32)  (re-exported at the crate root beside FixFieldIter, FixKey, FixMsg, FixRegistry; the signed XXH32 of a field's tag and its folded name, derived on every read and never stored)
  pub const DEFINITION_TAG_MIN: i32  (the block a named definition's derived tag is taken from)
  pub const DEFINITION_TAG_MAX: i32
  pub const fn is_definition_tag(tag: i32) -> bool
  pub fn of(tag: i32, name: &str) -> Result<Self>  (refuses a nonpositive tag - zero is unresolved arrival provenance only; `MsgType`, `msgtype` and `Msg_Type` under one tag are one id)
  pub const fn digest(self) -> i32
  pub const fn from_digest(digest: i32) -> Self
  impl Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash for FixId
  impl fmt::Display for FixId  (the digest in decimal)

  impl<'a> From<&'a String> for FixKey<'a>

### yggdryl::fix::codec  [rust/src/fix/codec.rs]
pub const SOH: u8 = 0x01;
pub const DEFAULT_NULL_VALUES: [&str; 6] = ["", "null", "<null>", "none", "n/a", "[n/a]"];
pub struct FixCodec
  pub const DEFAULT_BATCH_BYTE_SIZE: u64 = 128 * 1024 * 1024;  (the raw bytes one Arrow batch targets; a target, not a ceiling, and a batch always holds one row)
  pub const DEFAULT_BATCH_ROW_SIZE: usize = 32 * 1024;  (the rows one Arrow batch targets; a batch closes on whichever of the two bounds it reaches first)
  pub const DEFAULT_OFFICIAL_TIME_DELAY_MS: i64 = 1_000;  (how far from SendingTime(52) an official transaction clock may stand and still date the message, when the caller states none)
  pub const DEFAULT_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS: i64 = 60_000;  (how long, in milliseconds of event time, the lifecycle remembers an identity it yielded, when the caller states none: one minute)
  pub fn new(registry: Arc<FixRegistry>) -> Self
  pub fn from_env() -> Result<Self>  (a codec over `FixRegistry::from_env()` sharing the process instrument registry `IsinRegistry::from_env()` names - the one constructor that attaches it; the caller commits it)
  pub const fn registry(&self) -> &Arc<FixRegistry>
  pub const fn default_sending_time(&self) -> Option<&Scalar>  (the sending clock of a message stating no SendingTime(52) whose carrier states none either - a row cell reaching tag 52, else the `currunix` of the text line it was read out of; None reads UTC now once; the raw-byte doors have no carrier)
  pub fn set_default_sending_time(&mut self, value: Option<Scalar>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn try_with_default_sending_time(self, value: Option<Scalar>) -> Result<Self>  (exact ns/UTC or None; initial intake only)
  pub fn direction(&self) -> Option<&str>  (the code of tag 385's set an unmarked line takes on the batch door; the set's Send code by default)
  pub fn msgdirection(&self) -> &MsgDirection  (the registry's reading of tag 385, the field's FIX:directions or the defaults, every pattern compiled once when the codec is built)
  pub const fn batch_byte_size(&self) -> u64
  pub fn payload_column(&self) -> &str
  pub const fn separator(&self) -> Option<u8>
  pub fn null_values(&self) -> &[String]
  pub const fn with_separator(mut self, separator: u8) -> Self
  pub fn with_payload_column(mut self, column: impl Into<SmolStr>) -> Self
  pub fn with_capture_names<I, S>(mut self, names: I) -> Self where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (what a run's row-header captures are called, resolved once and read by position)
  pub fn try_with_direction(mut self, code: Option<&str>) -> Result<Self>  (any spelling of a code of tag 385's set, resolved once; None or empty = no pin; a spelling outside the set refused naming it)
  pub fn with_source(mut self, id: &str) -> Result<Self>  (reads under one source of the dictionary - the catalog entry `id` names, `FixRegistry::get_source`, folded, resolved once - whose plugin's role every message the codec builds on every door states as its `msgpluginside`, the FIX rows `messages` rebuilds and every reader composed over it included; a row-header capture or a row cell named `msgpluginside` - the cell a FIX row carries - is the row's word over the stamp; a codec told no source stamps `UKNW`; an id the catalog does not hold is Error::Absent naming it)
  pub fn source(&self) -> Option<&str>  (the source the codec reads under, folded; None where none was named)
  pub const fn with_batch_byte_size(mut self, bytes: u64) -> Self
  pub const fn with_batch_row_size(mut self, rows: usize) -> Self  (zero leaves the bytes to decide)
  pub const fn batch_row_size(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn with_threads(mut self, threads: usize) -> Self  (the threads the line and row doors read on: available CPUs by default; zero reads as one and one pulls lazily. With more, the line, message-row and write doors use PARALLEL_CHUNK items at lane depth two and answer in input order; parse_text_arrow_reader and parse_arrow_messages hand each worker one job at lane depth one - an input batch, or a near-equal row range of one past twice PARALLEL_JOB_ROWS rows - and yield them in input order; lifecycle remains one ordered walk)
  pub const fn set_threads(&mut self, threads: usize)
  pub const fn threads(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn with_snapshot_ns(mut self, snapshot_ns: i64) -> Self  (the epoch-aligned lifecycle snapshot grid, each view the live message as of its tick, dated at it with the identity that instant derives; zero or less disables it)
  pub fn with_isin_registry(self, registry: Arc<Mutex<IsinRegistry>>) -> Self  (the instrument registry every lifecycle this codec runs shares and every parse fills from: each lifecycle learns what its messages state into it (`IsinRegistry::learn`) and fills what they leave unstated from it (`IsinRegistry::fill`), one lock per message across the two, so a walk run after another starts from what the first learned; each parse door fixes the registry's table once as it opens, under one lock on the calling thread, and every message it reads - a batch's entries included - takes from that table the security identifiers it leaves unsaid as derived identifiers, which reach no field, no wire and no part of its identity; a parse learns nothing; without one each walk learns into its own and a parse fills nothing)
  pub fn isin_registry(&self) -> Option<&Arc<Mutex<IsinRegistry>>>
  pub const fn snapshot_ns(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the positive grid width, where enabled)
  pub const fn with_sorted_lifecycle(mut self, sorted: bool) -> Self  (states that the messages `lifecycle` is handed arrive in instant order - a table read hour partition by hour partition, sorted by `currunix`: the walk then reads them as they come and holds one epoch hour at a time, a delivery's observations grouped across the hours still held, each hour folded and sorted exactly as a whole capture is and walked once the stream has read a message two hours past it - the hour of grace a transaction-dated message and a delivery's hops across an hour's end need; a message dated before an hour already walked is walked where it arrives, and a source failure is yielded once every hour still held is walked. Off by default: the capture is collected and sorted whole. On a sorted source both answer the same walk)
  pub const fn sorted_lifecycle(&self) -> bool  (whether the lifecycle reads its messages as they come, in instant order, one hour at a time)
  pub const fn with_official_time_delay_ms(mut self, official_time_delay_ms: i64) -> Self  (how far from SendingTime(52) an official transaction clock may stand and still date the message: the parse takes the TransactTime(60) it states, else the TrdRegTimestamp(769) of the TrdRegTimestamps(768) group whose TrdRegTimestampType(770) stamps the event or a hop, nearest the sending clock; a nonpositive delay admits only a clock equal to it)
  pub const fn official_time_delay_ms(&self) -> i64  (the delay in milliseconds; DEFAULT_OFFICIAL_TIME_DELAY_MS when unstated)
  pub const fn with_dedup_window_ms(mut self, dedup_window_ms: i64) -> Self  (how long, in milliseconds of event time, `lifecycle` remembers the curruuid of a message it yielded so it yields that identity once: a later message under a remembered identity is dropped, adjacent or not, a grid view exempt and a nil identity never a repeat; a nonpositive window remembers none)
  pub const fn dedup_window_ms(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the window in milliseconds, where the lifecycle remembers any)
  pub const fn with_market_metadata(mut self, market_metadata: bool) -> Self  (whether a market operation this codec builds carries, in its metadata, what its message states that no typed column reads and no identifier map of the leaf holds, and lifts into the set its type belongs to (`Operation::identifier_set`) each scalar of it whose key names an identifier - one its message's type declares under `FIX:identifiers`, or for a key no dictionary field is, one of the crate's identifier names; on by default; read by market_data, market_arrow_reader and book_arrow_reader; the map and what it lifts feed the leaf identity; the parties and the `Account(1)` stay the leaf's `partyids` either way)
  pub const fn market_metadata(&self) -> bool
  pub const PARALLEL_CHUNK: usize = 64  (the items in one lane chunk for the line, message-row and write doors; their lane depth is two)
  pub const PARALLEL_JOB_ROWS: usize = 256  (the fewest rows an Arrow capture job is cut to on several threads: a batch past twice this many rows is cut, zero-copy, into near-equal row ranges, four per thread at most; never joined)
  pub fn with_include_msgtypes<I, S>(mut self, spellings: I) -> Self where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (only these types are read; naming any clears the default refusals, since a caller saying what it wants has said what it does not; any spelling the dictionary resolves, a code it does not know kept as written, `unknown` naming a row that states no type)
  pub fn with_exclude_msgtypes<I, S>(mut self, spellings: I) -> Self where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (replaces DEFAULT_REFUSED_MSGTYPES; empty reads everything; a type named here is refused whether or not it is included)
  pub fn include_msgtypes(&self) -> &[SmolStr]
  pub fn exclude_msgtypes(&self) -> &[SmolStr]
  pub fn reads_msgtype(&self, spelling: &str) -> bool  (what the parse asks of the type a row states before it builds, and the walk of every message it is handed)
  pub fn with_null_values<I, S>(mut self, spellings: I) -> Self where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: Into<String>
  pub fn infer_msgtype_bytes(line: &[u8]) -> Option<&[u8]>
  pub fn infer_msgtype_text(line: &str) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn parse_line(&self, row: &[u8]) -> Result<FixMessages>  (none, one or many messages a row: one per frame the row opens, one `unknown` message with no entries for a JSON document the row carries and no frame, and none at all where the row opens no frame, states no bridge pair and carries no document; every message built is parsed whole - typed facts lifted, a nested XmlData row exploded into it, deprecated fields restated to their latest aliases and by the crate's native table of retirements, the crate's native derivations run - no registry carries a rule of its own - identifiers filled, the identity settled - and no later pass adds to it)
  pub fn parse_lines<I>(&self, lines: I) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<FixMsg>> + use<I> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: AsRef<[u8]>  (owns a codec clone; each message placed among the messages of its instant in stream order, `seqnum`; a line that is not a row, or a frame that builds no message, is left out with a deduplicated warning; only a source failure is an `Err` item; fuses source exhaustion)
  pub fn parse_fix_line(&self, body: &[u8]) -> Result<FixMsg>  (one frame; a body holding a second is refused at the byte it opens at - `invalid fix expression at byte N: expected one frame, got a second`; a clock naming no instant is left unstated beside an anomaly and a warning)
  pub fn parse_ullink_line(&self, body: &[u8]) -> Result<FixMsg>  (one bridge row; likewise refused, at the frame opening behind the row, target `ullink`)
  pub fn parse_fixml_line(&self, body: &[u8]) -> Result<FixMsg>  (one document; likewise refused, at a second root element, target `fixml`)
  pub fn parse_pairs<'a, I>(&self, pairs: I) -> Result<FixMsg> where I: IntoIterator<Item = (&'a [u8], &'a [u8])>  (one frame; likewise refused, at the second frame's key in the page the pairs were copied into)
  pub fn parse_text_line(&self, line: &TextLine) -> Result<FixMessages>  (takes the page the line already holds; copies nothing; states the line's `curruuid` as each message's one source - `srcuuids` - before it settles; the line's `mtime` - its `currunix` - fills each message's `recdunix` and is the sending clock of one stating no SendingTime(52) that no capture reaching tag 52 dated, ahead of `default_sending_time` and now, never re-emitted nor stated at the row's tag-52 column; none, one or many as `parse_line` answers, so a row opening no frame, stating no bridge pair and carrying no document yields nothing, and an empty payload nothing; malformed syntax may recover as one `unknown` message; a clock naming no instant is left unstated beside an anomaly and a warning, and a registry declaring a clock under another datatype is the one builder refusal left)
  pub fn parse_text_lines<I, L>(&self, lines: I) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<FixMsg>> + use<I, L> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<L>>, L: Borrow<TextLine> + Into<TextLine>  (owned or borrowed lines and either Result; an owned line moves, a borrowed one is read in place on one thread and cloned once to cross to a worker; each message placed among the messages of its instant in stream order, `seqnum`; a line whose message does not build is left out with a deduplicated warning, a source failure moves through unchanged; fuses source exhaustion)
  pub fn lifecycle<I>(&self, messages: I) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<FixMsg>> + use<I> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<FixMsg>>  (the one walk, `graph::EventIterator`, over the messages: collected first - or, under `sorted_lifecycle`, read as they come and held one epoch hour at a time; raw observations with the same complete nonempty capture `(msgtype, msgsessionid, msgctxid, msgseqnum)` - one `FixCapture::msgsesseventid` - ranked once over all of them by latest `recdunix`, a stated one before an unstated one, then `currunix`, and fully merged into the first with the reference leading scalar conflicts, older facts filling absences, and repeating-group occurrences merged recursively at equal indexes in FIX tag order; `execunix` and `recdunix` retain their earliest duplicate-observation values; retained messages stably sorted by instant - a capture's lines are not in their messages' order - then each placed among the messages of its instant and stated as following the live message - keeping its own place unless that happened at the same instant or later, where it takes the higher of its own and one past its place - carrying the lifecycle's latest `execunix`; missing `parentorderid` takes a different predecessor `orderid`, missing `clordid` takes the predecessor's, and stated links win; the chain is keyed on the cross identity every incarnation shares with a fallback on a shared identifier; a message arriving under the identity the live one arrived under - the same message logged at another hop - restates it instead; each curruuid yielded once within `dedup_window_ms` of event time, a grid view exempt; an item its source refused for what it states is left out with a warning, a source failure ends the intake - the messages read before it are walked and yielded, then the failure, once - and no failure advances the walk; a content merge, order link or side the rebuild refuses leaves the message as it stated itself, beside a warning; fused; a message whose sending clock the parse supplied is dated by its TransactTime(60) before the walk, `FixMsg::dated_by_transaction`)
  impl Clone for FixCodec
pub const DEFAULT_PAYLOAD_COLUMN: &str = "body";
pub const DEFAULT_REFUSED_MSGTYPES: [&str; 3] = ["0", "1", "unknown"];  (the types a codec refuses until a caller says otherwise: Heartbeat, TestRequest, and a row stating no type)

### yggdryl::fix::batch  [rust/src/fix/batch.rs]  (arrow feature)
impl FixCodec
  pub fn parse_text_arrow_reader(&self, source: BatchReader) -> Result<BatchReader>  (capture rows in, one FIX row per message out - a line carrying two frames is two rows and one carrying none is no row; the capture's columns lead and repeat over every message of a row, and the capture's own cells are read off the source row and carried by every message parsed out of it, stated again at their columns when the messages are written back; the row's `currunix` - the reader's `mtime` - becomes each message's precise `recdunix` and the sending clock of one stating no SendingTime(52), as `parse_text_line` reads the line; a carrier's seventeen event columns are the line each row is: its `curruuid` is every message's one source, `srcuuids`, and the other sixteen fill nothing and are dropped as the row's own twins take their names; batches close as `arrow_reader` closes them, on the bytes each row lands as. With several threads, whole input batches are at most one job per thread in flight and answer in input-batch order)
  pub fn lifecycle_arrow_reader(&self, source: BatchReader) -> Result<BatchReader>  (FIX rows in, chained FIX rows out under the same schema: each row through `FixMsg::from_row`, the ordered walk `lifecycle` runs, each back into a row; no re-parse. Each row's capture cells are read into the message it makes and travel with it through the walk's own ordering, then are stated again at their own columns, so the pairing is by message and never by position); the root keeps the source's metadata but its SORT:by, which the walk no longer proves
  pub fn market_data_arrow_reader(&self, source: BatchReader) -> Result<BatchReader>  (FIX rows in, MarketData::field rows out: messages into market_arrow_reader; a schema making no root refused before a row is read)
  pub fn parse_arrow_messages(&self, source: BatchReader) -> Result<impl Iterator<Item = Result<FixMsg>> + Send + use<>>  (capture rows in, the messages the rows carry out: each row read cell by cell and parsed through the line funnel - the payload, the beginstring and msgdirection columns, the currunix column - the reader's mtime - dating the row's line and becoming each message's `recdunix` and the sending clock of one stating no SendingTime(52), the carrier's `curruuid` column read as each message's one source where the carrier states one - else the identity the same bytes and instant derive - the other sixteen event columns filling nothing, every other column named after a dictionary field filling it - and the capture's own cells carried by every message parsed out of the row, under their columns; where each column sits is decided once from the schema; refuses a source with no payload column or one holding neither text nor bytes. With several threads, whole input batches are at most one job per thread in flight and answer in input-batch order)
  pub fn messages(&self, source: BatchReader) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<FixMsg>> + Send + use<>  (each row through `FixMsg::from_row` under the source's schema, the capture's own cells read into the message with it, so a `messages` -> `arrow_reader` round trip over a carrying schema states them again at their columns; `msgpluginside` the row's own cell where the schema holds the column and the role of the codec's `with_source` - `UKNW` under none - where it does not; one thread holds one batch at a time, while several retain 64-row chunks at lane depth two that can span source batches, in source order; a row or a batch the data refuses is left out with a deduplicated warning, and a source failure is yielded after the messages before it and ends the stream)
  pub fn arrow_reader<I>(&self, schema: Field, messages: I) -> Result<BatchReader> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<FixMsg>>, I::IntoIter: Send + 'static  (owned messages or results, each through `FixMsg::into_row` on the codec's threads in 64-item chunks at lane depth two; batches close on the bytes each row lands as - the leaves of every column it fills and a per-row width; a message the row will not hold is left out with a warning, a source failure is the completed prefix then the error and fusion)
  pub fn format_messages<'codec, 'field, I>(&'codec self, messages: I, field: &'field Field) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<Scalar>> + use<'codec, 'field, I> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<FixMsg>>  (each message through `FixMsg::into_row` under one message field of the registry; a projected column is read from its holder or residual entry record; a packed occurrence, a composed key and a row-header capture are the codec's readings and are not in the record to lift)
  pub fn format_arrow_reader(&self, source: BatchReader, field: &Field) -> Result<BatchReader>  (the Arrow twin; the capture's own columns still lead the row, stated from the cells each message carries; a source carrying no residual entry record is cast through `arrow::cast_reader` instead of read back as messages)
  pub fn write_arrow_reader(&self, source: BatchReader, sink: impl std::io::Write) -> Result<u64>  (semantic content rebuilt from projected columns and the residual entry record, then written with the codec's separator else `SOH`; refuses a batch without `fixentries`)
  pub fn lifecycle_serie(&self, source: impl Into<Serie>) -> Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (lifecycle_arrow_reader likewise; the source's root without SORT:by)
  pub fn market_data_serie(&self, source: impl Into<Serie>) -> Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (market_data_arrow_reader likewise, under MarketData::field)

  pub fn chunked_stream<I>(&self, schema: Field, messages: I) -> Result<StreamChunkedSerie> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<FixMsg>>, I::IntoIter: Send + 'static  (arrow_reader answered under StreamChunkedSerie::root_of(schema); a SORT:by it declares verified as records land)

### yggdryl::fix::messages  [rust/src/fix/messages.rs]
pub struct FixMessages
  impl Iterator for FixMessages  (Item = Result<FixMsg>; none, one or many a row - one per frame of a line, re-entering the frame reader at each frame's offset, one `unknown` for a document - each followed by the messages the parse splits off it, once: an execution report's fill as its order's report (`ORDR`, `QUOT` naming a QuoteID(117)) then the execution (`EXEC`, `FILLED`, chained under its ExecID(17)), a trade's one execution per side it states, a batch's (`MarketDataKind::is_batch`) one message of its `item` category per entry of its entry group - a quote batch's entry one `QUOT` holding both its legs - chained by the order the entry names, else its quote or order entry, else its place - each split again as its category is, each naming its source's identity beside its source's sources; a quote is never split - one message holds its bid and its offer, whatever side it tags; nothing collected either way; fused after exhaustion or the first error)
  impl FusedIterator for FixMessages

pub const CRATE_TAG_MIN: i32 = 65_000;
pub const CRATE_TAG_MAX: i32 = 65_100;  (one past the block; the crate defines 52 contiguous entries from 65_001 to 65_052; `fixmsg` is the separate fixed-row document at 65_053, with no retired tag keeping a slot)
pub const CURRUNIX_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_007, "currunix");  (non-null nanosecond UTC clock: when the event happened - a stated `currunix`, else the official transaction clock standing within the codec's official_time_delay_ms of `SendingTime(52)`, else that `SendingTime`)
pub const MSGCTXID_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_045, "msgctxid");  (the message context a bridge handled the line in; a capture fact, held by `FixCapture`)
pub const MSGPLUGINID_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_042, "msgpluginid");  (display `Message Plugin ID`: the plugin that logged the line, stated by a row's own `msgpluginid` capture or column and never derived; selects nothing)
pub const MSGPLUGINSIDE_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_043, "msgpluginside");  (required `pluginside`, display `Message Plugin Side`: the codec source's role, `UKNW` where no role is known)
pub const CURRHASHCODE_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_004, "currhashcode");  (non-null uint64: the XXH3-64 of what the event states, the text, the metadata, the lifted FIX fields and the named content of the row - every field but the standard header and trailer)
pub const CROSSHASHCODE_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_005, "crosshashcode");  (non-null uint64: the XXH3-64 of the stored cross code - `{kind}:{side}:{base}` - zero where the event names none)
pub const PREVUNIX_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_011, "prevunix");  (nullable clock: when the event this one follows happened)
pub const PREVUUID_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_013, "prevuuid");  (nullable uuid: the identity of the event this one follows)
pub const CREAUNIX_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_008, "creaunix");  (non-null clock: a stated creation, else `currunix`; the earliest a chain knows once walked)
pub const SNAPUNIX_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_012, "snapunix");  (nullable clock: the opening instant of the grid step a walk read the event as the snapshot of; a view is dated at it, its `currunix` the same tick)
pub const SOURCEURL_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_052, "sourceurl");  (the object a line was read from, typed url; the capture's own column and no column of the fixed row: declared by whoever read the line, carried in front of the row and by every message read out of it, stated again at its column by `into_row`; held as no fact, so outside the currhashcode, the entries and the wire)
pub const MSGSESSIONID_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_046, "msgsessionid");  (display `Message Session ID`: the session instance a bridge handled a line on, as its own row header brackets it and never what the message states; a capture fact)
pub const CURRUUID_TAG_NAME: (i32, &str) = (65_001, "curruuid");  (non-null uuid: the event's identity, the UUIDv7 with its millisecond `currunix`, `seqnum` in the 12-bit ordered lane, and a 62-bit XXH3 payload over `currhashcode` plus the whole sequence seeded by `crosshashcode`; sequences above 4095 saturate in the ordered lane but remain in the payload)

### yggdryl::fix::identity  [rust/src/fix/identity.rs]
pub const FIX_TYPED_TAGS: [i32; 35]  (re-exported at the crate root; every tag a message holds typed outside the crate's own block, in the order the wire states them - the header 8, 35, 49, 56, 34, 43, 52 and 385, the seventeen lifted 6, 11, 14, 17, 31, 32, 37, 38, 41, 44, 53, 117, 131, 151, 198, 262 and 1003, the trailer 93, 89 and 10, then Text(58); a caller walking a message's typed facts reads this rather than a list of its own, which drifts silently the moment a tag is lifted or retired. The crate's own tags are the contiguous block CRATE_TAG_MIN..CRATE_TAG_MAX, minus the sourceurl no message holds)
pub struct FixHeader  (the standard header and trailer facts a message holds typed, beside its row - tags 8, 35, 49, 56, 34, 52, 43, 385 and the trailer 93, 89, 10; the frame is the one band the wire emits out of tag order, the header first and the trailer last; re-exported at the crate root beside FixCapture; no public constructor)
  pub fn beginstring(&self) -> &str  (empty where the message stated none)
  pub fn msgtype(&self) -> &str
  pub fn sendercompid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn targetcompid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub const fn msgseqnum(&self) -> Option<u64>
  pub const fn sendingtime(&self) -> i64  (nanoseconds since the Unix epoch, UTC: what the message stated, else its carrier's - a row cell reaching tag 52, else the `currunix` of the text line it was read out of - else the codec's default sending time, else one UTC-now read at intake; the raw-byte doors have no carrier)
  pub const fn stated_sendingtime(&self) -> bool  (whether the message stated it; only a stated one goes back on the wire)
  pub const fn possdupflag(&self) -> Option<bool>
  pub fn msgdirection(&self) -> Option<&str>  (a code of tag 385's set)
  pub const fn signaturelength(&self) -> Option<i32>  (`SignatureLength(93)`, where the message was signed)
  pub fn signature(&self) -> Option<&[u8]>  (`Signature(89)`)
  pub fn checksum(&self) -> Option<&str>  (`CheckSum(10)` exactly as the line spelled it, three digits and all: a re-emission states what was read rather than a sum of bytes nobody sent)
  impl Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq for FixHeader
pub struct FixCapture  (what a bridge's own row header stated about the line it wrote, typed and read off the line's own bytes - the plugin, the role of its dialect's plugin, message context and session instance: capture facts outside the entries and wire. Where message type, session, context and sequence are stated, `msgsesseventid` is the four joined by `:`; that derived capture key is excluded from the FIX content identity. What a reader said about the line is carried rather than stated; no public constructor)
  pub fn msgpluginid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub const fn msgpluginside(&self) -> PluginSide  (the role of the FIX plugin whose session produced the message - `BUYS`, `SELL`, or `UKNW` where the codec read under no source or one stating no role; never absent, the codec's stamp at build, a row cell's word over it)
  pub fn msgctxid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn msgsessionid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn msgoriginator(&self) -> Option<&str>  (the plugin a bridge's log line names the message as coming through)
  pub fn conversationid(&self) -> Option<&str>  (a stated CONVERSATIONID, else the log line's `{conversationId: ...}`)
  pub fn msgsesseventid(&self) -> Option<&str>  (`<MsgType>:<msgsessionid>:<msgctxid>:<MsgSeqNum>` with no length prefixes - `8:e7256476:9effef3e6a:1094` - where all four are stated, else none; derived by the message whenever it settles; the fixed row's `msgsesseventid` column, `by_tag(65045)`)
  impl Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq for FixCapture
pub struct FixLifted  (the FIX fields a message lifted out of its row and holds typed: eight exact numbers - Price(44), OrderQty(38), Quantity(53), LastPx(31), LastQty(32), AvgPx(6), CumQty(14), LeavesQty(151) - and nine identifiers - ClOrdID(11), OrigClOrdID(41), OrderID(37), SecondaryOrderID(198), ExecID(17), QuoteID(117), QuoteReqID(131), MDReqID(262), TradeID(1003). Every slot is an Option, and absent means the message never said: nothing writes here but a tag, so a slot's presence is the whole test of whether the fact reaches the wire, the residual entry record or the code the message digests to. What the message *implies* about its market is the `Market` getters' to answer off these and off the row, and reaches none of the three; re-exported at the crate root; no public constructor)
  pub const fn price(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn orderqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn quantity(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (its own slot rather than a second name for OrderQty: a line that said `53=` re-emits `53=`, and a row keeps one column per tag)
  pub const fn lastpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn lastqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn avgpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn cumqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub const fn leavesqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>
  pub fn clordid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn origclordid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn orderid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn secondaryorderid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn execid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn quoteid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn quotereqid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn mdreqid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn tradeid(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn lastspotrate(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (`LastSpotRate(194)`; `lastforwardpoints` (195), `bidspotrate` (188), `bidforwardpoints` (189), `offerspotrate` (190), `offerforwardpoints` (191) the same - the six FX parts of a price, held in one boxed `LiftedFx` allocated on the first stated part)
  impl Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq for FixLifted
pub struct LiftedFx  (the six FX parts `FixLifted` holds boxed; re-exported beside it, no public constructor)

### yggdryl::fix::msg  [rust/src/fix/msg.rs]
pub struct FixMsg  (a market operation event with a FIX body around it: the crate-private event facts the graph traits answer, the typed `FixHeader` carrying the frame, the typed `FixLifted` carrying the FIX numbers and identifiers the message lifted, the typed `FixCapture` holding the line's own bridge row header, `Text(58)`, a bridge's namespaced metadata, and the content row - every child the message states beyond those typed facts, typed by the registry's field for it; each fact held once. Every market fact the traits answer is *derived* from the lifted facts and the row, so it reaches neither the wire, the residual entry record nor the code the message digests to - only what the message stated does)
  impl From<FixMsg> for Result<FixMsg>  (Ok wrapper; permits infallible and fallible stream intake through one standard conversion)
  pub fn stable_hash(&self) -> u64
  pub fn new(field: Field, value: Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (links `FixRegistry::from_env()`)
  pub fn with_registry(registry: Arc<FixRegistry>, field: Field, value: Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (the value canonicalized under the root; a child stating a typed fact - one of `FIX_TYPED_TAGS` or a crate column - fills its holder and leaves the row; a child named under a namespace, `TECH.CLIENTID`, lands in the metadata; the clocks are settled and the identity derived; runs no derivation)
  pub const fn header(&self) -> &FixHeader  (the standard header and trailer)
  pub const fn capture(&self) -> &FixCapture
  pub fn anomalies(&self) -> &[FixAnomaly]  (what the message states that its reading could not take as it stands, in arrival order - the parse's, then the last settle's: a value that will not type, an alias stating another value than the field it lost to, a dropped conflicting identifier or identifier-map value, a `#`-twin stating another value, a contradicted conversation, a non-ISO MIC alias, a later observation's provenance; never a column or a digest input)
  pub const fn lifted(&self) -> &FixLifted  (the FIX numbers and identifiers the message lifted, exactly as it stated them; a fact the `Market` getters answer but this holder lacks is derived, which is why it reaches neither the wire nor the code)
  pub fn text(&self) -> Option<&str>  (`Text(58)`)
  pub const fn metadata(&self) -> &BTreeMap<SmolStr, SmolStr>  (a bridge's namespaced keys, folded, in sorted order; empty where it stated none)
  pub fn entries(&self) -> &[FixEntry]  (the content row read as a tree, derived on the first ask and dropped by every write: one entry per non-null child, a group as one entry filed under its counter's tag valued its length as text, the count no field of its own, with one valueless entry per occurrence heading the members, a component as a valueless entry heading its members; the typed facts are not entries)
  pub fn into_bytes(&self, separator: u8) -> Vec<u8>  (the message as it now stands: header tags 8, 35, 49, 56, 34, 43 and 52 where stated, then the lifted facts in tag order - 6, 11, 14, 17, 31, 32, 37, 38, 41, 44, 53, 117, 131, 151, 198, 262, 1003 - then the entries pre-order, then the trailer 93, 89, 10, which closes the frame whatever the body says. A coded fact spells as its wire code - `54=1`. A fact the message derived rather than stated is emitted nowhere)
  pub fn into_text(&self, separator: char) -> Result<String>  (InvalidRecord when a value holds a control byte)
  pub fn digest(&self) -> u128  (the XXH3-128 of what `into_bytes` emits in wire order, whatever separator either message was read with; what `FixDedup` keys on. `Element::get_currhashcode` is distinct: it frames and canonically sorts named sibling fields in the entry tree while preserving repeated occurrence order, so direct and reconstructed rows share one event identity.)
  pub const fn registry(&self) -> &Arc<FixRegistry>
  pub const fn as_field(&self) -> &Field  (the content row's root Struct: no typed fact in it)
  pub const fn as_value(&self) -> &Scalar  (the content row)
  pub fn get_by_id(&self, id: FixId) -> Option<Scalar>  (owned; a typed field's identity answers its holder, any other the child the identified field's name reaches)
  pub fn by_id(&self, id: FixId) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get_by_tag(&self, tag: i32) -> Option<Scalar>  (a typed tag answers the holder's fact - `by_tag(35)` the type as text, `by_tag(52)` the sending clock as `datetime64(ns, UTC)`, `by_tag(54)` the side as `BUY`/`SELL`, a crate tag its column's type - but 65051, the capture's own column, which no message holds and which therefore answers nothing - `CURRHASHCODE_TAG_NAME` a UInt64, `CURRUUID_TAG_NAME` a Uuid - or nothing where the holder states none; any other tag the child carrying it, else the child named as the dictionary names the tag, else the child named by the tag's decimal)
  pub fn by_tag(&self, tag: i32) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get_by_name(&self, name: &str) -> Option<Scalar>  (folds through the registry to the canonical spelling - a typed field answers its holder - then an exact root-child match)
  pub fn by_name(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<Scalar>  (an indexed segment takes one occurrence: `Parties[0].PartyID`)
  pub fn by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get<'key>(&self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Option<Scalar>  (a name that reaches nothing and spells more than one segment is read as a path)
  pub fn value<'key>(&self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn set<'key>(&mut self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (a key reaching a typed fact records it on its holder, a Null clearing it; any other key writes the row, typed by the field the key reaches, replaced in place or appended; a bare unknown tag appends a nullable utf8 child named by its decimal; a key reaching the capture's own column - sourceurl, by tag or by name - is refused, naming the column, because a message holds no fact for it and a row child would put it on the wire; a name nothing reaches is refused and the message unchanged; every write settles the identity again)
  pub fn set_many<'key, I, K>(&mut self, values: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator<Item = (K, Scalar)>, K: Into<FixKey<'key>>  (several `set`s with one rebuild; the later of two writes to one child lands; one refusal refuses them all)
  pub fn with_value<'key>(self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>, value: Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (the consuming twin of `set`)
  pub fn remove<'key>(&mut self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>  (a typed fact cleared on its holder, a row child taken out, either answered; None for a key reaching nothing, a capture column among them - which `set` refuses rather than answering None)
  pub fn from_row(registry: Arc<FixRegistry>, schema: &Field, row: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (in schema.rs; the inverse of `into_row`: the typed facts read off their columns, content rebuilt from projected typed columns plus residual `fixentries` - each entry typed as the builder types a pair, a group no dictionary declares rebuilt as the serie it is; every capture column is carried - the one the crate tags, sourceurl, and every column no tag and no counter names, each non-null cell under its column's name - and none reaches an entry, a digest or a `body=` on the wire; a column whose name holds a `.` is a bridge's own statement and lands in the metadata; `into_row` states each carried cell again at its column; a row without that column rebuilds its projected typed facts; `from_row` then `into_row` preserves the canonical row and semantic identity, not original arrival order or wire spelling; refuses a row the schema does not hold, a `fixentries` key that is neither a resolved field's `tag:name` nor a captured `0:<key>` - its name reaching a field its tag does not answer to, as its own or as the counter heading the group it names, included - or a value whose JSON does not decode, and an identifier column - `securityids`, `identifiers`, `partyids` - holding a key that reads as none, a value its type refuses or two spellings of one key with two values, at that column, a read map closed by the base rule; a key the row's `metadata` states under no namespace, or a `0:<key>` entry of `fixentries`, is a key no dictionary resolved and is restored as the message's own entry - its text, or the entries its JSON holds - so the wire re-emits it and the digest is the parse's, while a namespaced key, `TECH.CLIENTID`, stays metadata)
  pub fn into_row(&self, schema: &Field) -> Result<Scalar>  (in schema.rs; each column filled by its tag or its group's counter, a column carrying neither by the child of its name; the capture's own columns answer the cell the message carries under the column's name, null where it carries none, because no message holds a fact for them, a value a nullable column will not hold as that column's null; tag 52 only where the message stated it; the residual entries close the row under `fixentries`, keyed `tag:name`; a root entry no dictionary resolved is stated in `metadata` under its own spelling instead, unless an identifier map holds it with its value - captured, it rides `fixentries` under `0:<key>` as it arrived; a schema with a `metadata` column and no `fixentries` keeps every one in `metadata`)
  pub fn dated_by_transaction(self) -> Self  (never refuses: a step the enriching pass cannot write is warned about and the message keeps what it stated; the message dated by the TransactTime(60) it states with a clock where the parse supplied its sending clock - a carrier's, the default, the intake's - created at an earlier OrigSendingTime(122), its stand-in sending clock moved with it, and enriched again under the rules its date selects; a stated SendingTime stands, a day-only TransactTime dates nothing; what `lifecycle` reads before it walks)
  pub fn carried(&self) -> &[(SmolStr, Scalar)]  (the capture's own cells the message carries, each under the column it was read from: where the line was read from, its place in the object, the body it was cut from, when it was recorded; provenance and never content - none is an entry, none reaches the wire or the currhashcode - and `into_row` states each again at its column; empty for a message parsed from bytes, a row's for one parsed out of or read back from a row)
  pub fn set_carried(&mut self, cells: Vec<(SmolStr, Scalar)>)  (states the capture's own cells, replaced whole)
  impl Element, Event, Market, Operation for FixMsg  (through the held event facts: `is_after` is the instant, `finalize` settles the identity again, `with_previous` is `Operation::following_operation` - the market facts the chain is about, the time in force and whether it trades where unstated, each identifier whose `FIX:idmap` entry follows, each followed type's parents with it (`is_followed_identifier`), and every party id of the chain - each identifier it states taking the parents its chain gave it (`Identifiers::follow_parents`, read through `FixRegistry::parents_of`) - and every `metadata` key of the chain it does not state, its own values standing - recording the predecessor as `prevuuid` and `prevunix`, keeping its own `seqnum` unless the predecessor happened at its instant or later, where it takes the next, while the sources stay its own, `merge_with` is `Operation::merging_operation_event` and `restating` `graph::market::restating_operation`, which takes the same as following does; every market setter forces its fact over the derivation; the identifier facts are logical - read off the fields, the wire kept as sent: `get_securityids` is rebuilt at every settle from `SecurityID(48)` under `SecurityIDSource(22)` (the base key of its type), each `SecAltIDGrp(454)` occurrence, an unmapped entry whose key names a security type (`Identifier::from_key`: `OMS_InstrumentID` is `oms:instrumentid`, a bridge's keyed alias `ISINCODE` or `OMS_RICCODE` its type, a value its type refuses an anomaly, the first stated code under a key kept unless a later one outranks it) and the crated ISIN/Bloomberg/FIGI/forex views, resolved once when the message is whole (`ISINCODE_TAG_NAME`), and its `set_`/`insert_`/`remove_` verbs state the set itself, writing no field; `get_identifiers` is rebuilt from the fields the registry's `FIX:idmap` entries name (`FixRegistry::idmap_sources`), and from each regulatory trade id - `NoRegulatoryTradeIDs(1907)` and a side's `NoSideRegulatoryTradeIDs(1971)` - under `regtradeid`, `prevregtradeid`, `blockregtradeid`, `relatedregtradeid`, `clearedregtradeid`, `tvtic` or `reporttrackingnumber` by its type, else `regtradeid{n}`, each its type's base key, and from each unmapped entry whose key names an operation identifier (any non-security, non-party type of `Identifier::from_key`); an unmapped entry a set holds with its value is captured - it leaves the metadata, rides a row's `fixentries` under `0:<key>` and stays on the wire; `OrigClOrdID(41)` is the `clordid`'s `origclordid` parent and a finalize fills each parent's own type from its nearest parent (`Identifiers::fill_parents`); `get_partyids` is rebuilt at every settle from the parties of `NoPartyIDs(453)` and `NoRootPartyIDs(1116)` - each `PartyID` typed by its `PartyRole`'s code-set name folded (`executingtrader`, `customeraccount`), `partyrole{n}` for a wire code the set names nothing for, `party` where none is stated, and sourced by its `PartyIDSource`'s name folded, a word the set does not resolve its own spelling folded and a bare code it names nothing for - one character, or digits - `partyidsource{code}`, the base source where none is stated, a named source filling the role's base key - and from `Account(1)` typed `account` from its `AcctIDSource(660)`'s name by the same rule, `acctidsource{code}` for a code the set names nothing for, and from each unmapped entry whose key names a party type, a side's own party ids and account first, the first value of a key standing unless a later one outranks it (`IdType::rank`) and a second staying on the wire; a set a caller or a row stated keeps every value it holds and is filled at each settle with what the fields state that it lacks; `get_execunix` reads the crate's `execunix` (65_023); `get_side` is `UKNW` where no `Side(54)` is stated, `BOTH` on a quote holding both legs and tagging neither; `marketdatakind` is the category its type files under (the dictionary's `FIX:msgcat`, `UKNW` where it files none) - an execution report of no fill its order's `ORDR`, or its quote's `QUOT` naming a `QuoteID(117)` - and `is_sided` that kind's `MarketDataKind::is_sided`, the cross code stored as `{kind}:{side}:{base}` for every kind (`Market::stored_crosscode`), the side `0` unless sided; `get_bidpx`, `get_bidqty`, `get_askpx` and `get_askqty` read `BidPx(132)`, `BidSize(134)`, `OfferPx(133)` and `OfferSize(135)`, and `get_bidccy`/`get_askccy` a stated `BidCurrency`, else `AskCurrency` or `OfferCurrency`, else the message's currency where that side states a price or a size - a quote one message holding both legs, whatever side it tags; no field fills `get_fxrates`, which answers what a caller set; `is_execution` is the category `EXEC` and a report of an execution - explicit execution-like ExecType(150), an initial AE when 487 is absent/New even if 150 is absent, never a non-New/cancel/correct/reverse/status AE or AD/AQ/AR - so the execution a parse splits off an order's, a quote's or a trade's report is one, and the report it was split from - restated `ORDR`/`QUOT`, or the `TRAD` trade - is not)
  impl Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash for FixMsg  (equal over the holders, the row and the registry - the same Arc or equal registries; hashed over the currhashcode, the row's schema and its value)
