### yggdryl::fix::catalog  [rust/src/fix/catalog.rs]
impl FixRegistry
  pub fn get_msgtype(&self, spelling: &str) -> Option<&MsgType>  (one code namespace: the code's first holder answers a bare code; a name reaches the message of that name)
  pub fn msgtype(&self, spelling: &str) -> Result<&MsgType>
  pub fn get_codeset(&self, name: &str) -> Option<FixCodeSet<'_>>  (the code set held under the folded name; the lenient door beside codeset)
  pub fn codeset(&self, name: &str) -> Result<FixCodeSet<'_>>  (Error::Absent over "codesets" where none is held)
  pub fn codeset_of(&self, field: &Field) -> Option<FixCodeSet<'_>>  (the set a field's FIX:codeset names; the one door between a field and its vocabulary)
  pub fn codesets(&self) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = FixCodeSet<'_>>  (every set held, in name order)
  pub fn set_codeset(&mut self, name: &str, codes: &[FixCode]) -> Result<()>  (states the members, replacing what the name held; rendered canonically in the caller's order; two codes sharing a name is Error::InvalidRecord at codes[i] naming both names and both values, and so is an empty value or name - except a `none`, `null` or blank name, which names nothing and so claims nothing; a name no store can file is InvalidRecord; an empty slice removes the set and is a Conflict while a held field names it; `marketdatakindcodeset` and `marketdatatypecodeset` are intrinsic and accepts only the unchanged canonical document)
  pub fn merge_codeset(&mut self, name: &str, codes: &[FixCode]) -> Result<()>  (folds by wire value, keeping what the set already held: a placeholder name yields to a real one, every surviving spelling stays an alias, and a code arriving with a new value under a name another code claims - by a lookup, or by the rule rendering holds names to - keeps its value under no name, logged at warn level, and a `none`, `null` or blank name claims nothing; `marketdatakindcodeset` and `marketdatatypecodeset` accept only canonical-preserving no-op subsets and refuses a remap or widening even when the ordinary fold would discard it)
  pub fn remove_codeset(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<Option<Vec<FixCode>>>  (answers the members it held; a Conflict naming the first field that reads by it, because a field may not be left naming a vocabulary nothing states; intrinsic `marketdatakindcodeset` is never removable)
  pub fn derived_codeset_name(field: &Field) -> SmolStr  (the name a field's own set is filed under where the specification names none: the folded field name and "codeset")
  pub fn get_definition(&self, category: FixCategory, name: &str) -> Option<&Field>  (one category's folded name; Fields answers the scalar of that name)
  pub fn definition(&self, category: FixCategory, name: &str) -> Result<&Field>  (absence reported with its category)
  pub fn definitions(&self, category: FixCategory) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Field>  (one category in the registry's own order, nothing collected; the field doors above reach a definition whatever category it is filed under, and these are the same definitions scoped to one)
  pub fn insert_definition(&mut self, category: FixCategory, field: Field) -> Result<Option<Field>>  (inserts or replaces, references validated before mutation, answering what it replaced; a Fields category is `insert`; a folded input name keeps the stored canonical spelling)
  pub fn create_definition(&mut self, category: FixCategory, field: Field) -> Result<()>  (refuses an existing name or wire identity atomically; creation reserves canonical identities only, so another field's alias may hold the spelling and a tag another field holds under another name is free - the identity is the pair)
  pub fn update_definition(&mut self, category: FixCategory, field: Field) -> Result<Field>  (replaces an existing definition and answers its previous value; refuses absence, and refuses a Fields replacement that changes the FIX identity)
  pub fn remove_definition(&mut self, category: FixCategory, name: &str) -> Result<Option<Field>>  (None when absent; refused while another definition still references it)

### yggdryl::fix::registry  [rust/src/fix/registry.rs]
pub struct FixRegistry  (one namespace of scalar fields, components and groups, every one reached through the field doors below; a message is a component carrying FIX:msgtype)
  pub fn stable_hash(&self) -> u64
  pub fn msgdirection(&self) -> MsgDirection  (the reading of tag 385 as the registry holds it now - its set and its FIX:directions rules, the defaults where the field carries none)
  pub fn new() -> Self  (the crate's own definitions, then `SendingTime(52)` and `TransactTime(60)` seeded as nanosecond UTC clocks)
  pub fn idmap_sources(&self) -> &[(i32, FixIdSource)]  (every field's FIX:idmap entries beside its tag, in tag order, compiled once and forgotten by every change to the fields; what a message rebuilds its identifiers from)
  pub fn parent_sources(&self) -> &[(IdType, Box<[IdType]>)]  (every field's FIX:parents list under the identifier type its field states - its idmap key, else its folded name - compiled once and forgotten by every change to the fields; a field named as another's parent (`parent` or `orig` before its name) is listed on that field wherever fields arrive, the committed dictionary stating `clordid` -> `[origclordid]` among them)
  pub fn parents_of(&self, base: &IdType) -> Cow<'_, [IdType]>  (the parent types of `base`, nearest first: the list a field stating it names under FIX:parents, else `IdType::parents`)
  pub fn parent_of(&self, kind: &IdType) -> Option<(IdType, usize)>  (the base `kind` is a parent of and its place among the base's parents: a stated list first, else `IdType::parent_of` for a base that states none)
  pub fn marketdatatype_sources(&self) -> &[(i32, SmolStr, MarketDataType)]  (every `FIX:marketdatatype` wire value of every field beside its tag and member, compiled once and forgotten by every change to the fields)
  pub fn timeinforce_sources(&self) -> &[(i32, SmolStr, TimeInForce)]  (every `FIX:timeinforce` wire value of every field beside its tag and member, compiled once and forgotten by every change to the fields)
  pub fn timeinforce_of(&self, tag: i32, wire: &str) -> Option<TimeInForce>  (the member one field's wire value reads as: a registry's own mapping first, else `TimeInForce::from_fix`; `None` for a field that states no time in force)
  pub fn marketdatatype_of(&self, tag: i32, wire: &str) -> Option<MarketDataType>  (the member one field's wire value types a message as: a registry's own mapping first, else `MarketDataType::from_fix`; `None` for a field that types nothing)
  pub fn from_fields<I>(fields: I) -> Result<Self> where I: IntoIterator<Item = Field>,
  pub fn get_field_by_id(&self, id: FixId) -> Option<&Field>  (a scalar by its identity, else a component or a group by the identity its derived tag and name make)
  pub fn field_by_id(&self, id: FixId) -> Result<&Field>
  pub fn get_field_by_tag(&self, tag: i32) -> Option<&Field>  (the tag's canonical holder, then an alternate; two fields may hold one tag under two names, and the first holder answers; a definition tag in `[DEFINITION_TAG_MIN, DEFINITION_TAG_MAX)` answers the component or group it identifies; a Map group's counter answers nothing here)
  pub fn field_by_tag(&self, tag: i32) -> Result<&Field>
  pub fn get_field_by_counter(&self, tag: i32) -> Option<&Field>  (the repeating group the counter `tag` opens - `453` the `Parties` Serie, `65036` the `metadata` Map; two groups on one counter name nothing; the counter itself is `get_field_by_tag`'s)
  pub fn field_by_counter(&self, tag: i32) -> Result<&Field>  (absence or ambiguity raised)
  pub fn get_field_by_name(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Field>  (a scalar by the canonical fold, then an alias fold, then - only for a name neither reaches - the four word pairs `offer`/`ask`, `size`/`qty`, `bid`/`demand` and `px`/`price` read either way inside the folded name, `AskPx` reaching `OfferPx(133)` and `DemandQty` `BidSize(134)`, a spelling reaching two fields reaching none and nothing written into the registry; then a component, then a group of that name)
  pub fn field_by_name(&self, name: &str) -> Result<&Field>
  pub fn get_field_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&Field>  (a schema has no positions, so an indexed segment answers the item every occurrence holds)
  pub fn field_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Result<&Field>
  pub fn get_field<'key>(&self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Option<&Field>
  pub fn field<'key>(&self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Result<&Field>
  pub fn contains<'key>(&self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> bool
  pub fn insert(&mut self, field: Field) -> Result<Option<Field>>  (a Struct inserts as a component, a Serie/LargeSerie of a Struct or a Map as a group, anything else as a scalar; replaces the same identity only, answering what it replaced; a held tag under another name adds the arrival beside the holder, neither learning the other's name, and a field named by nothing but its tag is added the same way)
  pub fn update(&mut self, field: Field) -> Result<()>  (merges metadata for the existing identity under the per-key rules; a definition is replaced whole)
  pub fn add_field(&mut self, field: Field) -> Result<bool>  (true = arrived; a stored identity or a folded stored name/alias merges under an equal datatype, a field named by nothing but its tag merges into the field answering its tag, as its own or as an alternate - except the other half of a pair it names among its own alternates - and an unnamed holder takes the name of a field arriving on its tag where no field answers that name, the members reading it following; a held tag under another name adds beside the holder, neither learning the other's name, unless that name is a third field's canonical name, which it merges into as a held name under another tag does, the tag staying with its holder - a name another field holds only as an alias stands beside; a nested field folds into the component or group of its name)
  pub fn add_fields<I>(&mut self, fields: I) -> Result<(usize, usize)> where I: IntoIterator<Item = Field>,  (added, merged) under add_field's rules, one staged mutation
  pub fn merge_with(&mut self, other: &Self) -> Result<FixMerge>  (code sets folded first, then scalars and definitions under add_field's rules, memberships unioned, one staged mutation; the counts are FixMerge's, over the scalars alone, the definitions folding beside them under their own rules. A name both dictionaries hold merges by wire value, and a field keeps the set it already reads by with the incoming set folded into it, so a merge widens a vocabulary and never narrows one; a code arriving with a new value under a name another code claims - by a lookup, or by the rule rendering holds names to (`eom` beside a placeholder `EOM`) - keeps its value under no name, logged at warn level, and refuses nothing; a `none`, `null` or blank name claims nothing. An incoming code set that still cannot fold - a document that does not parse, or one rewriting marketdatakindcodeset or statecodeset - is passed over as a FixDrop naming the set and the field that reads by it, the held set staying and the rest of the source folding. A datatype the other dictionary states at another precision of the stored one - unbounded text against any datatype, the integer, floating and decimal families against each other, an integer against an enum leaf, the byte layouts against one another, a date against a datetime whatever the zone, one time width against another - folds under the stored declaration, membership, aliases and code set included, and is counted in FixMerge::restated. The one exception to a merge never changing a declared datatype: a field the other dictionary counts a repeating group by that this one holds as unbounded text, a float or another integer width is retyped int32, logged at warn level ("tag 8070 counts a repeating group, so nocustflds is retyped int32 from float64"), so the answer does not depend on fold order; a decimal, enum, code, flag or temporal stays as held and the group is passed over. A field named by nothing but its tag folds into the field answering its tag, as its own or as an alternate, except the other half of a pair the source linked, and an unnamed holder takes the arriving name, the members reading either - the source's and this dictionary's - rewritten to the identity held; a field on a held tag under another name stands beside the holder, neither learning the other's name. A member reading a source field passed over on a tag this dictionary holds reads the held field; two members of one struct reading one field after that rewrite keep wire order under {name}, {name}2. A group's counter is read through the same rewrite, so a counter merged by name onto another tag counts the held group. What the other dictionary declares otherwise than this one does is passed over into FixMerge::dropped, while the rest of the source still folds: a scalar whose datatype contradicts the one stored for the field its identity or folded name reaches (a time of day against a held instant naming why: "the stored instant reads a clock only with its offset, a bare time of day as null", the reverse "the stored time of day reads no date and no offset"), a spelling or alternate tag another field already holds, a member a held definition declares in another shape, a group whose counter is held as a contradiction of a count or was passed over, or which disagrees on its own counter with the group held under its name, together with every member reading it, a member reading a field that was itself passed over, and a definition whose fold refuses rather than passing a member over - one definition is one mutation, so it is passed over whole with its writes undone, and one that arrives new and goes takes the source's members reading it along. A group whose name is held on another counter is another group, named {name}_{counter}, and a message member reading it stands beside the held member as {member}_{counter}. Two references under one member name to two groups or two components are one member read two ways - a dialect that split a group for one message against the group the dictionary holds - so the incoming target's members fold into the held target recursively under these same rules, and a group the source draws from another component widens the held component the same way. A fold refuses whole, adopting nothing, only where nothing is left to keep: an incoming dictionary whose own catalog does not validate, a folded catalog that does not resolve, and for add_cfb_file and add_json_file a document that does not parse)
  pub fn add_cfb_file(&mut self, handle: &dyn IOBase, dialect: Option<&str>) -> Result<FixMerge>  (the file read as from_cfb_file reads it - its charset, its type words, its warnings with line, column and what the reader did instead - under the dialect or, with none, the handle's URL stem where it reads as a name: opening with an ASCII letter, carrying no comma, its percent escapes decoded (`Morgan%20Stanley.cfb` stamps `morgan stanley`); folded under merge_with's rules, another precision restated and a contradiction passed over; each drop names the handle's URL; a file that does not parse refuses the call whole)
  pub fn add_cfb_files(&mut self, locations: &[Holder], dialect: Option<&str>) -> Result<FixMerge>  (the locations alone, each read for what it is: a glob - cblocks/*.cfb, cblocks/**/*.cfb - holds every file its pattern matches, walked as IOBase::glob walks it, a container matched passed by and private entries never matched; a folder holds the .cfb files directly inside it, the suffix in any case; a file holds itself whatever it is named; a location where nothing is holds nothing - registry.add_cfb_files(&[Holder::local("cblocks")?], None)?. A file reached twice folds once; dialect resolved per file, a name given here stamping every file, `None` letting each file's own stem stand in. Files fold in ascending URL order whatever order they were listed in, so where two disagree about one tag the first-sorting file's declaration is held and the later one is passed over. Files parse side by side on every core, at most one per thread in hand, and fold into one staged dictionary in that order with the catalog resolved once for the whole call: each file read and its stem taken as add_cfb_file reads and takes them, FixMerge::sources counts the files folded, restated sums over them, and each drop names its file. One file is one mutation: a file that cannot be read, is not a well-formed CBlock, or whose fold refuses rather than passing a declaration over is rolled back alone and named in FixMerge::failed while every other file folds; nothing is adopted until the last file is in and the catalog resolves, and where the union does not resolve the files fold again one at a time, each resolved before the next, so the file the union cannot hold is the one left out. Errs on a listing that fails - a location's or one entry of it, before any file is parsed - and on this dictionary's own catalog refusing to read)
  pub fn remove<'key>(&mut self, key: impl Into<FixKey<'key>>) -> Option<Field>  (a scalar, a component or a group; None when absent or still referenced)
  pub fn dialects(&self) -> Vec<String>  (every source id any field or definition names in its FIX:sources, sorted, each once: the ids on the fields, not the catalog)
  pub fn sources(&self) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &FixSource>  (the sources catalog in id order: one entry per id, holding the file it was read from and the role of its plugin; `sources.json` in a store; part of equality and of the hash)
  pub fn get_source(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&FixSource>  (folded)
  pub fn add_source(&mut self, source: FixSource) -> bool  (true = arrived; a held id keeps its entry and takes only what it lacked - a file where it stated none, a plugin role where it stated `UKNW`, two stated roles that disagree keeping the held one at a warn log; the fold of two registries unions the catalogs through it)
  pub fn remove_source(&mut self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<FixSource>>  (Error::Conflict naming the first field or definition still naming the id; None where none is held)
  pub fn next_field_after(&self, after: Option<FixId>) -> Option<&Field>  (scalars only, tag-major, then id)
  pub fn iter(&self) -> FixFieldIter<'_>  (the scalars tag-major, the tag's holder first, then by identity; then the components and the groups in the catalog's name order)
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (scalars, components and groups)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  impl Clone, Default, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash for FixRegistry
  impl<'registry> IntoIterator for &'registry FixRegistry  (IntoIter = FixFieldIter<'registry>)
  pub struct FixFieldIter<'registry>
  impl<'registry> Iterator for FixFieldIter<'registry>  (Item = &'registry Field; also DoubleEndedIterator, ExactSizeIterator and FusedIterator)
pub struct FixMerge  (re-exported at the crate root; what one fold of another source into a dictionary did - answered by merge_with and every door over it, add_cfb_file, add_cfb_files and add_json_file; the counts are the scalars', the definitions folding beside them under their own rules; dropped is what the source said that the dictionary already says otherwise, kept in the order the fold met them rather than refusing the whole source for it; failed is the files add_cfb_files left out whole; restated counts the merged fields whose source stated another precision of the stored datatype and folded under it rather than being passed over)
  pub sources: usize  (the sources folded: one for a dictionary or a file, the files add_cfb_files folded, a file left out not counted)
  pub added: usize  (the scalar fields that arrived)
  pub merged: usize  (the scalar fields folded into ones already held)
  pub restated: usize  (among the merged, the fields whose source declared another precision of the stored datatype and folded under it)
  pub dropped: Vec<FixDrop>  (the declarations passed over, in the order the fold met them)
  pub failed: Vec<FixFailure>  (the sources left out whole, in the order the fold met them: a file add_cfb_files could not read, parse or fold, which contributed nothing while every other file still folded)
  pub fn is_clean(&self) -> bool  (dropped and failed both empty)
  impl Clone, Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq for FixMerge
pub struct FixDrop  (re-exported at the crate root; one declaration a fold passed over because the dictionary already declares the same thing otherwise: a scalar whose datatype contradicts the one stored for the field its identity or name reaches - another precision of it is no contradiction and folds, counted in FixMerge::restated - a member a definition already holds in another shape, a definition that disagrees with the one held under its name or whose fold refuses, a member reading a field the fold passed over, or an incoming code set that does not fold, at codesets.<name> naming the field that reads by it; the dictionary's declaration is what stays)
  pub source: Option<SmolStr>  (where the declaration was read, the file's URL where the fold read one; `None` where nothing located it)
  pub incoming: Field  (the declaration as the source stated it: the scalar field, or a member or definition in the compact shape a store writes)
  pub reason: SmolStr  (the refusal the fold would otherwise have ended on, naming what the dictionary keeps)
  impl Display for FixDrop  ("{source}: {reason}", the source omitted when none)
  impl Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq for FixDrop
pub struct FixFailure  (re-exported at the crate root; one source a plural fold left out whole: add_cfb_files folds each file as one mutation, so a file that cannot be read, is not a well-formed CBlock, or whose fold refuses rather than passing a declaration over is rolled back alone and named here while every other file folds; nothing it said is held)
  pub source: Option<SmolStr>  (the file's URL, where the source is a file with one)
  pub reason: SmolStr  (the refusal the file was left out over)
  impl Display for FixFailure  ("{source}: {reason}", the source omitted when none)
  impl Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq for FixFailure

pub const FIXENTRIES_COLUMN: &str = "fixentries";  (the residual record: a nullable sorted `map<utf8, utf8>` keyed by each resolved field's `tag:name` - `55:symbol` - valued by a scalar's wire text, or a group's or component's JSON keyed the same way all the way down, a key stated twice at one level the JSON array of its values in arrival order, a text opening `[`, `{` or `"` its JSON string; a key no dictionary resolved is in it only where an identifier map holds it with its value - captured, under `0:<key>` as it arrived - and `metadata` holds every other; the map's length is its count; the one column of the row the registry does not define)
pub fn fix_schema_tags() -> Vec<i32>  (the row in nine bands - when it happened, which event is observed, which message and session carried it, which instrument and what the market says about trading it, which order, what values it states as one ladder of prices and one of quantities, the price before the bid and offer that quote it, how it went, the four groups kept whole, then every standard header, body and trailer tag no band claimed, then the metadata: 128 tags. Price(44), OrderQty(38) and Quantity(53) are among them, each exact and stated once; what a message is about is what `Market::get_price` and `get_quantity` read off them, and no column of this crate's restates either)

pub fn fix_schema_carrying(carrier: &Field, read: &Field) -> Result<Field>  (a capture's own columns in front of the fixed ones, less any whose folded name a FIX column takes; each carried column is made nullable whatever the capture declared, because only a pass holding the source row can state one)

### yggdryl::fix::source  [rust/src/fix/source.rs]
pub struct FixSource  (re-exported at the crate root; one source a dictionary was built from - the entry a FIX:sources id names - its id folded to ASCII lowercase, the file it was read from where one is known and the role of its plugin (`UKNW` where none is known); the id grammar is this file's, read by every door an id crosses)
  pub fn new(id: &str) -> Result<Self>  (the id folded; refuses an empty id or one holding a quote, a backslash or a control character, naming FIX:sources)
  pub fn with_file(self, file: impl Into<SmolStr>) -> Self
  pub const fn with_pluginside(self, pluginside: PluginSide) -> Self
  pub fn id(&self) -> &str
  pub fn file(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub const fn pluginside(&self) -> PluginSide  (`BUYS`, `SELL`, or `UKNW` where the source states none)
  pub fn into_scalar(&self) -> Scalar  (the stored entry `{"id": .., "file": .., "pluginside": ..}`, the file left out where none is known, the plugin side always stated)
  pub fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (Error::InvalidRecord for anything but an object stating a text id, at most a text file and at most a `pluginside` naming a member - absent reads `UKNW` - the refusal naming the id; the id grammar's refusal for an id that is none)
  impl Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd for FixSource  (by id, then by file, then by plugin side)

### yggdryl::fix::store  [rust/src/fix/store.rs]
impl FixRegistry
  pub fn from_json(input: &str) -> Result<Self>  (`{codesets, sources, fields, components, groups}` and no other key, `sources` the catalog's entries, stated only where there is one; the code sets are read first, because a field naming one the dictionary does not hold is refused)
  pub fn into_json(&self) -> Result<String>
  pub fn add_json_file(&mut self, handle: &dyn IOBase) -> Result<FixMerge>  (the file parsed through from_json, then folded under merge_with's rules, each drop naming the handle's URL; no dialect - a snapshot states its own FIX:sources and the catalog they name)
  pub fn from_handle(handle: &dyn IOBase) -> Result<Self>  (`codesets/<name>.json` first - one document per named code set, stating its name and its members, refused when the name and the filename stem disagree - then `sources.json`, the sources catalog, absent read as none and a malformed one or two entries of one id refused naming the file, then `fields/<shard>.json` arrays under nine-digit shard names - tag 55 in `fields/000000000.json`, tag 5001 in `fields/000000050.json` - and one native Field document per component or group; a crate-tagged document and `components/fixmsg.json` are passed over, the crate's own definition being the one that types a row; references resolve once, missing names and cycles refused)
  pub fn write_into(&self, root: &mut dyn IOBase) -> Result<()>  (`commit` with its report discarded, one implementation so the two never answer differently)
  pub fn commit(&self, root: &mut dyn IOBase) -> Result<FixCommit>  (the three category directories, `codesets/` and `sources.json` - written where the registry holds a source, removed where it holds none - and nothing else; writes the crate's own beside the dictionary's - `fields/000000650.json`, `groups/metadata.json` and the fixed row `components/fixmsg.json` - then removes every `.json` it did not write and every empty category directory. Each document is digested where it lies and skipped where it already states this registry, so a commit writes what moved and a second commit of one registry writes nothing)
pub struct FixCommit  (re-exported at the crate root; what one `commit` moved under a store root: `written` and `removed` name the documents, `skipped` counts the ones already stating the registry, because a store holds thousands a run leaves alone; `is_clean` is the replay answer and `len` what the commit considered)
  pub written: Vec<SmolStr>
  pub skipped: usize
  pub removed: Vec<SmolStr>
  pub fn is_clean(&self) -> bool
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool

### yggdryl::fix::ulbridge  [rust/src/fix/ulbridge.rs]
pub const ULBRIDGE_ROWHEADER: &str  (the row header ULBridge writes in front of every line of its log: `mtime`, `msgthreadid`, `msgsessionid`, `msgctxid`, `msgseqnum`, `msgpluginid`, `loglevel`; `mtime` is the clock, consumed into each line's `currunix` - a point or a comma before three digits or grouped microseconds, or no fraction - so the header dates its lines and a message stating no SendingTime(52) is read against its line's clock; `msgsessionid`, `msgctxid`, `msgseqnum` and `msgpluginid` are named for the fields they fill, and `msgthreadid` (int64) and `loglevel` (utf8) name none: columns of the line's row, carried in front of a FIX row as the row's own cells and filling no field; kept ULBridge's because the line shape is the product's)

### yggdryl::fs::location  [rust/src/fs/location.rs]
pub struct BoundLocationIdentity  (one resource identity: filesystem equality domain plus exact raw path; its hash uses only the path, because Arrow exposes filesystem equality and no corresponding hash)
  impl Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Debug for BoundLocationIdentity
pub struct BoundLocation  (a filesystem and its exact path, with separate caller and diagnostic URIs)
  pub fn new(filesystem: Arc<dyn FileSystem>, path: impl Into<String>, uri: Option<String>) -> Result<Self>  (the injected path is neither parsed nor normalized, and `uri`, when supplied, is retained exactly and never used to derive the backend path)
  pub fn filesystem(&self) -> &Arc<dyn FileSystem>
  pub fn path(&self) -> &str
  pub fn uri(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn masked_uri(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub const fn diagnostic_url(&self) -> &Url
  pub fn identity(&self) -> BoundLocationIdentity
  pub fn same_location(&self, other: &Self) -> bool
  pub fn try_same_location(&self, other: &Self) -> Result<bool>  (fallible equality for host-runtime filesystem adapters)
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Self>  (a raw child name, with no URL decoding or path normalization; the empty name and `.` both name this location)
  pub fn listed(&self, path: impl Into<String>) -> Result<Self>  (an exact path returned by this filesystem)
  pub fn parent(&self) -> Option<Result<Self>>  (the raw parent, preserving repeated separators and escapes)
  impl Clone, Debug, Display for BoundLocation  (both mask the path, and Display prefers the masked URI)
pub fn mask_uri(uri: &str) -> String  (mask URI user information and credential-like query values; re-exported at `yggdryl::fs`)

### yggdryl::fs::memory  [rust/src/fs/memory.rs]
pub struct MemoryFileSystem  (one isolated in-memory filesystem equality domain)
  pub fn new() -> Self
  impl Clone, Default, Debug, FileSystem for MemoryFileSystem

### yggdryl::fs::path  [rust/src/fs/path.rs]
pub struct FsPath  (a bound location whose current file/directory role is resolved per action)
  pub fn new(bound: BoundLocation) -> Self
  pub fn from_path(filesystem: std::sync::Arc<dyn FileSystem>, path: impl Into<String>, uri: Option<String>) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn bound(&self) -> &BoundLocation
  pub fn filesystem(&self) -> &std::sync::Arc<dyn FileSystem>
  pub fn path(&self) -> &str
  pub const fn url(&self) -> &Url
  pub fn as_directory(&self) -> FsFolder  (without probing the location)
  pub fn as_file(&self) -> FsFile  (without probing the location; carries a declared media type across)
  impl IOBase, IOPath, IOMedia, Debug for FsPath

### yggdryl::fs::stream  [rust/src/fs/stream.rs]
pub trait ByteReader: Send  (a sequential input stream)
  fn read(&mut self, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize>
  fn tell(&self) -> u64
  fn close(&mut self) -> Result<()>  (repeated calls after a successful close are successful)
  fn closed(&self) -> bool
  fn as_any(&self) -> &dyn Any
  fn into_any(self: Box<Self>) -> Box<dyn Any>
pub trait RandomAccessReader: ByteReader  (a seekable input file with positional reads)
  fn read_at(&mut self, offset: u64, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize>  (without changing the current position)
  fn seek(&mut self, from: SeekFrom) -> Result<u64>
  fn into_random_any(self: Box<Self>) -> Box<dyn Any>
pub trait ByteWriter: Send  (a sequential output or append stream)
  fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize>
  fn tell(&self) -> u64
  fn flush(&mut self) -> Result<()>  (publish buffered writes without closing)
  fn close(&mut self) -> Result<()>  (flush and close exactly once; a failed close remains visible)
  fn closed(&self) -> bool
  fn as_any(&self) -> &dyn Any
  fn into_any(self: Box<Self>) -> Box<dyn Any>

### yggdryl::fs::system  [rust/src/fs/system.rs]
pub struct FileSelector  (options for one directory listing)
  pub base_dir: String
  pub recursive: bool
  pub allow_not_found: bool
  pub fn new(base_dir: impl Into<String>, recursive: bool, allow_not_found: bool) -> Self
  impl Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd for FileSelector
pub struct OutputMetadata(BTreeMap<String, String>)  (key/value metadata supplied while opening an output stream)
  pub fn from_entries(entries: impl IntoIterator<Item = (impl Into<String>, impl Into<String>)>) -> Self
  pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&str, &str)>  (deterministic key order)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  impl Clone, Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq for OutputMetadata
pub struct FileInfo  (what a filesystem reports about one exact path)
  pub path: String
  pub kind: IOKind
  pub size: Option<u64>
  pub mtime_ns: Option<i64>
  pub fn file(path: impl Into<String>, size: impl Into<Option<u64>>, mtime_ns: Option<i64>) -> Self
  pub fn directory(path: impl Into<String>, mtime_ns: Option<i64>) -> Self
  pub fn not_found(path: impl Into<String>) -> Self
  impl Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd for FileInfo
pub struct FileInfos  (a deterministic listing that fuses after its first failure)
  pub fn empty() -> Self
  pub fn new(entries: impl Iterator<Item = Result<FileInfo>> + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self
  pub fn failing(error: Error) -> Self
  impl Iterator for FileInfos  (Item = Result<FileInfo>; also FusedIterator)
  impl Debug, Default for FileInfos
pub trait FileSystem: Send + Sync + Any  (the complete synchronous Arrow filesystem contract)
  fn type_name(&self) -> &str  (backend name used only for safe diagnostics)
  fn is_thread_bound(&self) -> bool  (whether it answers only on the thread that made it - a JavaScript handler's; native backends answer false)
  fn equals(&self, other: &dyn FileSystem) -> bool  (including configuration and credential scope)
  fn try_equals(&self, other: &dyn FileSystem) -> Result<bool>  (defaults to `equals`; a language adapter overrides it so a handler exception is never reclassified as inequality)
  fn normalize_path(&self, path: &str) -> Result<String>
  fn file_info(&self, path: &str) -> Result<FileInfo>  (absence is a not-found FileInfo)
  fn list(&self, selector: &FileSelector) -> FileInfos  (ascending path order)
  fn create_dir(&self, path: &str, recursive: bool) -> Result<()>
  fn delete_dir(&self, path: &str) -> Result<()>  (the empty directory itself)
  fn delete_dir_recursive(&self, path: &str) -> Result<()>  (defaults to delete_dir_contents then delete_dir; overridden where the backend has one native recursive operation)
  fn delete_dir_contents(&self, path: &str, missing_dir_ok: bool) -> Result<()>  (the descendants, retaining the selected directory)
  fn delete_root_dir_contents(&self) -> Result<()>
  fn delete_file(&self, path: &str) -> Result<()>  (directories are rejected)
  fn copy_file(&self, source: &str, target: &str) -> Result<()>
  fn move_file(&self, source: &str, target: &str) -> Result<()>
  fn open_input_file(&self, path: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn RandomAccessReader>>
  fn open_input_stream(&self, path: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn ByteReader>>
  fn open_output_stream(&self, path: &str, metadata: Option<&OutputMetadata>) -> Result<Box<dyn ByteWriter>>  (truncating)
  fn open_append_stream(&self, path: &str, metadata: Option<&OutputMetadata>) -> Result<Box<dyn ByteWriter>>
  fn create_file(&self, path: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<()>  (one file where the path holds nothing; a file or a directory already there is Error::Conflict, left as it was, a missing parent the absence the caller repairs; the default is file_info then open_output_stream and not exclusive, LocalFileSystem overrides it with create_new and MemoryFileSystem inserts under its one lock)
  fn as_any(&self) -> &dyn Any  (the concrete backend, for a language binding)

### yggdryl::fs::transfer  [rust/src/fs/transfer.rs]
pub fn copy_bound(source: &BoundLocation, target: &BoundLocation) -> Result<u64>  (the backend copy_file where both locations share one filesystem; otherwise streamed to a temporary name beside the target and moved into place, the temporary removed on any failure)
pub fn move_bound(source: &BoundLocation, target: &BoundLocation) -> Result<u64>  (the backend move_file within one filesystem, else copy_bound then delete_file on the source)

### yggdryl::graph::element  [rust/src/graph/element.rs]
pub trait Element  (one element of a graph: its own identity, the identity it has in another graph and the code that names it there, the codes it digests to, its sources' identities and the order it stands in, all read and written; object-safe but for the order and the by-value readings; it carries no identifier map - the names an operation goes by are `Operation::get_identifiers`, and what a book reads to place an entry is `OperationEvent::book`)
  fn get_curruuid(&self) -> Uuid
  fn set_curruuid(&mut self, curruuid: Uuid)
  fn get_crossuuid(&self) -> Uuid  (the cross element: what this element is in another graph; never absent - its own identity where it states no cross code, so every element stands in exactly one chain)
  fn set_crossuuid(&mut self, crossuuid: Uuid)
  fn get_crosscode(&self) -> &str  (the text naming the element in the other graph - an order's OrderID, a quote's QuoteID - the identifier every incarnation of one thing shares; empty where none is stated)
  fn set_crosscode(&mut self, crosscode: String)  (empty states none; the crate's dated leaves' setters eagerly bring the cross hash and cross identity in step and reproject or retain-on-refusal `curruuid`, whose UUIDv7 payload is seeded by that cross hash; their `set_crosshashcode` does the same projection directly; a foreign implementor owns whether an assigned identity survives its setters)
  fn get_currhashcode(&self) -> u64  (the XXH3-64 digest the element's content answers to, what `finalize` recomputes)
  fn set_currhashcode(&mut self, hashcode: u64)
  fn get_crosshashcode(&self) -> u64  (the XXH3-64 of the cross code; zero where none is stated)
  fn set_crosshashcode(&mut self, crosshashcode: u64)
  fn get_srcuuids(&self) -> &[Uuid]  (the sorted unique identities of the elements this one was read from - a message's text line; empty where it was read from no element; provenance, never its chain or reference priority: no provided reading carries it along one)
  fn set_srcuuids(&mut self, sources: Vec<Uuid>)  (replaces the list whole, sorting and deduplicating it)
  fn is_after(&self, other: &Self) -> bool where Self: Sized  (the order the implementor states - an event's instant, a node's predecessor; with `is_before` one strict weak order: never after itself, and neither-nor is equal)
  fn is_before(&self, other: &Self) -> bool where Self: Sized  (provided: `other.is_after(self)`)
  fn finalize(&mut self)  (the implementor's: fills what its traits imply - `fill_market`, `fill_operation` - brings the cross codes in step with `sync_cross`, digests its content from `digest` or the continuation its traits provide, and hands the code to `set_currhashcode` - or, for an event, to `Event::finalized`; the holders always derive this way, while a foreign implementor may preserve an identity it owns by not calling the provided finalizer; every provided reading calls it once it changed the element)
  fn sync_cross(&mut self) -> bool  (provided: the cross hash code set to the cross code's XXH3-64, zero where none, and the cross element to `cross_uuid`; whether either moved)
  fn cross_uuid(&self) -> Uuid  (provided: RFC 9562 UUIDv8 over the cross hash code where it is not zero, else the element's own identity)
  fn digest(&self) -> Xxh3  (provided: an XXH3-64 state fed the cross code under its own name, and never an identity, an instant or its sources; an implementor feeds its own content behind and reads `as_u64`)
  fn with_previous(self, previous: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Sized  (this element stated as the one after `previous`, or nothing where it cannot follow it or following changes nothing; the implementor's, and an event delegates to `Event::following` or to the market reading of its kind)
  fn merge_with(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Sized  (provided: another statement of the same element folded in - the cross code where this one states none, the cross codes brought in step, and a position-independent sorted unique source union - and finalized; nothing for another element or a fold that changes nothing; an event delegates to `Event::merging`, a market element to `Market::merging_market`, an operation entry to `Operation::merging_operation`, a market event to `Market::merging_market_event`, an operation event to `Operation::merging_operation_event`)
pub(crate) fn feed_event_facts<E: Event + ?Sized>(state: &mut Xxh3, this: &E)  (the event facts a foreign implementor's digest starts from - the cross code, the state and the predecessor's identity, no place and no instant - what `FixMsg`'s content code feeds first)
pub trait Event: Element  (an element that happened at one instant, stands in one state and takes one place among the events of that instant)
  fn get_currunix(&self) -> i64  (nanoseconds since the Unix epoch, UTC)
  fn set_currunix(&mut self, unix: i64)
  fn get_state(&self) -> &State  (the crate's lifecycle-sorted member, never absent: `UNKNOWN` where none was reached)
  fn set_state(&mut self, state: State)
  fn is_execution(&self) -> bool  (provided: whether this observation itself reports an execution, defaulting to `State::is_execution`; a protocol whose lifecycle state and report kind differ overrides it, as FIX does when an explicit correction or cancellation `ExecType` accompanies a filled `OrdStatus`, and an `OperationEvent` answers its kind)
  fn get_seqnum(&self) -> u64  (its place among the events of its instant: zero for the first its stream hands over there, one more for each next)
  fn set_seqnum(&mut self, seqnum: u64)
  fn get_creaunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (when it was created, in the same count, where known)
  fn set_creaunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>)
  fn get_recdunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the precise recording instant, in the same count, where known)
  fn set_recdunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>)
  fn get_exprunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (when it expires, where it has an expiry)
  fn set_exprunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>)
  fn get_prevunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (when the element it follows happened, where it follows one)
  fn set_prevunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>)
  fn get_prevuuid(&self) -> Option<Uuid>  (the identity of the element it follows, where it follows one)
  fn set_prevuuid(&mut self, uuid: Option<Uuid>)
  fn get_snapunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the opening instant of the grid step a walk read the element as the snapshot of, where one did)
  fn set_snapunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>)
  fn following(self, previous: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Sized  (provided: records the predecessor's identity and instant, keeping its own place unless the predecessor happened at the same instant or later, where it takes the higher of its own and one past the predecessor's (saturating), forces the predecessor's cross code where this one's differs with the cross codes brought in step, and folds the lifecycle - the predecessor is the one element it names, nothing recording the chain further back; sources and recording instant stay this observation's own; nothing for its own predecessor, one that happened after it, or where nothing moves; finalized)
  fn restating(self, live: &Self) -> Self where Self: Sized  (provided, and overridden by every market event - `OperationEvent`, `TradeEvent`, `BookEvent`, `SnapshotEvent`, `FixMsg` - to take the market's place in the chain too: the step before it, `prevpx` and `prevqty`, what the chain is about where this reading said nothing of it - the metadata keys it lacks included - and for an operation the time in force, whether it trades, the followed identifiers and the party ids. This event as another statement of `live` - the same instant and content read a second time, one message logged at every hop - taking live's predecessor, place and snapshot, the chain's cross code - never its sources - the lifecycle folded, the earliest recording instant kept - and, for a market event, the earliest execution clock - then finalized, so both statements finalize to one identity and the chain grows by nothing; the caller establishes the twin by the identity `live` arrived under, which `EventIterator` records)
  fn merging(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Sized  (provided: the element-level merge with the later `recdunix` as reference - stated recording beats absent, ties fall back to later `currunix`, an exact tie keeps this one - then that reference's instant, code, predecessor and snapshot leading; the higher place, folded lifecycle, and the earliest recording instant retained, so a merged statement ranks by its earliest recording against a third and the reference among three depends on merge order; nothing for another element or a fold that changes nothing; finalized)
  fn fold_lifecycle(&mut self, other: &Self) -> bool where Self: Sized  (provided: the earliest creation, the latest expiration, the furthest state as `State::merge_with` reads two states; whether any moved)
  fn finalized(&mut self, hashcode: u64)  (provided: records the code, resets the current identity to `time_uuid` - kept where the instant has no UUIDv7 - and the cross element to `cross_uuid` over it; what an implementor's `Element::finalize` hands its content's digest to)
  fn digest_event(&self) -> Xxh3  (provided: `Element::digest` continued with the state and the predecessor's identity; no place, no instant)
  fn txhash(&self) -> Result<TxHash>  (provided: `currunix` at nanosecond resolution coupled with `currhashcode` as an XXH3-64 digest; ArithmeticOverflow past signed 64-bit nanoseconds)
  fn time_uuid(&self) -> Result<Uuid>  (provided: calls `txhash()?.into_sequenced_uuid(seqnum, crosshashcode)`; the TxHash instant is restated from nanoseconds to Unix milliseconds, `rand_a` holds the sequence up to 4095 and saturates above it, and `rand_b` holds the low 62 bits of XXH3 over `currhashcode` and the whole `u64` sequence seeded by `crosshashcode`, so representable sequences order within a millisecond and overflowed values remain probabilistically distinct; what an implementor's `Element::get_curruuid` answers where its identity is its instant and its content)

### yggdryl::graph::market  [rust/src/graph/market.rs]  (the market vocabulary: the slim reading anything that stands in a market gives, what an operation on it adds, and what an event that is one or the other answers, provided on the traits `where Self: Event`)
pub type Metadata = BTreeMap<SmolStr, SmolStr>;  (free-form facts a market element carries beside its typed ones; never an identifier, which has a typed home)
pub fn empty_metadata() -> &'static Metadata  (the one shared empty map an element holding none answers)
pub type FxRates = BTreeMap<Ccy, Decimal>;  (the FX rates a market element states, sorted by target currency: an amount in the element's currency divided by the rate under a target is that amount in the target)
pub fn empty_fxrates() -> &'static FxRates  (the one shared empty map an element stating no rate answers)
pub trait Market  (an element that stands in a market, no supertrait: every fact a `MarketColumn` names and whether it is sided - the ISIN, the cross code an order or an execution stores under its side, and the book key provided beside them - every accessor `get_` and every mutator `set_` taking a trailing `overwrite` - `true` states the value whatever the element held, `None` included, `false` fills scalar facts and ordinary map keys only where absent; `Identifiers` maps replace lower-ranked values and keep equal-ranked held values - a change carrying what it implies onto the facts that follow it (side and price onto the bid or ask, quantity and `displayqty` onto `hiddenqty`, `ordqty`/`cumqty`/`leavesqty` onto one another), each written directly so a chain cannot loop; but the security identifiers, which a holder answers as a set and changes through fallible verbs - a holder that is a view of another store, a FIX message over its own fields, writes the store through and may refuse; a plain holder always answers `Ok`)
  fn get_price(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (exact, `decimal128(38, 18)` preapplied; `None` where none)
  fn set_price(&mut self, price: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_stoppx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the price a stop order triggers at, where it states one)
  fn set_stoppx(&mut self, px: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_strikepx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the strike price of the option the element is about, where it states one: an instrument fact - a follower of the same instrument stating none takes its chain's, a merge fills it, the digest feeds it only where stated)
  fn set_strikepx(&mut self, px: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_currency(&self) -> &Ccy  (`Ccy::none()` where unstated)
  fn set_currency(&mut self, currency: Ccy, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_origccy(&self) -> &Ccy  (the currency the instrument was issued in, where stated or filled by a registry; `Ccy::none()` otherwise - never defaulted, so a fill lands wherever nothing is held)
  fn set_origccy(&mut self, ccy: Ccy, overwrite: bool)  (moves nothing else: the currency is never filled from it, nor it from the currency)
  fn origin_currency(&self) -> &Ccy  (provided: `get_origccy` where held, else `get_currency` - the currency an amount converts from, read and never stored)
  fn get_quantity(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (`None` where none)
  fn set_quantity(&mut self, quantity: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_displayqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the part of the quantity shown to the market - an iceberg's peak)
  fn set_displayqty(&mut self, qty: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)  (moves `hiddenqty`, the quantity past the shown part)
  fn get_hiddenqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the part of the quantity kept from the market - an iceberg's reserve; a follower stating none takes what its predecessor kept back less what traded since)
  fn set_hiddenqty(&mut self, qty: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)  (fills `displayqty` back, the quantity less it)
  fn get_unit(&self) -> &Unit  (`Unit::none()` where unstated)
  fn set_unit(&mut self, unit: Unit, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_side(&self) -> Side  (by value, one byte; `Side::Unknown` where none: a sided element's one side (`is_sided`), any other's tag - a one-sided quote names the leg it states, a two-sided one `BOTH`; a book always `BOTH`)
  fn set_side(&mut self, side: Side, overwrite: bool)  (a sided element's cross code restated under the new side and the side it left no longer quoting the price and quantity; any other element's a tag over the legs it holds, withdrawing none, the price and quantity moving from the leg the old tag took to the one the new takes; either way the side taken quotes them; never touches another element's)
  fn marketdatakind(&self) -> MarketDataKind  (required: the category the holder is filed under - an order `ORDR`, a quote `QUOT`, an execution `EXEC`, a trade `TRAD`, a book `BOOK`, a FIX message the category its dictionary files its type under - and the category a lifecycle chains within)
  fn is_sided(&self) -> bool  (provided: `MarketDataKind::is_sided` of `marketdatakind` - whether the cross code states the side in its prefix: an order or an execution; a quote, whose side is a tag, a trade, a book, a snapshot control and every other category state `0` there)
  fn get_marketdatatype(&self) -> MarketDataType  (the type of its kind the element is - an order type, a quote type, a trade type, a book entry type; `MarketDataType::Unknown` where it states none)
  fn set_marketdatatype(&mut self, mdtype: MarketDataType, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_securityids(&self) -> &Identifiers  (the security identifiers, a sorted map from the key to the value; `ids.get(&IdType::Isin)` answers the base key - the type's answer, whichever source stated it)
  fn set_securityids(&mut self, ids: Identifiers, overwrite: bool) -> Result<()>  (replaces the map whole, each identifier keeping its own source, or, without `overwrite`, validates security types and rank-merges through `Identifiers::merge(..., false)`: higher-ranked values replace lower-ranked ones, equal ranks keep held values, and other keys remain)
  fn insert_securityid(&mut self, id: Identifier) -> Result<bool>  (`IdType::check_security` then `Identifiers::insert`: fills a free key or replaces a value ranking below it (`IdType::rank`), a named source filling its type's base key, a statement taking back a `derived` one of its type that does not outrank it; `ticker` refused; whether it was added)
  fn remove_securityid(&mut self, key: &IdKey) -> Result<bool>  (`Identifiers::remove`: a named source's key that identifier alone, a base key every key of its type; where no ISIN is left every `derived` identifier is taken back too, since each hangs on it; whether one was held)
  fn derive_securityid(&mut self, kind: &IdType, code: &str) -> bool  (from `derived`, only where no identifier of the type is held: what the element implies rather than states, never reaching a store the holder is a view of)
  fn get_cficode(&self) -> Option<&Cfi>
  fn set_cficode(&mut self, code: Option<Cfi>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_miccode(&self) -> Option<&Mic>  (the market it trades on, ISO 10383, where known)
  fn set_miccode(&mut self, code: Option<Mic>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_execunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (when it last executed: the latest execution clock its lifecycle reached, nanoseconds since the Unix epoch, UTC, where known; a market fact, never an `Event` one, and no digest feeds it - an event whose state reports an execution and states none is dated from its own instant by the market readings below, following keeps the later of its own and its predecessor's, and two statements of one event keep the earliest)
  fn set_execunix(&mut self, unix: Option<i64>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_lastpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (what the element last traded, with `get_lastqty`; each with its `set_`)
  fn get_avgpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (what it averaged; `get_cumqty` and `get_leavesqty` how much is done and how much is left, `get_cxlqty` how much someone canceled of what was left; each with its `set_`)
  fn get_prevpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (with `get_prevqty`: what the step before this one settled on, which a walk fills; each with its `set_`)
  fn get_spotrate(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (with `get_forwardpoints`: the two FX parts of a price, `None` unless set until FIX lifts them; each with its `set_`)
  fn get_ticker(&self) -> Option<&str>  (the name a person knows the instrument by, beside the codes rather than among them)
  fn set_ticker(&mut self, ticker: Option<SmolStr>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_metadata(&self) -> &Metadata  (a shared empty map where the element carries none)
  fn set_metadata(&mut self, metadata: Option<Metadata>, overwrite: bool)  (`None` and an empty map are the same)
  fn get_fxrates(&self) -> &FxRates  (target currency to the rate to divide by; a shared empty map where none is stated; nothing fills it)
  fn set_fxrates(&mut self, rates: FxRates, overwrite: bool)  (an empty map states none)
  fn insert_fxrate(&mut self, target: Ccy, rate: Decimal) -> bool  (provided: fills an absent target, never replaces a stated one; whether it was added)
  fn get_bidpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the bid a quote's `BidPx(132)` or a book's best tradable bid level states, with `get_bidqty`; each with its `set_`; never filled from a price or a last price)
  fn get_bidccy(&self) -> Option<&Ccy>  (the currency the bid is stated in, where the element states a bid; with its `set_bidccy`)
  fn get_askpx(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the ask a quote's `OfferPx(133)` or a book's best tradable ask level states, with `get_askqty`; each with its `set_`)
  fn get_askccy(&self) -> Option<&Ccy>  (the currency the ask is stated in; with its `set_askccy`)
  fn get_isincode(&self) -> Option<&str>  (provided: the `isin` security identifier, borrowed - a projection of `get_securityids`, never a second store; the `isincode` column)
  fn book_crosscode(&self) -> &str  (provided: the key of the book the element stands in - its instrument's ISIN (`get_isincode`) where it holds one, whatever its rank, else its ticker where it states a non-empty one, else `Isin::NONE` (`XX0000000000`), the number that states none; one book per instrument wherever an ISIN is known, a ticker-only statement joining it once the lifecycle's registry learned the pair; every arm borrows, so no input allocates; what `BookIterator` keys its books by)
  fn stored_crosscode<'code>(&self, code: &'code str) -> Cow<'code, str>  (provided: the cross code `code` is stored as - `{kind}:{side}:{code}`, the `MarketDataKind` code of the category the element is filed under and the `Side` code of the side it takes where `is_sided` answers - an order, an execution - `0` for any other element, a quote among them, or a side not stated: `10:1:ORD-1`, `8:0:E-1`, `14:0:Q-1`, `21:0:T-1`, `3:0:CH0012214059`; idempotent, a prefix of another kind or side replaced, an empty code kept empty; the one place the prefix is spelled, every market holder storing its cross code through it, and a copy of a sided element into another kind's holder taking its base code)
  fn fill_market(&mut self) where Self: Sized  (provided: fills what the element implies from what it states and stops where it would be inventing - the price and the quantity stay what it states, never a last executed price or quantity and never an average, `cumqty` and `leavesqty` beside them because together they are the quantity ordered; the national identifier a closing ISIN embeds derived into a key the element does not state; an element trading a currency pair and stating no unit takes the currency dealt - its stated currency where that is a leg of the pair, else the pair's base; idempotent, and what an implementor's `finalize` runs before it digests)
  fn digest_market(&self) -> Xxh3 where Self: Element  (provided: `Element::digest` continued with the price, the currency, the quantity, the unit, the side, each security identifier as key and code, the classification, the market, each optional number where stated - `prevpx` and `prevqty` left out, as the predecessor's instant and identity are - the bid and ask prices, quantities and currencies where stated, each FX rate by target, the ticker and the metadata in key order)
  fn merging_market(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Element + Sized  (provided: the element-level merge, then each optional fact - the price and the quantity as every other, the bid and ask and their currencies among them - and the ticker this element's where stated and the other's where not, the execution clock the earliest either states, the metadata and the FX rates a union by key with this element's values leading, the currency, the side and each code - the unit among them - the better of the two as `CodeValue::merge_with` and `Side::merge_with` read them, a value of higher rank replacing a lower one whatever the order - `UNKNOWN`, an unknown CFI position, the `XXXX` MIC, a `ZZ`, masked or mistyped ISIN taking the other's - a security identifier only the other states filling its key; nothing for another element or a fold that changes nothing; finalized)
  fn digest_market_event(&self) -> Xxh3 where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: `Event::digest_event` continued with the market's facts)
  fn following_market(self, previous: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: an execution event stating no `execunix` dated from its own instant before it names a predecessor, `Event::following`, then the execution clock kept at the later of this event's and the predecessor's - a delayed report cannot regress it - and the price and the quantity that statement settled on as this event's `prevpx` and `prevqty` where it states none of its own, and what the chain is about where this event says nothing of it - the currency, the unit, a sided element's side (an order's, an execution's; any other's side is its own tag), each leg of a quote - `bidpx`/`bidqty`/`bidccy`, `askpx`/`askqty`/`askccy` - an unsided follower states neither the price nor the quantity of, whole, an absent ticker, each security identifier under a key this event does not state - an ISIN it states yielding to the predecessor's that outranks it, none taken from a predecessor naming another instrument - the classification, the market and every `metadata` key it does not state - this statement always leading, even when the timed link is already present; finalized once. What `SnapshotEvent`, `BookEvent` and `FixMsg::with_previous` delegate to)
  fn merging_market_event(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: `Event::merging`'s reference - the later `recdunix`, then the later `currunix`, an exact tie keeping this one - leading the market too: each optional fact - the price and the quantity as every other, the bid and ask and their currencies among them - and the ticker are the reference's where stated and the other's where not, the metadata and the FX rates a union by key with the reference's values leading, the currency, the side and each code - the unit among them - the better of the two by rank, a security identifier the reference states under a key replacing the other's unless the other's outranks it, the execution clock the earliest either statement knows; nothing where the fold changes nothing; finalized)
pub trait Operation: Market  (a market element that is also an operation on the market: five more facts)
  fn get_ordqty(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (the quantity the operation ordered, `OrderQty(38)`; working, any two of it, `cumqty` and `leavesqty` fill the third, filled leaves nothing, no longer active leaves nothing and `cxlqty` what was left; never the quantity the element is about)
  fn set_ordqty(&mut self, qty: Option<Decimal>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_timeinforce(&self) -> Option<&TimeInForce>  (how long it stands; `TimeInForce::from_spelling("day")` stores the member `DAY`)
  fn set_timeinforce(&mut self, timeinforce: Option<TimeInForce>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_tradable(&self) -> Option<bool>  (`None` a market that said nothing either way)
  fn set_tradable(&mut self, tradable: Option<bool>, overwrite: bool)
  fn get_identifiers(&self) -> &Identifiers  (the names the operation goes by - ids, with the parents a chain gave them - each a value under its key, in key order)
  fn set_identifiers(&mut self, ids: Identifiers, overwrite: bool) -> Result<()>  (replaced whole, or only what it lacks without `overwrite`)
  fn insert_identifier(&mut self, id: Identifier) -> Result<bool>  (`Identifiers::insert`: fills a free key or replaces a value ranking below it; whether it was added)
  fn remove_identifier(&mut self, key: &IdKey) -> Result<bool>  (`Identifiers::remove`; whether one was held)
  fn get_partyids(&self) -> &Identifiers  (the parties the operation names, each typed by its role - `customeraccount`, `executingtrader`, `account` - from its source; a FIX message's are its `Parties(453)` and `RootParties(1116)` occurrences and its `Account(1)`)
  fn set_partyids(&mut self, partyids: Identifiers, overwrite: bool) -> Result<()>
  fn insert_partyid(&mut self, partyid: Identifier) -> Result<bool>  (`Identifiers::insert`: fills a free key or replaces a value ranking below it; whether it was added)
  fn remove_partyid(&mut self, key: &IdKey) -> Result<bool>  (`Identifiers::remove`; whether one was held)
  fn identifier_set(&self, kind: &IdType) -> &Identifiers  (provided: the set an identifier of `kind` belongs to - a security type's `get_securityids`, a party's `get_partyids`, any other type's `get_identifiers` - where a key naming one lands, and so where a reader asks whether a key is held)
  fn is_followed_identifier(&self, id: &Identifier) -> bool  (provided: whether an operation that follows another carries the identifier where it states none - every type but `mdentryrefid`, a book entry's reference to its predecessor, which names one step, unless the holder's own dictionary says otherwise; `FixMsg` answers its registry's `FIX:idmap` follow flags, each followed type's parents - `FixRegistry::parent_of` - with it)
  fn parents_of(&self, base: &IdType) -> Cow<'_, [IdType]>  (provided: the parent types of one of the operation's identifier types, nearest first - `IdType::parents` - unless the holder's own dictionary says otherwise; `FixMsg` answers its registry's `FIX:parents`, else the name's)
  fn parent_of(&self, kind: &IdType) -> Option<(IdType, usize)>  (provided: the base `kind` is a parent of and its place among the base's `parents_of` - `IdType::parent_of` - as the same holder reads it; `FixMsg` answers its registry's)
  fn digest_operation(&self) -> Xxh3 where Self: Element  (provided: `digest_market` continued with the time in force, whether it trades and the identifiers and the party ids, each source, type and value in key order, fed under the labels `identifiers` and `partyids`, the parents among them)
  fn merging_operation(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Element + Sized  (provided: the element and market merges, then the time in force, whether it trades, and the alternate and account identifiers a union with this element's values leading; finalized where anything moved)
  fn digest_operation_event(&self) -> Xxh3 where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: `Event::digest_event` continued with the market's and the operation's facts)
  fn following_operation(self, previous: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: `following_market` continued with the operation - what its chain ordered, traded and at what average (`ordqty`, `cumqty`, `avgpx`) where it states none of them, never a last fill, which no rise in `cumqty` invents; the time in force and whether it trades where unstated, every party id a source and role this statement names none for (a `FixMsg` refuses a new role, so takes none), and every identifier it lacks that `is_followed_identifier` answers for; the metadata keys it lacks come with the market's; a sided element's side is this statement's own where it states one, else the chain's; the FX rates are never followed. What `OperationEvent::with_previous` delegate to)
  fn merging_operation_event(self, other: &Self) -> Option<Self> where Self: Event + Sized  (provided: `merging_market_event` continued with the time in force, whether it trades, and the alternate and account identifiers - a union with the reference's values leading)
pub(crate) fn restating_market<E: Event + Market>(this: E, live: &E) -> E  (the timed restatement, the execution clock folded to the earliest of the two statements, then the market's - the step before it and what the chain is about; what every market event's `Event::restating` delegates to)
pub(crate) fn restating_operation<E: Event + Operation>(this: E, live: &E) -> E  (`restating_market` continued with the time in force, whether it trades, the followed alternate identifiers and the accounts; what every operation event's `Event::restating` delegates to, `FixMsg` included)
pub(crate) fn merge_market_event_into_reference<E: Event + Market>(this: &mut E, supplement: &E)  (the supplement folded into the reference statement, finalizing where anything moved)
pub(crate) fn merge_operation_event_into_reference<E: Event + Operation>(this: &mut E, supplement: &E)  (the same, the operation's facts included)
pub(crate) fn merge_operation_event<E: Event + Operation>(this: &mut E, other: &E, other_is_reference: bool) -> bool  (the operation's facts another statement of it adds, finalizing nothing; whether any moved - what a FIX session event's fold of several observations reads, its identity stamped once after the last)

### yggdryl::graph::market_column  [rust/src/graph/market_column.rs]

  pub const fn name(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn display(self) -> &'static str

### yggdryl::graph::operation_column  [rust/src/graph/operation_column.rs]  (the five columns every market operation is stated in, beside the market's thirty-five)
