### yggdryl::graph::operation  [rust/src/graph/operation.rs]  (the operation leaves: an order, a quote or an execution, undated or dated, generic over a sealed kind, with the book-control facts a market-data entry carries)
pub trait OperationKind: Sealed + Copy + Clone + Debug + Eq + PartialEq + Send + Sync + 'static  (sealed: `OrderKind`, `QuoteKind` and `ExecutionKind` are the only kinds)
  const KIND: MarketKind  (the word the kind digests and stores under: `order`, `quote` or `execution`)
pub struct OrderKind;  (`MarketKind::Order`; Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)
pub struct QuoteKind;  (`MarketKind::Quote`)
pub struct ExecutionKind;  (`MarketKind::Execution`)
pub enum MdUpdateAction { New = 0, Change = 1, Delete = 2, DeleteThru = 3, DeleteFrom = 4, Overlay = 5, Snapshot = 6 }  (FIX's `MDUpdateAction(279)` plus the full snapshot a `W` replaces a scope with)
  impl Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd for MdUpdateAction
  pub const ALL: [Self; 7] = [ Self::New, Self::Change, Self::Delete, Self::DeleteThru, Self::DeleteFrom, Self::Overlay, Self::Snapshot, ]
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str  (the FIX code, or `snapshot`)
  pub fn read(text: &str) -> Option<Self>  (the code, the code set's name folded, or the legacy `SNAPSHOT`)
  pub const fn is_range_delete(self) -> bool  (delete-through or delete-from)
  pub const fn is_partial(self) -> bool  (a change or an overlay, which inherits what it leaves unstated)
pub struct BookRef { pub action: Option<MdUpdateAction>, pub scope: Option<SmolStr>, pub position: Option<u32>, pub entry_px: Option<Decimal>, pub entry_size: Option<Decimal> }  (the typed book-control facts a market-data entry carries - the update action, the scope, `MDEntryPositionNo(290)`, and `MDEntryPx(270)` and `MDEntrySize(271)` as stated, so a partial update still knows what it stated - never an identifier: the entry's own and referenced identifiers are the operation's `mdentryid` and `mdentryrefid` identifiers. the scope, the action and the position are row columns (`bookscope`, `bookaction`, `bookposition`), which a book's delta replays by, and only the scope feeds the operation's digest; Clone, Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq)
  pub fn is_stated(&self) -> bool  (whether any control fact is stated)
pub struct OperationElement<K: OperationKind>  (one operation with no instant: the element, market and operation facts; Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)
  pub fn new() -> Self  (stating nothing, not yet finalized)
  pub const fn kind(&self) -> MarketKind  (`K::KIND`)
  pub fn at(self, unix: i64) -> OperationEvent<K>  (this element dated at `unix` and finalized: a move)
  impl<K: OperationKind> Element, Market, Operation for OperationElement<K>  (`finalize` fills the market and the operation, brings the cross codes in step and digests `digest_operation` continued with the kind: the identity is RFC 9562 UUIDv8 over the code; `is_after` is always false; following and merging go through the facts and refinalize)
pub struct OperationEvent<K: OperationKind>  (one dated operation: the facts, the clocks and a boxed `BookRef` absent on every operation that is no market-data entry, so one pointer; what every market message expands to and what a book holds; Clone, Debug, PartialEq; `Default` stating nothing at the epoch)
  pub fn at(unix: i64) -> Self  (an operation that happened at `unix`, stating nothing else yet)
  pub const fn kind(&self) -> MarketKind  (`K::KIND`)
  pub fn into_element(self) -> OperationElement<K>  (the event clocks and the book control dropped, the market facts - `execunix` among them - kept: a move)
  pub fn book(&self) -> Option<&BookRef>  (where the operation is a market-data entry)
  pub fn set_book(&mut self, book: Option<BookRef>)  (a control stating nothing is `None`; the operation is not refinalized)
  pub fn with_book(self, book: BookRef) -> Self
  pub fn action(&self) -> Option<MdUpdateAction>
  pub fn is_full_snapshot(&self) -> bool  (`action() == Some(MdUpdateAction::Snapshot)`, the marker every FIX `W` occurrence carries)
  pub fn scope(&self) -> &str  (empty where none)
  impl<K: OperationKind> Element, Event, Market, Operation for OperationEvent<K>  (`is_execution` true for an execution whatever its state, and for an order or a quote where its state reports one - `State::is_execution`, a partial fill or a fill; `finalize` fills the market and the operation, brings the cross codes in step and digests `digest_operation_event` continued with the kind and the book control's scope; `with_previous` is `following_operation`, `merge_with` `merging_operation_event`, `restating` the operation restatement)
  impl<K: OperationKind, E: Event + Operation + ?Sized> From<&E> for OperationEvent<K>  (copies every fact, the identities as stated, onto an operation of this kind with no book control, not refinalized)
pub type Order = OperationElement<OrderKind>;
pub type Quote = OperationElement<QuoteKind>;
pub type Execution = OperationElement<ExecutionKind>;
pub type OrderEvent = OperationEvent<OrderKind>;
pub type QuoteEvent = OperationEvent<QuoteKind>;
pub type ExecutionEvent = OperationEvent<ExecutionKind>;

### yggdryl::graph::trade  [rust/src/graph/trade.rs]
pub struct TradeEvent  (a composite trade: one market operation event whose executions are the sided fills it is made of; the root's identity derives from its own facts and its executions' canonical identities, so two trades stating the same fills are one trade; Clone, Debug, PartialEq)
  pub fn from_parts<E: Event + Operation + ?Sized>(root: &E, executions: Vec<ExecutionEvent>) -> Result<Self>  (the root's facts copied; finalizes the children and sorts them by side, cross code and identity; requires at least one, each on any side - `UNKNOWN` included - at the root instant, with the root ticker where it states one, and cross codes unique within the trade; the root takes the maximum child sequence, earliest creation and recording and latest execution instants, and digests the execution count and each child `curruuid` once)
  pub fn executions(&self) -> &[ExecutionEvent]  (canonical side/cross-code/identity order)
  impl Element, Event, Market, Operation for TradeEvent  (`Event::is_execution` is true; `set_currunix` atomically rebases root and children and re-finalizes them while retaining child `execunix`; following and merging require the same root cross code, combine children by execution cross code and rebase before deriving the composite again)

### yggdryl::graph::book  [rust/src/graph/book.rs]
pub const ENTRY_ID: IdType = IdType::MdEntryId;  (the identifier type an entry's own `MDEntryID(278)` is held under: `mdentryid`)
pub const ENTRY_REF_ID: IdType = IdType::MdEntryRefId;  (the type its `MDEntryRefID(280)` is held under: `mdentryrefid`)
pub struct SnapshotEvent  (the full-snapshot control an empty `W` is: an event stating the scope it replaces under `MdUpdateAction::Snapshot` and no entry of its own; Clone, Debug, PartialEq)
  pub fn snapshot<E: Event + Market + ?Sized>(event: &E, scope: Option<SmolStr>) -> Self  (the one owner of the control role: copies `event`'s event and market facts - never an operation's, a snapshot states none - and finalizes through the market-event digest, so the control is canonical however `event` arrived)
  pub fn book(&self) -> &BookRef  (the action, always `Snapshot`, and the scope)
  impl Element, Event, Market for SnapshotEvent  (`is_execution` is false)
pub struct BookEvent  (one view of a market at one exact nanosecond instant, keyed by `Market::book_crosscode`: its `delta` - the orders and quotes its instant applied since the book before it, in the order applied - and its `events` - every other event its instant recorded, in the order applied: the executions, each resting on no side and moving none, since a fill moves a book through its order's or quote's own report, and the snapshot controls whose membership replacement made it complete; every input `MarketDataKind::is_recorded` does not admit - a trade - is pruned before the fold - and, on a complete book (`is_complete`), its bid and ask sides, each entry an `OrderEvent` or a `QuoteEvent` resting on every side it states a leg for - an order on the side it takes, a quote on its bid and its ask, a two-sided quote one entry shared by both - price ordered, an entry stating no price resting at one unpriced level after every priced one; an `Event` and a `Market` and no operation, stating its first tradable level of each side as `bidpx`/`bidqty` and `askpx`/`askqty`, and `bidccy`/`askccy` the book's currency where that side has one - the top of book every book answers, complete or delta: `best_price`, `best_quantity`, `spread`, `bbo_midpoint`, `median_quantity`, `is_crossed`, `is_locked`; its digest is its market event, its instant, each side's live entries only where it states a snapshot instant, then its delta - its count and each entry's operation word and `curruuid` - then its events - their count and each one's kind and `curruuid` - in the order applied, so a delta book and the complete book rebuilt from it share one identity; the scopes a snapshot replaced are walk state no digest, equality or row reads; Clone, Debug, PartialEq; `Default` `new(0, "")`)
  impl Element, Event, Market for BookEvent  (`with_previous` rebuilds: a delta book over `previous`' sides, its `delta` replayed in the order applied and its `events` kept as they are, whole and under its own identity - over the empty book where it names no `prevuuid`, a code's first book - and a complete book, authoritative, linked to `previous` alone; `None` for another book code, an earlier instant, another book than its `prevuuid` names, a delta book over one holding no sides either, a delta entry that does not replay or a rebuild settling on another top of book than the one it states, and for a complete book where nothing moves; `merge_with` only the same cross code and instant, the later `recdunix` then `currunix` selecting the reference - two complete books' sides joined unless the reference is a snapshot, a complete book authoritative over a delta book, two delta books the reference's facts over the union of both books' `delta` and of both books' `events`; `is_execution` is false)
  pub fn new(unix: i64, symbol: impl Into<String>) -> Self  (an empty complete book of the ticker `symbol`, keyed by it and stating it; an empty `symbol` keys the book `Isin::NONE` and states no ticker)
  pub fn keyed(unix: i64, key: impl Into<String>) -> Self  (an empty complete book keyed `key` - what `Market::book_crosscode` answers for every input it takes: an ISIN, a ticker or `Isin::NONE` - stating neither a ticker nor an ISIN until its first input states each; the empty base a code's first book, a delta book following no book, rebuilds over: `delta.with_previous(&BookEvent::keyed(unix, key))`)
  pub fn is_complete(&self) -> bool  (whether the book is a complete book, holding its sides, rather than a delta book stating its `delta` and `events` alone: a book a caller builds, one a walk emits at a snapshot tick and one `with_previous` rebuilt are complete)
  pub fn alive(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &MarketData>  (every alive entry, each once: the bid side's best first and unpriced last, then the ask side's but those resting on the bid too - a two-sided quote listed with the bids; same-price entries by stated `BookRef::position`, then arrival; nothing on a delta book)
  pub fn alive_on(&self, side: Side) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &MarketData>  (the entries resting on the side `side` takes, best price first and unpriced last, borrowed from the side's store - a two-sided quote on both sides, at its leg's price on each; nothing for a side neither bid nor ask, or on a delta book)
  pub fn delta(&self) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &MarketData>  (the membership operations the book's instant applied since the book before it, in the order applied across both sides: the orders and quotes placed, changed, ended, expired, withdrawn or range-deleted, each the very entry a side holds where it rests; what a delta book states beside its `events` and `with_previous` replays; `orddelta` and `quotes` partition it)
  pub fn ordlive(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &OrderEvent>  (the orders resting on the book: every `alive` entry that is an order, in its order - the bid side's best first and unpriced last, then the ask side's; nothing on a delta book; borrowed, nothing allocated)
  pub fn orddelta(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &OrderEvent>  (the orders among `delta`, in the order applied - placed, changed, ended, expired or withdrawn at the book's instant - each the very entry a side holds where it rests, so an order placed at the instant is also `ordlive`'s; borrowed)
  pub fn quotes(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &QuoteEvent>  (the quotes among `delta`, in the order applied; a quote resting since an earlier instant is `alive`'s and not here; `orddelta` and `quotes` partition `delta`; borrowed)
  pub fn executions(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &ExecutionEvent>  (the executions among `events`, in the order applied, each recorded at the book's instant and resting on no side; borrowed)
  pub fn controls(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &SnapshotEvent>  (the snapshot controls among `events`, in the order applied: each control whose membership replacement made the book complete, so only a complete book states one; `executions` and `controls` partition `events`; borrowed)
  pub fn events(&self) -> impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &MarketData>  (every other event the book's instant recorded, in the order applied: the executions, resting on no side and moving none, and the snapshot controls; an instant recording only an execution is a delta book whose `delta` is empty; `with_previous` replays none of them; a book refuses any other kind among them at `$.events[i]`; borrowed)
  pub fn best_price(&self, side: Side) -> Option<Decimal>  (the first priced level's of the side `side` takes whose `Limit::tradable` holds - a level that cannot trade is skipped, never answered; `None` for an empty side, one of unpriced entries only, one no level of which trades, or a side neither bid nor ask; what the book states as `bidpx` (`BUYS`) and `askpx` (`SELL`), and read from it, so a delta book answers it too)
  pub fn best_quantity(&self, side: Side) -> Option<Decimal>  (exact aggregate quantity at `best_price` - an order its quantity, a quote that leg's - an entry stating none adding nothing; the book's `bidqty` and `askqty`, read from it)
  pub fn limits(&self, side: Side) -> impl Iterator<Item = Limit> + '_  (one `Limit` per level of that side in held order - best first, the unpriced limit last - its entries' `curruuid`s in position order, ties in arrival order, tradable when any of them does not state `tradable = false` - an entry stating nothing trades, and only a level every entry of which states `false` cannot; allocates each limit's `uuids` and nothing else; nothing for a side neither bid nor ask, or on a delta book. What a complete book's row states under `bidlimits` (`BUYS`) and `asklimits` (`SELL`); the first priced limit that can trade is `best_price` and `best_quantity`)
  pub fn depth(&self, side: Side, levels: usize) -> Option<Decimal>  (checked sum of that side's first `levels` limits' quantities, the unpriced limit counted where reached; zero for an empty side or no level; `None` past decimal, for a side neither bid nor ask, or on a delta book)
  pub fn is_crossed(&self) -> bool  (best tradable bid strictly above best tradable ask; a locked book is coherent, a side stating no best crosses nothing)
  pub fn is_locked(&self) -> bool  (both best tradable prices stated and equal)
  pub fn spread(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (best tradable ask less best tradable bid, negative when crossed; `None` where a side states no best or past decimal)
  pub fn imbalance(&self, levels: usize) -> Option<Decimal>  ((bid - ask) / (bid + ask) over the two sides' `depth`; 1 bid-only, -1 ask-only; `None` when the total is zero - both sides empty or no level - past decimal, or on a delta book)
  pub fn bbo_midpoint(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (overflow-safe arithmetic midpoint of a coherent two-sided BBO)
  pub fn median_quantity(&self) -> Option<Decimal>  (overflow-safe two-value median of aggregate best quantities; the one side where one-sided)
  pub fn add_operations<I>(&mut self, operations: I) -> Result<()> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<MarketData>>  (atomic; every input `MarketDataKind::is_recorded` does not admit - a `TradeEvent` - pruned first, so a group of nothing else changes nothing; an `OrderEvent`, `QuoteEvent` or `SnapshotEvent` folds and an `ExecutionEvent` is recorded, each applied order or quote a `delta` entry and each execution and snapshot control an `events` entry in the order applied, an execution moving no side and dating the book's last execution, and any other variant is `InvalidRecord` at `$.operations[index].kind`; one nonregressing `currunix` across the group, a later instant following the book it was - `prevuuid`, `prevunix`, `prevpx`, `prevqty` - and starting its `delta` and `events` again while retaining live depth; an order or a quote rests on every side it states a leg for, as one entry, a leg sized zero resting nowhere, and one resting on no side, first seen ended or ending an entry the book no longer holds places nothing, never refused, and is still in its `delta`; a statement repeating the live entry it continues - every fact but its identity, digests and chain place, the same book control - records nothing; a full-snapshot order, quote or `SnapshotEvent` clears only its symbol + scope partition first, an entry its scope restates kept identity and all; the live key is symbol + scope + cross identity; a stated same-partition `mdentryrefid` resolves before the incoming identity or its `mdentryid`, and a distinct occupied destination is ambiguous and refused; a level whose aggregate quantity would pass decimal is refused at `$.quantity`, naming its price; a delta book refused at `$.alive` - rebuild it with `with_previous` first; the book unchanged on every error)
pub struct BookIterator<I> where I: Iterator, I::Item: Into<Result<MarketData>>  (books from an already sorted infallible or fallible stream of `MarketData`, one per book key and effective timestamp - the key `Market::book_crosscode`: an input's ISIN where it holds one, else its ticker, else `Isin::NONE`, one book per instrument wherever an ISIN is known; a book opens keyed (`BookEvent::keyed`) and takes its ticker and its ISIN from the first input stating each, no input moving them after; the walk folds what `MarketDataKind::is_booked` admits - orders, quotes and snapshot controls - records every execution among the `events` of its instrument's book at its instant, and prunes every other input `is_recorded` does not admit where it is pulled, a FIX message's leaves once it is split, so a pruned input touches no book, no instant and no grid; an identity restated under another key - a chain stated by its ticker alone and then under its ISIN - leaves the book it stood in by a `REMOVED` delta there reporting no fill, a snapshot's member as any other statement, and opens in its own at the same instant, so an entry rests in one book at a time; a book is emitted where its instant recorded a `delta` entry - every order and quote folded but a repeat of the live entry it continues - or an `events` entry - an execution alone emitting a delta book whose `delta` is empty - or at a snapshot tick where it holds an entry, a snapshot emptying a book emitted too, empty and complete, its control among its `events`; complete - its alive entries, its limits, `snapunix` the instant - only at a snapshot tick: every grid tick `snapshot_millis` crosses for every code, the ticks caught up on and the quiet buckets included, and a group that replaced membership - a full refresh, an empty snapshot control, inputs stating a `snapunix`; every other book a delta book, its `delta` and `events` alone beside the top of book they settled on, naming the book it follows as `prevuuid` - none for a code's first book, which follows the empty book - so `Element::with_previous` over the complete book before it rebuilds it; a variant a book does not fold is `InvalidRecord` naming its kind and ends the walk; an operation dated before its book and a group the book refuses are left out with a deduplicated warning, the book standing as it was; each timestamp and book commits its delta and events, expirations and explicit scope-membership snapshots atomically; a source failure follows the completed prefix once and fuses)
  pub fn new(operations: I, snapshot_millis: u64) -> Result<Self>  (zero disables the epoch-aligned snapshot grid, a book then complete only at a full refresh; checked conversion to nanoseconds)
  pub fn with_filter(mut self, filter: impl IntoFilter) -> Result<Self>  (folds only the inputs `filter` keeps: an expression over the `MarketData::field` row, bound once here and answered by the expression engine over one batch per 1,024 recorded inputs pulled ahead; the kind rule prunes first, so a filter narrows what a book folds and never admits what `MarketDataKind::is_recorded` does not; a filter keeping every row installs nothing; refuses the filter's own parse error, a column the row does not carry and an answer that is no boolean)
  impl<I> Iterator<Item = Result<BookEvent>> for BookIterator<I> where I: Iterator, I::Item: Into<Result<MarketData>>
  impl<I> FusedIterator for BookIterator<I> where I: FusedIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<MarketData>>

### yggdryl::graph::candle  [rust/src/graph/candle.rs]  (one OHLC per book cross code and bucket over a sorted stream of books: the best bid, the best ask, the midpoint and the spread, the quantities at the touch when the bucket closed and the books folded; the buckets aligned to a zone's wall clock)
pub struct Ohlc { pub open: Decimal, pub high: Decimal, pub low: Decimal, pub close: Decimal }  (one reading's open, high, low and close over a bucket: the first value, the greatest, the least and the last, a bucket that saw one value stating it four times; Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)
  pub const fn at(value: Decimal) -> Self  (a reading that saw one value)
  pub fn fold(&mut self, value: Decimal)  (a later value: the close moves, the high and the low widen)
pub struct Candle { pub crosscode: SmolStr, pub ticker: Option<SmolStr>, pub start: i64, pub end: i64, pub bid: Option<Ohlc>, pub ask: Option<Ohlc>, pub mid: Option<Ohlc>, pub spread: Option<Ohlc>, pub bidqty: Option<Decimal>, pub askqty: Option<Decimal>, pub books: u64 }  (one book's OHLC over `[start, end)`, nanoseconds UTC: `bid`, `ask`, `mid` and `spread` folded over the books that stated the reading - `best_price` per side, `bbo_midpoint`, `spread` - `None` where none did; `ticker` the first book's; `bidqty`/`askqty` the last book's `best_quantity`; `books` how many folded, each read by its top of book, complete or delta; a value of its own, never a datatype; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)
  pub fn field() -> Result<Field>  (the required struct `candle`, 23 cells: `crosscode: utf8 not null`, `ticker: utf8`, `start` and `end` `datetime64(ns, UTC) not null`, `bidopen`, `bidhigh`, `bidlow`, `bidclose` and the same under `ask`, `mid` and `spread` as a nullable `decimal` each, `bidqty` and `askqty` the same, `books` `uint64 not null`)
  pub fn into_scalar(&self) -> Scalar  (the named struct of the 23 cells)
  pub fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (the named struct, or the ordered row `field()` canonicalizes to, through the field's own value door once; an absent name reads null, a reading stating some but not all of its four cells is refused at `$.candle`)
  pub fn arrow_reader<I>(candles: I, batch_row_size: Option<usize>) -> Result<BatchReader> where I: IntoIterator<Item = Result<Self>>, I::IntoIter: Send + 'static  (candles laid out as batches of `field()`, lazily; a source error follows the completed prefix)
pub struct CandleOptions  (the interval, nanoseconds, and the zone whose wall clock the buckets align to - a daily candle opens at local midnight, hourly candles follow a saving-time change, the skipped hour yielding no candle and the repeated hour one; every edge the earliest instant whose wall clock reads at or after the local edge, so a sorted stream never re-enters a bucket it left; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)
  pub fn new(interval: i64) -> Result<Self>  (aligned to UTC; a non-positive interval refused at `$.interval`)
  pub fn with_timezone(mut self, timezone: Timezone) -> Self
  pub fn interval(&self) -> i64
  pub fn timezone(&self) -> &Timezone
  pub fn from_spelling(text: &str) -> Result<Self>  (a count and a unit - `w`, `d`, `h`, `m`, `s`, `ms`, `us`, `ns`: `30s`, `1m`, `5m`, `1h`, `1d`, `1w` - aligned to UTC; anything else, or an interval past `i64` nanoseconds, refused at `$.interval`)
  pub fn spelling(&self) -> String  (the count and the widest unit dividing the interval exactly, what `from_spelling` reads back: `90s`, `2m`, `1500ms`)
pub struct CandleIterator<I>  (the fold: books already sorted by `get_currunix()` pulled lazily, the candles of a bucket yielded in cross-code order when the stream moves past it and at the end, an empty bucket yielding none; a regression is refused at `$.book.currunix` as `expected an instant at or after <prev>, got <this>`, an instant the zone cannot hold in `i64` nanoseconds at the same path; a source error or a refusal ends the walk after the completed buckets, the open bucket dropped rather than emitted incomplete, and the iterator fuses)
  pub fn new(books: I, options: CandleOptions) -> Self where I: Iterator<Item = Result<BookEvent>>
  pub fn options(&self) -> &CandleOptions
  impl<I: Iterator<Item = Result<BookEvent>>> Iterator for CandleIterator<I>  (Item = Result<Candle>)
  impl<I: Iterator<Item = Result<BookEvent>>> FusedIterator for CandleIterator<I>

### yggdryl::graph::arrow  [rust/src/graph/arrow.rs]
impl MarketData
  pub fn field() -> Result<Field>  (the required `marketdata` struct: `ElementColumn::ALL` and `EventColumn::ALL` - every one nullable because an undated leaf states no clock - then `MarketColumn::ALL`, opening with `marketdatakind` and `marketdatatype`, `OperationColumn::ALL`, the book controls a market-data entry states - `bookscope`, `bookaction` (utf8: its `MDUpdateAction(279)` code, or `snapshot`) and `bookposition` (uint32: its `MDEntryPositionNo(290)`), which a book's delta replays by - then the nullable nested `alive` and `delta` (a book's orders and quotes, alive on either side and applied by its instant, in the order applied), `events` (the executions and snapshot controls its instant recorded, in the order applied), `executions` (a trade's: a book holds none), and `bidlimits` and `asklimits` - a complete book's price levels, `serie<limit>` best first and the unpriced one last, an empty side an empty list; a delta book states `delta` and `events` and leaves `alive` and the limits null; null on every other leaf; 64 columns, 6 + 9 + 35 + 5 + 3 + 6; an operation row, the item of the four nested event lists, is the first 58 and nests nothing; every fact its own typed column, every decimal the registered `decimal`)
  pub fn arrow_reader<I>(elements: I, batch_row_size: Option<usize>, batch_byte_size: Option<u64>) -> Result<BatchReader> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: Into<Result<MarketData>>, I::IntoIter: Send + 'static  (lazy: the source is not pulled until the reader is; laid out column by column from the typed leaves with no per-row `Scalar`; a `None` row size uses the crate default and zero normalizes to one; a byte bound closes a nonempty batch once the rows held reach it; a value is written only as its canonical self - a stale identity or fact is refused on its row; a source or refusal error follows the completed prefix and fuses)
  pub fn delta_serie(source: impl Into<Serie>, kind: Option<MarketDataKind>) -> Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (the delta of the books `source` holds laid out as `marketdata` rows in book order, as a stream pulled with the rows under one plan compiled once: every order and quote each book's instant applied, in the order applied, of `kind` (`ORDR`, `QUOT`) where one is stated, both otherwise; a row that is no book refused by its kind as an item of the stream; what a stage reads a window's orders or quotes out of a table of books with)
  pub fn events_serie(source: impl Into<Serie>, kind: Option<MarketDataKind>) -> Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (the events of the books `source` holds, as `delta_serie` lays out the delta: every execution and snapshot control each book's instant recorded, in the order applied, of `kind` (`EXEC`, or `BOOK` for the controls) where one is stated, both otherwise; `delta_serie`'s refusals; what a stage reads a window's executions out of a table of books with)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(batches: BatchReader) -> Result<impl FusedIterator<Item = Result<MarketData>> + Send + 'static>  (tolerant: the root columns are resolved by name once per stream, whatever their case, any subset in any order, a foreign column ignored and a column of another castable type cast through one plan compiled before the first batch; each column's storage proven once and read per row; a missing or unknown `marketdatakind`, an undated `TRAD` or `BOOK` row, a dated `BOOK` row in a batch with no `alive` column - a non-null `alive` cell a complete book, a null one beside a `delta` or an `events` list a delta book, all three null a snapshot control, an empty `alive` told apart by its `delta` and `events`, its `curruuid` and its snapshot instant - an event-only row a delta book, never a control - an `EXEC` item in `delta`, an item other than `EXEC` or `BOOK` in `events`, a `BOOK` row stating `executions`, or a fact the kind requires, a trade's `executions`, is refused at the first row that needs it; every stated identity and fact must be the one the rebuilt leaf derives - a book's price levels included - and an identity column that stands must state one, a null `curruuid`, `crossuuid`, `currhashcode` or `crosshashcode` refused; a stated `isincode` fills an absent `ISIN` security identifier and must be the one `securityids` states; any other null cell states nothing; two columns naming one fact are refused before a row is read; fused after an error)

### yggdryl::graph::kind  [rust/src/graph/kind.rs]

  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str  (`order`, `quote`, `execution`, `order_event`, `quote_event`, `execution_event`, `trade_event`, `book_event`, `snapshot_event`, `fix`)
  pub fn read(text: &str) -> Option<Self>  (the stored spelling, ignoring ASCII case)
  pub const fn marketdatakind(self) -> MarketDataKind  (the category a row of the kind states: an order `ORDR`, a quote `QUOT`, an execution `EXEC`, a trade `TRAD`, a book or a snapshot control `BOOK`)

### yggdryl::graph::market_data  [rust/src/graph/market_data.rs]
pub enum MarketData { Order(Order), Quote(Quote), Execution(Execution), OrderEvent(OrderEvent), QuoteEvent(QuoteEvent), ExecutionEvent(ExecutionEvent), TradeEvent(TradeEvent), BookEvent(Box<BookEvent>), SnapshotEvent(SnapshotEvent), Fix(Box<FixMsg>) }  (one value over every leaf, resolved once at the boundary that built it; Clone, Debug, PartialEq)
  pub fn kind(&self) -> MarketKind
  pub fn marketdatakind(&self) -> MarketDataKind  (the leaf's kind's category: what the row's `marketdatakind` column states)
  pub fn is_event(&self) -> bool
  pub fn book(&self) -> Option<&BookRef>  (the control of an operation event or a snapshot control)
  pub fn as_fix(&self) -> Option<&FixMsg>  (a borrow of a FIX message held whole)
  pub fn as_order(&self) -> Option<&Order>  (a borrow of that variant; `as_quote`, `as_execution`, `as_order_event`, `as_quote_event`, `as_execution_event`, `as_trade_event`, `as_book_event`, `as_snapshot_event` the same)
  impl Element, Market for MarketData  (delegating to the leaf; `is_after` by instant where both are dated and false otherwise; `with_previous` and `merge_with` the leaf's own for the same variant; `with_previous` also chains an operation event after an operation event of another kind through their facts, its own kind kept; `merge_with` `None` across variants)

  impl From<FixMsg> for MarketData  (holds the message whole, boxed)
  impl TryFrom<MarketData> for FixMsg  (the `Fix` variant; another variant is `InvalidRecord` at `$.kind`)
  impl TryFrom<MarketData> for leaf  (every leaf; another variant is `InvalidRecord` at `$.kind`, expected against actual kind)
  impl From<MarketData> for Result<MarketData>  (the infallible stream item)

### yggdryl::graph::iterator  [rust/src/graph/iterator.rs]
pub struct EventIterator<E, I>  (the one walk: operation events read in their order, a missing `execunix` - a market fact - filled from `currunix` only where `Event::is_execution` says that incoming observation reports an execution, then each stated as following the live element - keeping its own place unless the live element happened at the same instant or later, where it takes the higher of its own and one past the live element's (saturating) - and yielded so; the live elements are kept under the identity every incarnation of one thing shares - the cross element, the element's own where it states no cross code - within the element's `Market::marketdatakind`, so an order and an execution under one cross code are two chains and an element joins only a chain of its own kind; every cross code is stored as `{kind}:{side}:{base}` (`Market::stored_crosscode`), so the two sides of one order or execution identifier are two chains - a quote's side is a tag, its chain one - and an element stating `UKNW` joins the one side its base code - indexed for sided elements alone - or a name it goes by, is alive on, a name alive on a sided chain's side or an unsided element's own tag; an element arriving under a live identity, or, where its own is alive under nothing, under a name a live element goes by - a `(type, value)` pair of its `get_identifiers()`, a parent identifier's value (`Operation::parent_of`) also under its base - is stated as the one after it by its own `with_previous`, a `NEW` stated over a live element that `State::is_new_like` reads leaving `UPDATED`; the yielded element stands as the live one where still alive (live state, not past its expiration) and retires the identity where not, so a filled order ends its chain and a later element under its identity starts afresh; one arriving under the identity the live element arrived under - the same instant and content, logged again - is yielded `Event::restating` it, keeping the earliest execution and recording instants, so the chain grows by nothing; one arriving under the identity a statement the chain moved past at the live element's own instant arrived under is yielded restating that statement, the chain staying where it moved, and a chain a step ends keeps that step and the statements it moved past at the step's instant the same way - one arriving under the identity one of them arrived under restating it, the chain staying ended - until the walk reads a later instant; one before the live element is yielded as it came and changes nothing, one its reading refuses is yielded as it came and stands; every walked element leaves stating its creation - a stated one never replaced, else its chain's earliest, else, starting a chain, its own instant - and a chain whose first element states no cross code stands under that element's identity, every later element carrying it as its `crossuuid`; Debug)
  impl<E, I: Iterator<Item = E>> EventIterator<E, I> where E: Walked  (`Walked` is crate-sealed: implemented for every `E: Event + Operation + Clone` and for `MarketData`, whose `OrderEvent`, `QuoteEvent`, `ExecutionEvent`, `TradeEvent` and `Fix` walk - each following and restating through its own reading - and whose every other variant is yielded unchanged, in place)
  pub fn new(elements: impl IntoIterator<IntoIter = I>, sorted: bool) -> Self  (`sorted` takes the caller's word that the elements arrive in their own order and streams them; otherwise they are collected and stably sorted by `is_after` / `is_before` first)
  pub const fn with_snapshot_ns(mut self, snapshot_ns: i64) -> Self  (a grid: a step in nanoseconds aligned on the epoch, and the walk then yields one owned view of every living identity at each crossed tick, each view the live element as of its tick - dated at it, `currunix` and `snapunix` both the tick, so its `curruuid` is the identity that instant derives - while its content (`currhashcode`), `seqnum`, `prevuuid` and `crossuuid` are the live element's; a view does not advance the chain; a finite deadline emits one owned `EXPIRED` event at that instant and retires the identity; zero or less takes the grid away)
  pub fn snapshot_ns(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the grid step, where there is one)
  pub fn alive(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &E>  (the elements still alive after what was read, one per identity, in no order)
  impl<E: Walked, I: Iterator<Item = E>> Iterator for EventIterator<E, I>  (Item = E; size_hint is the source's)
  impl<E: Walked, I: FusedIterator<Item = E>> FusedIterator for EventIterator<E, I>

### yggdryl::graph::view  [rust/src/graph/view.rs]  (the named readings of a `marketdata` stream, one Plan each over the rows MarketData::arrow_reader writes)
pub enum MarketView { Orders, Quotes, Executions, Trades, Books, Lifecycle { crosscode: SmolStr } }  (one named reading of a `marketdata` stream, one Plan each; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq; Display as `as_str`)
  pub const ALL: [&'static str; 6]  (`orders`, `quotes`, `executions`, `trades`, `books`, `lifecycle`)
  pub const fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str
  pub fn read(text: &str, crosscode: Option<&str>) -> Result<Self>  (ASCII case-insensitive; `lifecycle` requires its crosscode, every other view refuses one; InvalidRecord at `$.view` or `$.crosscode`)
impl MarketData
  pub fn plan(view: &MarketView, lifts: &[FieldPath]) -> Result<Plan>  (built structurally; its text reads back as the plan; lifts appended as projections, a missing key reading null and a missing column refused at bind)
  pub fn apply_view(view: &MarketView, lifts: &[FieldPath], reader: BatchReader) -> Result<BatchReader>  (exactly plan.apply_arrow_reader(reader), bound once)

### yggdryl::graph::serve  [rust/src/graph/serve.rs]  (behind the `http` feature: the HTTP face of a market-data table - the book keys it holds, the candles a key's books fold into, the book standing at an instant and the audit of every entry and delta of the books in a range - as JSON for the display and as CSV for a download; a table is any record location a `Holder` reads books from - an Iceberg folder, an Arrow, Parquet, Avro or CSV leaf, a partitioned folder - and every reading is one filtered read of it - `marketdatakind` the `BOOK` member, the book key, the instants, a `ticker` that is no key resolved by the projected scan `tickers` makes only once the read of it as a key answers nothing - pushed into the location's own record options and read back through `MarketData::from_arrow_reader`, the `BookEvent`s kept and stably sorted by instant; nothing cached, every request reading the table as it stands; each reading answered without HTTP too, the routes only reading the query and spelling its answer)
pub struct BookServiceOptions  (what a service states about itself; Clone, Debug, Default, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)
  pub const DEFAULT_MAX_EVENT_ROWS: u64 = 5_000  (the most rows `/api/events` answers when the query states no `limit`, and the most it answers whatever the query states)
  pub fn new() -> Self  (no snapshot grid, `DEFAULT_MAX_EVENT_ROWS` event rows)
  pub fn with_snapshot_millis(mut self, snapshot_millis: u64) -> Self  (the grid a capture is folded on before it lands in a table - what `yggdryl market serve --snapshot-millis` states and hands `BookIterator::new`; zero none - and so how far back `book` reads: a walk emits a book whole only at a grid tick holding an entry and at a full refresh, so with no grid the book at an instant is rebuilt from its first appearance or last full refresh; the service reads books already folded and re-folds nothing)
  pub fn with_max_event_rows(mut self, max_event_rows: u64) -> Self  (at least one; the CSV audit is never bounded)
  pub fn snapshot_millis(&self) -> u64
  pub fn max_event_rows(&self) -> u64
pub struct BookTable  (one served table: a name and the location holding its `marketdata` rows; Debug)
  pub fn name(&self) -> &str  (what every route's `table` parameter states)
  pub fn holder(&self) -> &Holder  (the location the books are read from)
pub struct BookQuery { pub table: String, pub ticker: String, pub from: i64, pub to: i64, pub timezone: Timezone, pub interval: Option<CandleOptions>, pub side: Option<Side> }  (one question about one book key's books over `[from, to)` - `ticker` the key (`Market::book_crosscode`: the ISIN, the ticker or `XX0000000000`), else the ticker one key's books state - nanoseconds UTC, `to` exclusive; `timezone` the zone instants are rendered in and a naive instant is read in, `interval` how candles are bucketed - `None` a minute in that zone - `side` the side the audit keeps - the entries alive on it and the delta and events entries standing on it - `None` both; what `/api/candles`, `/api/events` and the audit routes read off their query; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)
  pub fn from_parameters(parameters: &Parameters<'_>) -> Result<Self>  (`table` and `ticker` required; `from` and `to` required ISO-8601 instants - one stating an offset or `Z` that instant, a naive one a wall clock in `tz`; `tz` an IANA zone this build has rules for, default `UTC`; `interval` a candle spelling (`30s`, `5m`, `1h`, `1d`); `side` `bid` or `ask` in any case; `InvalidRecord` at `$.<parameter>` for one missing or unreadable, at `$.to` when `from` is not before `to`, at `$.tz` for a zone this build has no rules for)
  pub fn candle_options(&self) -> Result<CandleOptions>  (`interval` where stated, else a minute aligned to `timezone`)
pub struct BookService  (the service over its tables; Debug)
  pub fn new(options: BookServiceOptions) -> Self  (over no table yet)
  pub fn with_table(mut self, name: impl Into<String>, holder: Holder) -> Self  (a name already listed is replaced)
  pub fn tables(&self) -> &[BookTable]  (in the order they were added)
  pub fn options(&self) -> &BookServiceOptions
  pub fn events_field() -> Result<Field>  (the audit row: the required struct `event` of `bookunix: datetime64(ns, UTC) not null`, `role: utf8 not null` - `alive`, `delta` or `event` - then every flat column of `MarketData::field` - the element, event, market and operation columns and the book controls `bookscope`, `bookaction` and `bookposition` - the nested `alive`, `delta`, `events`, `executions`, `bidlimits` and `asklimits` left out; 60 columns)
  pub fn route(self: Arc<Self>, server: &Server, prefix: &str) -> Result<Url>  (the eight `GET` routes under `{prefix}/api` - `tables`, `tickers`, `candles`, `book`, `events`, `audit.csv`, `audit.csv.gz`, `audit.csv.zst` - and the endpoint, `prefix` under the server's own URL (`Server::url_of`; `public_url_of` is the one behind a proxy), a prefix routed again replacing the service that answered there; `HEAD` answered off a `GET`, `POST` the server's 405; every answer `Cache-Control: no-store` and its `Content-Type`, JSON `application/json`, a refusal JSON `{"error": "<text>"}` - 400 for a parameter at `$.table`, `$.ticker`, `$.from`, `$.to`, `$.at`, `$.tz`, `$.interval`, `$.side` or `$.limit`, 400 at `$.ticker` too for a ticker two keys' books state, 404 for a table, a key or ticker or a book there is none of, 500 otherwise; `/api/book` answering `isincode` and `complete` beside the book's readings, its entries and limits none where `complete` is false; instants spelled RFC 9557 in `tz` with nine fraction digits, decimals text, uuids canonical text, counts integers; the audit routes the events reader written by the CSV medium into a `Buffer` of the suffix's media type, `Content-Type` `text/csv`, `application/gzip` or `application/zstd` and `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="audit-<key>-<from>-<to>.<suffix>"`; a prefix carrying a query, a fragment or a control byte refused)
  pub fn tickers(&self, table: &str) -> Result<Scalar>  (a sequence of `{key, ticker, crosscode, from, to, books}` structs, one per book key (`Market::book_crosscode`: the instrument's ISIN, else the ticker, else `XX0000000000`) ordered by key, `ticker` the first ticker the key's books state or null, `crosscode` the stored one, `from` the whole second the first book stands in and `to` the whole second after the last, both `datetime64(ns, UTC)`, so `[from, to)` holds every book; every book listed; one projected scan, `select ticker, crosscode, currunix where marketdatakind = 'BOOK'`; `Absent` for a table the service does not hold)
  pub fn candles(&self, query: &BookQuery) -> Result<Vec<Candle>>  (the books of `query.ticker` - a book key, or the ticker of one key's books - in range, folded by `CandleIterator` under `query.candle_options()`; `InvalidRecord` at `$.to` when `from` is not before `to` and for a ticker two keys' books state, `Absent` for a table not held or a key or ticker the table holds no book of, an empty range of a known key the empty vector)
  pub fn book(&self, table: &str, ticker: &str, at: i64) -> Result<Option<BookEvent>>  (the book of `ticker` - a book key, or the ticker of one key's books - at `at`, nanoseconds UTC, whole: the last origin stored at or before it - a complete book, or a delta book naming no `prevuuid` - and each delta book after it folded over it in instant order, as `Element::with_previous` rebuilds one; best effort, a delta book the book before it does not rebuild answered as it stands, holding no entry, `BookEvent::is_complete` saying so and `/api/book` answering `"complete": false`; one read of the rows at or before `at`; `None` when none stands there yet, which `/api/book` answers as 404; `InvalidRecord` for a ticker two keys' books state)
  pub fn events(&self, query: &BookQuery) -> Result<BatchReader>  (the audit rows of `events_field()`: for every book of `query.ticker` - a book key, or the ticker of one key's books - in range, in instant order, its alive entries (`alive`) where the table holds it whole - at a snapshot tick - the orders and quotes its instant applied (`delta`), then the executions and snapshot controls it recorded (`event`), each in the order applied and kept where it stands on `query.side`; unbounded, what the CSV audit writes whole and `/api/events` bounds at `limit`, answering `{rows, truncated}`)

### yggdryl::txhash::value  [rust/src/txhash/value.rs]
pub const UNIX_WIDTH: usize = 8;
  pub const fn width(algorithm: DigestAlgorithm) -> usize
  pub const fn dtype(algorithm: DigestAlgorithm) -> DataType
  pub struct TxHash
  pub const fn new(unix: i64, digest: Digest) -> Self
  pub fn new_in(unix: i64, unit: TimeUnit, digest: Digest) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn unix(&self) -> i64
  pub const fn unit(&self) -> TimeUnit
  pub const fn digest(&self) -> Digest
  pub const fn algorithm(&self) -> DigestAlgorithm
  pub const fn width(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn dtype(&self) -> DataType
  pub fn with_unit(self, unit: TimeUnit) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn into_uuid(self) -> Result<crate::Uuid>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7: the instant is restated and floored directly to signed i64 microseconds and packed as the millisecond it falls in with the microsecond within it in rand_a's high ten, and the digest is stored whole behind it - its top two bits above the variant, its low sixty-two below; nothing is hashed a second time and nothing is narrowed, so the projection is lossless and two values share an identifier only where their microsecond and all 64 digest bits agree; requires a 64-bit digest; checked unit conversion; refuses an instant before the epoch or past the microsecond count the 48-bit millisecond timestamp reaches at `$`; zero successful allocations; raw TxHash bytes and ordering unchanged)
  pub fn into_sequenced_uuid(self, sequence: u64, seed: u64) -> Result<crate::Uuid>  (sequence-ordered RFC 9562 UUIDv7: the instant is restated to Unix milliseconds, `sequence.min(4095)` occupies `rand_a`, and `rand_b` holds the low 62 bits of XXH3 over the 64-bit digest and whole sequence with `seed`; larger sequences remain probabilistically distinct in the terminal lane; requires a 64-bit digest and a UUIDv7 timestamp; zero successful allocations)
  pub fn into_ordered_bytes(self) -> Result<[u8; 16]>  (the exact microsecond ordering with no UUID layout: sign-flipped signed i64 microseconds big-endian in 0..8, all 64 digest bits in 8..16; requires a 64-bit digest; checked unit conversion; Rust-only)
  pub fn into_bytes(self) -> TxHashBytes
  pub fn from_bytes(unit: TimeUnit, algorithm: DigestAlgorithm, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn into_datetime(self) -> Scalar
  pub fn into_scalar(self) -> Scalar
  pub fn from_scalar(unit: TimeUnit, algorithm: DigestAlgorithm, value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn stable_hash(&self) -> u64
  pub struct TxHashBytes
  impl Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Display, FromStr, Serialize, Deserialize for TxHash  (Ord is unit-major, then signed count, then digest; instants are not normalized across units)

### yggdryl::s3tables  [rust/src/s3tables/mod.rs]
  (behind the `s3tables` feature, which implies `s3` and `iceberg`: the catalog of an Amazon S3 Tables table bucket - its namespaces, its tables, and the metadata location a commit moves under a version token; every verb exactly one request, a listing one per page of 250; every request an `http::Request` signed by `Request::with_sigv4(session, "s3tables", region)` and sent over the session's HTTP client - a refused key read by that door, a session answering no set refused before any request; the values are descriptions and never shadow the warehouse's `Table`/`Namespace`/`Tables`/`Namespaces`, the verbs the crate's `create_*`/`get_*`/`remove_*` beside the service's own `rename_table` and `update_table_metadata_location`; Rust only)
  pub struct S3Tables  (Clone shares the session; Debug renders no credential: the stated endpoint holds no user information, the session masks its own)

  pub fn with_region(mut self, region: impl Into<String>) -> Self  (over the ARN's and the session's; blank states none)
  pub fn try_with_endpoint_url(mut self, url: impl AsRef<str>) -> Result<Self>  (over the session's and the partition's host, read once; blank states none; not http or https, no host, a query, a fragment or user information refused as Error::Parse, the user information never repeated)
  pub fn session(&self) -> &Session
  pub fn region_of(&self, bucket: Option<&Arn>) -> Result<String>  (stated on the client, else the table bucket ARN's, else the session's; none refused naming how to state one; one that is no host label refused as Error::Parse naming where it came from)
  pub fn endpoint_url(&self, region: &str) -> Result<String>  (stated, else `Session::service_endpoint("s3tables", region)?`: the session's stated or configured endpoint - AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3TABLES, AWS_ENDPOINT_URL, the profile's `[services]` entry `s3tables`, its `endpoint_url` - else `https://s3tables[-fips].{region}.{suffix}` with the session's FIPS and dual-stack switches; a stated or configured endpoint used whatever either switch says, as botocore uses one; what the session refuses of a configured endpoint, and one carrying a query, a fragment or user information, refused)

### yggdryl::http::request  [rust/src/http/request.rs]

  pub fn with_headers(self, headers: Headers) -> Self

  pub fn with_json(self, value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn with_form<I, K, V>(self, pairs: I) -> Self where I: IntoIterator<Item = (K, V)>, K: AsRef<str>, V: AsRef<str>
  pub fn with_authorization(self, authorization: Authorization) -> Self
  pub fn with_timeout(self, timeout: Duration) -> Self
  pub fn with_follow_redirects(self, follow: bool) -> Self
  pub fn with_idempotent(self, idempotent: bool) -> Self  (the caller attests the request does no harm twice, in place of what its method says: a POST its service documents idempotent is retried as a GET is, false keeps a GET from going twice; send_reader is never retried)
  pub fn with_attempt_headers(self, headers: impl Fn(&Attempt<'_>) -> Result<Headers> + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self  (headers computed at the top of every attempt from the Attempt as it is about to go out - its number from 1, the hop's method and URL, the headers already on it, the body - merged over everything else the attempt carries; an error it returns is the request's and never retried; not called for a redirect hop to another origin, where nothing it makes is sent)
  pub fn with_max_attempts(self, attempts: u32) -> Self  (this request's attempts in place of the client's, zero is one; every retry still paid from the client's one budget)
  pub fn with_connect_timeout(self, timeout: Duration) -> Self  (the bound on opening this request's connection, in place of the pool's)
  pub fn with_deadline(self, deadline: Duration) -> Self  (one bound on the whole of one attempt, beside the per-phase with_timeout)
  pub fn with_retry_on(self, rule: impl Fn(Status, &Headers, &[u8]) -> bool + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self  (for an idempotent request whose answer is no success and no status retried anyway: at most 64 KiB of the body read and handed to the rule beside the status and the headers, true retrying under the rules every retry reads; an answer not retried is handed back whole)
  pub fn with_direct(self, direct: bool) -> Self  (never through a proxy, whatever the options or the environment name)
  pub fn idempotent(&self) -> Option<bool>
  pub fn max_attempts(&self) -> Option<u32>
  pub fn connect_timeout(&self) -> Option<Duration>
  pub fn deadline(&self) -> Option<Duration>
  pub fn is_direct(&self) -> bool
  pub fn with_sigv4(self, session: &aws::Session, service: impl Into<SmolStr>, region: impl Into<String>) -> Self  [rust/src/aws/request.rs]  (behind `aws`: every attempt signed with Signature Version 4 as the session answers at that moment - the hop's method, the host with the port the URL names, the path as sent, the query, the content-type, content-md5 and x-amz-* headers, the SHA-256 of the body; the canonical URI as sent for the S3 family and normalized and encoded once more for every other service; no credential source answering refused by name rather than sent unsigned; a streamed body refused outside the S3 family; a 400 or 403 naming a refused key told to the session and the request sent once more, whatever its method, when another key answers)
  pub struct Attempt<'a>  (Clone, Copy, Debug naming no header value: one attempt as the request's hooks see it)
  pub const fn number(&self) -> u32  (from 1 within a hop)
  pub const fn method(&self) -> Method
  pub const fn url(&self) -> &'a Url
  pub const fn headers(&self) -> &'a Headers  (what the attempt carries before the hook's own)
  pub const fn body(&self) -> Option<&'a [u8]>  (the bytes in hand; None for no body and for a streamed one)
  pub const fn is_streamed(&self) -> bool  (send_reader: the body cannot be read beforehand)
  pub fn with_pagination(self, pagination: Pagination) -> Self
  pub fn with_records(self, path: FieldPath) -> Self
  pub fn with_media_type(self, media_type: MediaType) -> Self
  pub fn method(&self) -> Method
  pub fn url(&self) -> &Url
  pub fn headers(&self) -> &Headers
  pub fn body(&self) -> &Body
  pub fn session(&self) -> &Session
  pub fn send(&self) -> Result<Response>
  pub fn stream(&self) -> Result<Response>
  pub fn send_reader(&self, reader: &mut dyn Read, length: u64) -> Result<Response>
  pub fn pages(&self) -> Pages
  pub fn stats(&self) -> StatsSnapshot

  pub fn http3(&self) -> bool  (feature `http3`: QUIC on the UDP twin and TLS offering `h2` on the TCP port, under a self-signed certificate)
  pub fn with_http3(self, http3: bool) -> Self  (feature `http3`)
  pub fn server_header(&self) -> &str
  pub fn with_server_header(self, server_header: impl Into<String>) -> Self

  pub fn public_url(&self) -> Option<&Url>  (the base of every URL the server states, when stated; suppresses Alt-Svc)
  pub fn with_public_url(self, url: Url) -> Self  (http or https, no query, fragment or user information, validated by bind_with)
  pub fn trusted_proxies(&self) -> &[IpNetwork]  (the peers whose forwarded fields are read; none by default; also the peers whose cleartext HTTP/2 :scheme is believed)
  pub fn with_trusted_proxies<I, S>(self, networks: I) -> Result<Self> where I: IntoIterator<Item = S>, S: AsRef<str>  (IP addresses or CIDR networks)
  pub fn forwarded_headers(&self) -> &[ForwardedHeader]  (the forwarded fields read from a trusted peer; ForwardedHeader::DEFAULT unless set)

  pub fn path_prefix(&self) -> Option<&str>  (stripped before routing; a request outside it is routed as it is)
  pub fn with_path_prefix(self, prefix: &str) -> Result<Self>  (`/` is no prefix)
  pub struct Server  (Debug; Drop shuts down)
  pub const MAX_RECORDED: usize = 65_536;
  pub const MAX_RECORDED_BYTES: usize = 64 << 20;

pub enum Holder
  Buffer(Buffer)

  HttpSession(crate::http::Session), HttpRequest(crate::http::Request), HttpResponse(crate::http::Response), HttpStream(crate::http::Stream)  (behind the `http` feature)
  ZipNode(crate::zip::ZipNode), ZipPath(crate::zip::ZipPath), ZipLeaf(crate::zip::ZipLeaf)
  Buffered(Box<Buffered<Self>>), Coded(Box<Coded>), Text(Box<crate::text::Text<Self>>), Media(Box<crate::media::Media>)  (each wraps another Holder, so each is boxed)
  Uri(Uri)  (an identifier holding what it names, resolved through from_url on first use; From<Uri>, From<Url>, From<Urn>, From<Arn>)
  Catalog(Box<crate::Catalog>), Namespace(Box<crate::Namespace>), Table(Box<crate::Table>)  (the warehouse objects as handles: a catalog or a namespace a container whose `ls` yields its children as handles and whose byte verbs are `Error::NotAtomic`, a table every verb its implementation's; From<Catalog>, From<Namespace>, From<Table>, From<Object>)

  pub fn file(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self>

  pub fn zip(handle: Self) -> Self

### yggdryl::root::iobase  [rust/src/iobase.rs]
pub const DEFAULT_STREAM_BATCH_SIZE: usize = 64 * 1024;
  pub trait IOBase: Send + IOMedia
  fn pstream_bytes(&self, position: u64, batch_size: usize) -> Result<ByteStream<'_>>
  fn owned_stream_bytes(&self, position: u64) -> Result<Option<Box<dyn std::io::Read + Send + 'static>>>  (the bytes from `position` as a reader that outlives the handle where the backend has one of its own - an object store's one resuming `GET` for the whole drain; the default `None`, which a text read answers by reopening the handle at its location and reading through a cursor over it)

  fn create_bytes(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<()>  (required, beside write_all_bytes: the whole value written only where the location holds none, published on return; a value already there is Error::Conflict naming the location and left byte-identical, a container its own write refusal; one call to the handle beneath, won or lost, so of N concurrent creators exactly one returns Ok on local storage, the local and memory filesystems, ZIP, S3, Azure, GCS, an HTTP request and a Buffer; a bridged filesystem and an HTTP origin ignoring preconditions are not exclusive)
  fn append_bytes(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<u64>

  fn write_scalar(&mut self, value: &Scalar) -> Result<()>

  pub struct ArrowWriteSession

  pub fn append(options: &RecordOptions) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn merge(options: &RecordOptions) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn new(mode: IOMode, options: &RecordOptions) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn push(&mut self, handle: &mut (impl IOBase + ?Sized), batches: BatchReader) -> Result<bool>
  pub fn finish(&mut self, handle: &mut (impl IOBase + ?Sized)) -> Result<IOResult>  (the rows every pushed chunk carried and the rows its shaping kept)
  pub fn abort(&mut self)
  pub struct Reader<'source>
  pub struct Writer<'target>

### yggdryl::root::ioresult  [rust/src/ioresult.rs]
pub struct IOResult  (Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, Eq, Ord, Hash, serde, Display `read N rows, wrote N, skipped N`, Add, AddAssign, Sum - saturating; what every IOMedia write door and ArrowWriteSession::finish answer: read_rows pulled from the source, written_rows taken by the destination, skipped_rows read and not written - the rows a `where` kept out, the part of the batch a bound fell in, and the rows an append to an Iceberg table stating identifier-field-ids left out for a key it held; what lies past a bound is never pulled and counts nowhere; a write cut into several commits answers their sum; it says nothing of what was replaced)
  pub read_rows: u64
  pub written_rows: u64
  pub skipped_rows: u64
  pub const fn new(read_rows: u64, written_rows: u64) -> Self  (skipped_rows the read rows less the written, never below zero)
  pub const fn is_empty(&self) -> bool  (no row read and none written)
  pub fn stable_hash(&self) -> u64

### yggdryl::csv  [rust/src/csv/mod.rs]  (comma-separated values, RFC 4180, as record media over any byte handle: `text/csv` and `text/tab-separated-values` read and write through the ordinary record surface, streaming - a record cut at a time, a read holding one batch and a count one record; a declared field is the contract every cell is read under, else the header names the columns and a bounded sample types them; compression and charsets are the handle's, `trades.csv.gz` gzip by name and `;charset=windows-1252` a declared charset; `media`, `options`, `reader` and `writer` are private modules, every public name re-exported here)

pub fn overwrite_arrow_reader<H: IOBase + ?Sized>( handle: &mut H, batches: BatchReader, options: &CsvOptions, ) -> Result<()>  (the header - under `header` - then one record per row through the handle's coding and charset: text and code leaves as their text, numbers, booleans, temporals and uuids through the canonical `Display`, a byte leaf base64, a nested value compact JSON, null the first `null_values` spelling; a cell quoted when it holds the separator, the quote, CR or LF, opens or closes with a blank, spells a null value, or is a first cell opening with the comment byte, a quote inside doubled or escaped; refused by name under `quote` None; nothing reaches the handle until the last batch is encoded)

### yggdryl::iceberg::pointer  [rust/src/iceberg/pointer.rs]  (what names a table's current metadata document when its folder does not; a pointed table reads the one document named, writes each next as `metadata/{version:05}-{uuid}.metadata.json` from 00000 and publishes it under the token held - no read or commit listing, writing a hint or removing, the table's own `ls` refused and its `remove` the pointer's; a refused publication rebases an append or a metadata-only commit and is a CommitConflict for an overwrite, a merge or a compaction)
pub trait MetadataPointer: std::fmt::Debug + Send + Sync
  fn current(&self) -> Result<PointerState>  (the document named now - none before the first - and the token a publication is conditioned on)
  fn publish(&self, token: &str, location: &Url) -> Result<PointerState>  (a pointer no longer at `token` is a failure is_conflict answers; any other failure leaves the publication in doubt)
  fn remove(&self) -> Result<()>  (provided: drop the table from the catalog that keeps it, what a pointed table's IOBase `remove` asks; the provided method refuses as Error::Unsupported naming the pointer, a catalog that drops a table answers it - a table already gone is dropped)
pub struct PointerState  (Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)
  pub fn new(location: Option<Url>, token: impl Into<SmolStr>) -> Self
  pub fn location(&self) -> Option<&Url>
  pub fn token(&self) -> &str

pub struct IcebergTable<H: IOBase>  (the root eager, the current metadata document read on the first verb that needs it; Clone where H: Clone, opening afresh; Eq, Hash over the path; ObjectValue, TableValue, IOBase, IOMedia, so a warehouse `Table::Iceberg` holds one over a `Handle`; a pointed table's IOBase `ls` refused as Error::Unsupported, touching nothing, and its `remove(recursive)` the pointer's `remove` whatever `recursive` says - its catalog keeps it, and drops it)

  pub fn create_pointed(root: H, format_version: FormatVersion, schema: Field, spec: PartitionSpec, pointer: Arc<dyn MetadataPointer>) -> Result<Self>  (the pointer read once and naming no document, else Error::Conflict; the first document `metadata/00000-{uuid}.metadata.json`, version 0, published under the token read; the default order as `create` makes it)
  pub fn at(path: impl IntoObjectPath, root: H) -> Self  (described at its warehouse path, nothing read; an empty path is the root's last segment)
  pub fn from_url(location: impl AsRef<crate::Uri>, properties: &Properties) -> Result<Self>  (`IcebergTable<Handle>` only: the table a location names - a Url or any identifier that locates one - rooted on the handle `Holder::from_url` opens for it under the properties, resolved on first use and by every clone, opened as `open` opens one and stating the properties less the ones the store's reader took (`Holder::is_backend_property`, crate-private: the object store options' under `s3`, the HTTP options' under `http`, none for a local path); under `s3tables` an `s3tables://<bucket>/<namespace>/<table>` location at 1 `GetTableMetadataLocation` or a table's ARN at 1 `GetTable`, described and pointed at the control plane, a bucket or a namespace refused by its kind at `$.url`)

### yggdryl::text::line  [rust/src/text/line.rs]
pub struct TextLine  (one text row and the `Event` of the graph it is: what the reader cut - the index, the identifier it was read under - answered as a location where it is one and resolved to one where it is a name - the handle's modification time, the whole body with the row header included, the edges stripped and the byte limit applied - and the shared `Arc<TextOptions>` it reads itself by; every other fact is a reading of the body under the options, resolved on its first ask, once, into a slot of its own, so a caller reading the body and the row number resolves nothing else; a `set_` states a fact over the line's own reading, and `set_body` drops every resolved slot; the body is never empty, refused at both doors that set one, which is why the `body` column a read answers is not nullable; equality, order and hash read the stated facts alone)
  pub fn from_bytes(index: u64, body: TextBytes, options: Arc<TextOptions>) -> Result<Self>  (where the bytes become text: what is not UTF-8 is decoded once, each byte as its Windows-1252 character, and counted; an empty body is refused, naming the line, because a line is the line it holds)
  pub const fn index(&self) -> u64
  pub fn set_index(&mut self, index: u64)
  pub fn options(&self) -> &TextOptions
  pub fn sourceuri(&self) -> Option<&Uri>  (the identifier the line was read under - what the handle is addressed by, which is not always a place: a read through a name answers that name here; shared with every other line of that read, and what the cross code spells, so the code does not move with the directory a name resolved in)
  pub fn sourceurl(&self) -> Option<&Url>  (where the line was read from: the same value read as a location, which a located read - the common case - spells with the same text, and where a name resolves to by Uri::locator, which roots a URN's spelled path in the process working directory; none where the identifier resolves nowhere)
  pub const fn shared_url(&self) -> Option<&Arc<Url>>
  pub fn set_sourceuri(&mut self, uri: Option<Arc<Uri>>)  (the identifier is narrowed - and a name resolved, one working-directory read - once here rather than once per row, so `sourceurl` answers it where it is a location and where it resolves to where it is a name; refreshes the derived cross code, cross hash and the two identities)
  pub fn with_sourceuri(mut self, uri: Arc<Uri>) -> Self
  pub const fn handle_mtime(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the modification time of the handle the line was read from, nanoseconds UTC: what `mtime` falls back to)
  pub fn set_handle_mtime(&mut self, mtime: Option<i64>)
  pub fn with_handle_mtime(mut self, mtime: i64) -> Self
  pub fn bodytype(&self) -> &MimeType  (the stated classification, else the payload's, read on the first ask)
  pub fn set_bodytype(&mut self, bodytype: Option<MimeType>)  (unsaid, it resolves afresh)
  pub fn with_bodytype(mut self, bodytype: MimeType) -> Self
  pub fn body(&self) -> &str  (the line past its row header)
  pub const fn body_bytes(&self) -> &TextBytes
  pub fn set_body(&mut self, body: TextBytes) -> Result<()>  (drops every resolved reading and the header the cut matched; captures a caller stated stand, the payload then the whole new body, and so does every other stated fact; an empty body is refused and leaves the line as it was)
  pub const fn decoded_byte_size(&self) -> u64
  pub const fn dropped_byte_size(&self) -> Option<u64>
  pub const fn set_dropped_byte_size(&mut self, size: Option<u64>)
  pub fn captures(&self) -> &[Option<TextBytes>]  (the row header matched over the body on the first ask, one slot per declared capture in the order the expression declares them, each a range of the body's page; every slot empty where the header did not match, none under no header)
  pub fn capture(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn set_captures(&mut self, captures: Vec<Option<TextBytes>>) -> Result<()>  (the line's word over its header, decoded as a body is)
  pub fn with_captures(mut self, captures: Vec<Option<TextBytes>>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn entries(&self) -> Option<&TextEntries>  (the stated tree, else the payload's own by `TextEntries::from_bytes` on the first ask; `None` where it states no pair)
  pub fn entries_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut TextEntries>  (the tree moves from the resolved slot to the stated fact on the first mutation, so it stands over a body set later)
  pub fn set_entries(&mut self, entries: Option<TextEntries>)  (stated, a tree or its absence stands over the payload's own)
  pub fn with_entries(mut self, entries: TextEntries) -> Self
  pub fn get_entry_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&TextEntry>
  pub fn entry_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Result<&TextEntry>
  pub fn get_entry_by_path_mut(&mut self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&mut TextEntry>
  pub fn set_entry_by_path(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, value: TextBytes) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_entry_by_path(&mut self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<TextEntry>
  pub fn mtime(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  (the header's `mtime` capture as an instant at the column's clock where it declares one and the line matched, else the handle's modification time, else none; a capture that is not an instant is a refusal naming the row, the object and the column)
  pub fn seqnum(&self) -> Result<u64>  (the row number under `start_rownum`, else the physical index; an explicitly stated event fact wins)
  pub fn state(&self) -> Result<&State>  (a `state` capture, else `UNKNOWN`)
  pub fn creaunix(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  (a `creaunix` capture as an instant, else the first instant the read that cut the line dated a line of the object by, else none; a capture that does not read as an instant is refused by name)
  pub fn prevunix(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  (a `prevunix` capture as an instant, else the instant the read that cut the line dated the line before it by - none for an object's first line and after an undated one, each object read starting again; no `prevuuid` is stated, so no line identity moves; a capture that does not read as an instant is refused by name)
  pub fn recdunix(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  // and exprunix, snapunix: the capture of that exact name as an instant, else none; there is no `execunix`, which is a market fact and not a line's - a capture of that name is an ordinary text column
  pub fn prevuuid(&self) -> Result<Option<Uuid>>  (a `prevuuid` capture, else none)
  pub fn named_captures(&self) -> &BTreeMap<String, String>  (the named captures the line matched, each under the capture's name: what a row header states about the line, an element of nothing else - stated once, else resolved from the captures on the first ask)
  pub fn set_named_captures(&mut self, captures: BTreeMap<String, String>)  (replaces what the line matched)
  pub fn element_fact(&self, column: ElementColumn) -> Option<Scalar>  (what the line states under one of the six element columns, as the column's cell: the fixed identity facts through the trait doors)
  pub fn event_fact(&self, column: EventColumn) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>  (what the line states under one of the nine event columns, as the column's cell: the capture-fed facts through the refusing readings above, so a capture the fact cannot read refuses by name; the rest through the trait doors)
  impl Element, Event for TextLine  (the readings above through the trait doors, a refusal read as the fact's default: `get_currunix` is `mtime`, the handle's time over a refused capture, the epoch where the line has none; `get_seqnum` the row number; `get_currhashcode` the XXH3-64 of the cross code, the row number and the body, so two identical bodies read at one instant stay apart; `get_crosscode` the `sourceuri`'s canonical text - the identifier the line was read under, which a located read spells as its URL and a named read spells as the name rather than where it resolved - else empty, with `crosshashcode` derived; `get_curruuid` is `time_uuid` over millisecond time, the row number's ordered sequence lane and the cross-seeded XXH3 payload, nil where the instant has no UUIDv7; `get_crossuuid` from the same cross hash; explicitly stated event facts win; `get_srcuuids` what was stated, none otherwise; `is_after` by instant, then index; `finalize` lets the identity quartet derive again; `with_previous` is `Event::following`, `merge_with` is `Event::merging`)
  impl Clone, Debug, Display, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash for TextLine  (over the stated facts alone, never a resolved slot)
  impl From<&TextLine> for TextLine  (one clone: what a borrowed line pays where an owned one is wanted)

pub struct SortOptions  (Clone, Copy, Debug, Default - ascending, nulls last, the plan's `order by` and DuckDB's default and the opposite of arrow_schema::SortOptions::default(), which puts nulls first - Eq, Hash, Ord; Display the suffix the plan's ordering writes after its key, ` desc`, ` nulls first`, both, or empty; FromStr of that suffix, asc/desc and nulls first/last in either order, case folded, a stray or repeated word refused by name)

### yggdryl::root::spill  [rust/src/spill.rs]  (the bound a column stays resident under, and the walk that writes a column's buffers to one private file and maps them back; SpillOptions and DEFAULT_SPILL_BYTE_SIZE re-exported at the root)
pub const DEFAULT_SPILL_BYTE_SIZE: u64  (64 MiB: the bound SpillOptions::new states)
pub struct SpillOptions  (the bound in bytes and the folder the spill files are created in, None the platform temporary folder; Clone, Debug, Default = new(), Eq by the bound and the folder's URL)
  pub const NEVER: u64  (u64::MAX: a bound nothing passes, so nothing spills)
  pub const fn new() -> Self
  pub const fn with_byte_size(mut self, byte_size: u64) -> Self
  pub fn with_folder(mut self, folder: LocalFolder) -> Self
  pub const fn byte_size(&self) -> u64
  pub const fn folder(&self) -> Option<&LocalFolder>
  pub const fn is_never(&self) -> bool
  pub fn from_env() -> Result<&'static Self>  (the process default, read once from YGGDRYL_SPILL_BYTE_SIZE and YGGDRYL_SPILL_FOLDER - both unset is new() - and held for the process; a value that is not a byte count or a folder path is refused naming the variable; what every door the crate lays a column out at settles under)
  pub fn install_env(options: Self) -> Result<()>  (states the process default before anything reads it; refused by name once from_env has answered or install_env has been called)

### yggdryl::root::join  [rust/src/join.rs]  (the hash join engine: the kinds, the sides and the options of a join, re-exported at the root, its sides any Serie; the plan, the build table, the key range prune, the grace partitions and the output iterator are crate-private and reached through the three join_with verbs and the plan's join clause)
pub const DEFAULT_JOIN_SUFFIX: &str  ("_right": what a right column colliding with a left name is suffixed by)
pub const DEFAULT_PUSHDOWN_KEYS: usize  (10_000: the most distinct build keys a plan pushes down into the probe's read as `key in (...)`)
pub enum JoinKind  (Inner, Left, Right, Full, Semi, Anti; Copy, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display the word, FromStr the word with an optional trailing `outer` or `join` - `outer` alone is Full)
  pub const ALL: [Self; 6]
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn keeps_unmatched_left(self) -> bool  (Left, Full, Anti)
  pub const fn keeps_unmatched_right(self) -> bool  (Right, Full)
  pub const fn emits_right(self) -> bool  (every kind but Semi and Anti, which answer the left columns alone)
  pub const fn is_filtering(self) -> bool  (Semi, Anti)
pub enum JoinSide  (Left, Right; Copy, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display, FromStr)
  pub const fn other(self) -> Self
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
pub struct JoinOptions  (what a join is run under: Clone, Debug, Eq, Default = new())
  pub const fn new() -> Self  (coalesce true, suffix DEFAULT_JOIN_SUFFIX, build None, prune true, spill None - the process default - pushdown_keys DEFAULT_PUSHDOWN_KEYS)
  pub const fn coalesce(&self) -> bool  (a key stated as one bare column on both sides - `using` - appears once, under the left name, the left value else the right)
  pub const fn with_coalesce(mut self, coalesce: bool) -> Self
  pub fn suffix(&self) -> &str
  pub fn with_suffix(mut self, suffix: impl Into<SmolStr>) -> Self
  pub const fn build(&self) -> Option<JoinSide>  (the side held and hashed; None picks the held side over a stream, else the smaller by memory_size, else Right)
  pub const fn with_build(mut self, build: Option<JoinSide>) -> Self
  pub const fn prune(&self) -> bool  (a probe batch whose every present key lies outside the build keys' range is answered without a hash - emitted one-sided or dropped by the kind - and a plan pushes the build's distinct keys into the probe's read; the rows and their order are the same either way)
  pub const fn with_prune(mut self, prune: bool) -> Self
  pub const fn spill(&self) -> Option<&SpillOptions>  (the bound the output and the held side settle under, None the process default; a build side past it is grace-partitioned on the row-format rung: both sides scattered by key hash into P = ceil(build_bytes / bound) partitions, at most 64, spooled to disk and joined partition by partition, the same rows in partition order; the values rung hashes the build whole)
  pub fn with_spill(mut self, spill: SpillOptions) -> Self
  pub const fn pushdown_keys(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn with_pushdown_keys(mut self, keys: usize) -> Self
