### yggdryl::root::window_serie  [rust/src/window_serie.rs]  (a window over a serie that reads and writes through the serie's own implementation, every index window-relative; re-exported at the root)
pub struct WindowSerie<'a> (live parent, offset and length; 24 bytes; no attached key metadata)
  pub const fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub const fn offset(&self) -> usize  (the serie row the window starts at)
  pub const fn serie(&self) -> &'a Serie  (the whole)
  pub fn field(&self) -> Option<&'a Field>
  pub fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>  (a column's, read off the field; a run's agreed out of the window's rows)
  pub fn null_count(&self) -> usize  (none where the column holds no absent row, else one validity read per window row, no row built; a run walks its values)
  pub fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> Result<bool>  (window-relative; refuses past the window naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Cow<'a, Scalar>>
  pub fn iter(&self) -> WindowSerieRows<'a>
  pub fn rows(&self) -> Cow<'a, [Scalar]>  (lent for a run, built once for a column)
  pub fn memory_size(&self) -> usize
  pub fn window(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<Self>  (a narrower window, rebased onto the serie)
  pub fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (through the serie it views over the window's rows: a window is never spilled on its own - spill the serie; a run its memory_size)
  pub fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool  (the serie's own answer over the rows it views; a run never)
  pub fn into_serie(&self) -> Serie  (Serie::slice: zero copy, a column's buffers or a run's values shared; rows only, the record stays with the window)
  pub fn window_by(&self, by: impl IntoSelector, sorted: bool) -> Result<KeySeries<'a>>  (Serie::window_by over this window's rows alone, each window over the same serie at its offset in it, or over the copy of this window's rows a sorted gather took; a window that states a record keeps its cells and numbers rownum from its own; refuses a key cell folding onto a kept cell, windownum or rownum, naming both)
  pub fn is_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> bool  (what the sliced serie answers, and so for the rest of the ordering verbs)
  pub fn is_unique(&self) -> bool
  pub fn unique_count(&self) -> usize
  pub fn sort_indices(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn sort_indices_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Serie>  (Serie::sort_indices_by over the window's rows, window-relative positions)
  pub fn into_sort_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Serie>  (Serie::into_sort_by over the window, a serie of its own)
  pub fn into_unique(&self) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_reversed(&self) -> Serie
  pub fn into_taken(&self, indices: &Serie) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_filtered(&self, mask: &Serie) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn partition_by(&self, keys: &Serie) -> Result<Vec<(Scalar, Serie)>>
pub struct WindowSerieMut<'a> (mutable live parent, offset and length; no attached key metadata)
  pub fn as_window(&self) -> WindowSerie<'_>  (the borrowed read-only view)
  pub const fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub const fn offset(&self) -> usize
  pub fn serie(&self) -> &Serie
  pub fn field(&self) -> Option<&Field>
  pub fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>
  pub fn null_count(&self) -> usize
  pub fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> Result<bool>
  pub fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Cow<'_, Scalar>>
  pub fn iter(&self) -> WindowSerieRows<'_>
  pub fn rows(&self) -> Cow<'_, [Scalar]>
  pub fn memory_size(&self) -> usize
  pub fn window(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<WindowSerie<'_>>
  pub fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (as_window's)
  pub fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool
  pub fn window_mut(&mut self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<WindowSerieMut<'_>>  (a narrower mutable window over the same serie, the offsets summed; this one borrowed while it lives)
  pub fn window_by(&self, by: impl IntoSelector, sorted: bool) -> Result<KeySeries<'_>>  (WindowSerie::window_by over as_window; a mutable window states no record)
  pub fn into_serie(&self) -> Serie
  pub fn is_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> bool
  pub fn is_unique(&self) -> bool
  pub fn unique_count(&self) -> usize
  pub fn sort_indices(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn sort_indices_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_sort_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_unique(&self) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_reversed(&self) -> Serie
  pub fn into_taken(&self, indices: &Serie) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn into_filtered(&self, mask: &Serie) -> Result<Serie>
  pub fn partition_by(&self, keys: &Serie) -> Result<Vec<(Scalar, Serie)>>
  pub fn set(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (Serie::set on the rebased row; refuses past the window)
  pub fn fill(&mut self, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (the value proved once, written as clones over the window)
  pub fn swap(&mut self, left: usize, right: usize) -> Result<()>  (two reads, two sets)
  pub fn copy_from(&mut self, other: &WindowSerie<'_>) -> Result<()>  (the other window's rows written row for row through Serie::splice; refuses another length by name)
  pub fn splice(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>  (Serie::splice on the rebased range, only when rows.len() == range.len(): a window never grows or shrinks what it views)
  pub fn as_sorted(&mut self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<&mut Self>  (in place, chaining: a primitive column held alone sorts the window of its native slice where it stands, a boolean column its two bitmaps, a run its values, any other leaf writes the sorted rows back through splice)
  pub fn as_reversed(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn as_taken(&mut self, indices: &Serie) -> Result<&mut Self>  (exactly as many indices as the window, window-relative; refused by name otherwise)
  pub fn as_sort_by(&mut self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<&mut Self>  (the window's rows sorted by the keys and written back through Serie::splice on the rebased range; a refusal leaves the serie as it was)
pub struct WindowSerieRows<'a>  (Iterator<Item = Cow<'a, Scalar>> over a window's rows, DoubleEndedIterator, ExactSizeIterator, FusedIterator)
pub struct KeySeries<'a>  (what window_by answers on a Serie, a WindowSerie and a WindowSerieMut: the owner of the windows - the serie they view, borrowed, or the one copy a sorted gather took where the keys descend; the key column computed once; one bit per row where a window opens, or the gathered windows' table; the record every window states, typed once; in row order, where each window opens, indexed on the first get; #[must_use]; Clone, Debug; IntoIterator for &KeySeries)
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (how many windows)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool  (no row was windowed)
  pub fn serie(&self) -> &Serie  (the serie every window views: the windowed one, or the gathered copy)
  pub fn into_owned(self) -> KeySeries<'static>  (a borrowed serie cloned, its buffers shared and no row copied, so the windows and their records outlive the borrow)
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<(Scalar, WindowSerie<'_>)>  (the window index places from the first, as iter lends it, in constant time: in row order the first call indexes where every window opens - one allocation of a usize per window, kept - and every call after it reads that index; in key order the cuts are the index; None past the last)
  pub fn iter(&self) -> KeySeries iterator<'_>  (the windows from the first, as often as asked)
pub struct KeySeries iterator<'s>  (Iterator<Item = (Scalar, WindowSerie<'s>)>, each the window's key - the key column's row at its first row - and the window stating its record; ExactSizeIterator, FusedIterator, Clone, Debug; nth skips windows with no key built - one walk of the set bits in row order, one read of the cuts in key order)

### yggdryl::bytes  [rust/src/bytes.rs]  (the byte family: six leaves, each a DataType, Field and Scalar variant of its own, the number on fixed_binary and sized_binary carried inline; BytesType the view over them)
pub use scalars::{Bytes, INLINE_BYTES};  (re-exported at yggdryl::types through `pub use bytes::*`; Bytes and BytesType also at the crate root)
pub const BYTES_EXTENSION_NAME: &str = "yggdryl.bytes";
pub enum BytesType  (#[non_exhaustive]; Copy; Default = Binary; Binary, LargeBinary, BinaryView, LargeBinaryView, FixedBinary(u32), SizedBinary(u32); Display = the canonical name plus "(n)" on a numbered leaf - binary, large_binary_view, fixed_binary(16), sized_binary(16); serde as the name alone)
  pub const ALL: [Self; 6] = [ Self::Binary, Self::LargeBinary, Self::BinaryView, Self::LargeBinaryView, Self::FixedBinary(1), Self::SizedBinary(1), ];
  pub const fn id(self) -> DataTypeId
  pub const fn from_id(id: DataTypeId, width: u32) -> Option<Self>  (the width lands on FixedBinary and SizedBinary)
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str  // binary, large_binary, binary_view, large_binary_view, fixed_binary, sized_binary
  pub const fn bound(self) -> Option<u32>
  pub const fn fixed(self) -> Option<u32>
  pub const fn max(self) -> Option<u32>
  pub const fn is_fixed(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_bounded(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_view(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_large(self) -> bool
  pub const fn storage(self) -> Self  (the leaf whose storage the column fills: SizedBinary answers Binary; every other leaf itself)
  pub const fn same_shape_as(self, other: Self) -> bool  (the same storage shape, the number ignored)
  pub fn with_bound(self, bound: u32) -> Result<Self>  (FixedBinary keeps its shape; Binary and SizedBinary become SizedBinary(n); the large and view leaves refuse)
  pub fn with_declared_bound(self, bound: Option<u32>) -> Result<Self>  (None on a numbered leaf refused)
  pub const fn validate(self) -> Result<()>  (a number of 0 refused)
  pub fn extension_json(self) -> String  // {"layout":"sized_binary","max":16}

### yggdryl::decimal::fixed  [rust/src/decimal.rs]
pub struct Decimal  (one exact decimal at eighteen fractional digits and thirty-eight digits of precision - `decimal128(38, 18)` preapplied - as one i128 of units, and the value of the registered `decimal` datatype; Copy, Default (zero), Eq, Ord, Hash, Display (no trailing zero behind the point), Debug, FromStr; Add/Sub/Mul/Div and their assign forms refuse an overflow as the integers' do, Neg, Sum; From<i64>; From<Decimal> for Decimal128, for BigDecimal and for Scalar (`Scalar::Decimal`); Value and DecimalValue at its one scale; re-exported at yggdryl::Decimal)
  pub const SCALE: i8 = 18
  pub const PRECISION: u8 = 38
  pub const ZERO: Self; pub const ONE: Self; pub const MAX: Self; pub const MIN: Self  (thirty-eight nines, eighteen fractional, and its negation)
  pub const fn from_units(units: i128) -> Option<Self>  (the `decimal128(38, 18)` coefficient; None past the precision)
  pub const fn units(self) -> i128
  pub const fn from_int(value: i64) -> Self
  pub fn from_f64(value: f64) -> Option<Self>  (rounded to eighteen digits; None for a non-finite float or one past the precision)
  pub fn to_f64(self) -> f64
  pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Result<Self>  (exact, one pass, no allocation - the one grammar every decimal spelling in the crate reads, a `decimal` column's cell included: surrounding whitespace ignored, an optional sign, a leading (`.5`) or trailing (`5.`) point or none, zeros leading and trailing, `_` grouping between the digits ahead of the point, an `e` exponent moving the point; refuses with Error::Parse text stating no number - the empty text, a bare sign, two points, comma grouping (`1,250`), a letter, `NaN`, `inf` - a non-zero digit past the eighteenth fractional one (`truncated` is the explicit cut) and a value past thirty-eight digits)
  pub fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Option<Self>  (an exact decimal restated at eighteen digits, a whole integer, a finite float, or text `parse` reads)
  pub const fn dtype() -> DataType  (DataType::Decimal, the leaf over `decimal128(38, 18)` storage)
  pub const fn is_zero(self) -> bool; pub const fn is_negative(self) -> bool; pub const fn is_positive(self) -> bool; pub const fn abs(self) -> Self
  pub fn checked_add(self, other: Self) -> Option<Self>  // and checked_sub, checked_mul, checked_div: None past the precision, checked_div also for a divisor of nothing; mul and div keep eighteen digits and truncate the rest toward zero, widening to 256 bits for the one product
  pub fn truncated(self, places: u8) -> Self  (the value at `places` fractional digits, the rest truncated toward zero)
  pub const fn into_decimal128(self) -> Decimal128
  pub const fn widened(self) -> BigDecimal  (lossless: every Decimal is a BigDecimal)
pub struct BigDecimal(i256)  (one exact decimal at eighteen fractional digits and seventy-six digits of precision - `decimal256(76, 18)` preapplied - and the value of the registered `bigdecimal` datatype; Copy, Default (zero), Eq, Ord, Hash, Display (no trailing zero behind the point), Debug, FromStr; Add/Sub/Mul/Div and their assign forms refuse an overflow, Neg, Sum; From<i64>, From<Decimal>; From<BigDecimal> for Decimal256 and for Scalar (`Scalar::BigDecimal`); Value and DecimalValue at its one scale; re-exported at yggdryl::BigDecimal)

### yggdryl::uuid  [rust/src/uuid.rs]
pub struct Uuid  (transparent u128, 16 bytes; big-endian storage; Copy, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display, serde; Value)
  pub const fn new(value: u128) -> Self
  pub fn from_v7(unix_micros: i64, payload: u64) -> Result<Self>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7 over a microsecond instant, an exact packing rather than a fingerprint of one: 48-bit millisecond count, the microsecond within it in rand_a's high ten, then the whole 64-bit payload split two bits above the variant and sixty-two below; nothing is hashed or narrowed, so both facts read back out and two identifiers agree only where the microsecond and all 64 payload bits do; microsecond range 0..=281474976710655999; no allocation on success)

  pub fn from_bytes(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn get(self) -> u128
  pub const fn is_nil(self) -> bool  (RFC 9562's all-zero identifier, `Default`: the identity no element has, and what an instant no UUIDv7 holds derives)
  pub const fn into_bytes(self) -> [u8; 16]
pub struct Uuid  (transparent u128, 16 bytes; big-endian storage; Copy, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display, serde; Value)
  pub const TEXT_LEN: usize = 36;
  pub const fn new(value: u128) -> Self
  pub fn from_v7(unix_micros: i64, payload: u64) -> Result<Self>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7 over a microsecond instant, an exact packing rather than a fingerprint of one: 48-bit millisecond count, the microsecond within it in rand_a's high ten, then the whole 64-bit payload split two bits above the variant and sixty-two below; nothing is hashed or narrowed, so both facts read back out and two identifiers agree only where the microsecond and all 64 payload bits do; microsecond range 0..=281474976710655999; no allocation on success)
  pub fn from_v7_sequence(unix_millis: i64, sequence: u64, payload: u64) -> Result<Self>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7 with the 48-bit Unix millisecond timestamp, `sequence.min(4095)` in the ordered 12-bit `rand_a` lane, and the low 62 payload bits in `rand_b`; callers exceeding the lane include the whole sequence in the payload to avoid aliases probabilistically; refuses a timestamp outside 0..=281474976710655; no allocation on success)
  pub const fn from_v8(payload: u128) -> Self  (replace version/variant bits only; preserve 122 payload bits; no allocation)
  pub fn from_bytes(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn get(self) -> u128
  pub const fn is_nil(self) -> bool  (RFC 9562's all-zero identifier, `Default`: the identity no element has, and what an instant no UUIDv7 holds derives)
  pub const fn into_bytes(self) -> [u8; 16]
  pub fn render(self, slot: &mut [u8; Self::TEXT_LEN]) -> &str  (the canonical spelling into the caller's slot; allocates nothing)
  impl FromStr for Uuid  (32 hexadecimal digits or the 36-character hyphenated shape, in either case, the surrounding blanks not part of it; text only - sixteen characters are never the sixteen stored bytes, which `from_bytes` takes; Err = Error::Parse naming both spellings and what was found)

### yggdryl::scalar  [rust/src/scalar.rs]  (the Value contract and the leaf contracts a scalar's leaves answer are under yggdryl::value; a family is no value type, only the DataTypeId range its DataTypeKind owns)
  pub enum Scalar  (#[non_exhaustive]; Null, Boolean, Int8, Int16, Int32, Int64, UInt8, UInt16, UInt32, UInt64, Int128, UInt128, Float16, Float32, Float64, Decimal32, Decimal64, Decimal128, Decimal256, Decimal, BigDecimal (the fixed leaves' units at scale eighteen, wire tags `decimal` and `bigdecimal`, equal to and hashing as their Decimal128/Decimal256 twins), Date32, Date64, Time32, Time64, DateTime64, Duration32, Duration64, Interval, Utf8String .. SizedCp1252String (the eighteen string leaves, each its Str and, fixed or sized, its u32), Country, Ccy, Mic, Cfi, Side, State, TimeInForce, Isin, Cusip, Sedol, Bbg, Ric, Figi, Unit, Forex, Lei, Bic, Elf, Dti, Fisn, Uuid, Version, Url, Urn, Timezone, MimeType, MediaType, Binary .. SizedBinary (the six byte leaves, each its Bytes and, fixed or sized, its u32), Geometry, Geography, Serie, SerieView, FixedSizeSerie, LargeSerie, LargeSerieView (each a Serie: a schema-free run or a column, the variant the layout the value is declared under; kind() and the serde tag the layout's name, the old list tags still read), Map, SortedMap (each a Map), Struct, Variant (one Parquet Variant value: its metadata dictionary and its value payload); a held Arrow column is the serie it is, Scalar::from(serie), and a stream is never a Scalar; 48 bytes; every variant holds its leaf directly, the seventeen registered codes included; Eq/Ord/Hash across widths: integers by sign and magnitude, floats by the f64 reading, decimals normalized, temporals of one family by normalized count then zone)
  pub const fn id(&self) -> DataTypeId
  pub const fn family(&self) -> DataTypeKind  (the family whose DataTypeKind::range id() is in; which temporal family is id().temporal_family())
  pub const fn kind(&self) -> &'static str
  pub fn from_sequence(values: impl IntoIterator<Item = Self>) -> Self
  pub fn from_mapping(entries: impl IntoIterator<Item = (Self, Self)>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn from_struct<K, I>(entries: I) -> Result<Self> where
  pub const fn as_bool(&self) -> Option<bool>
  pub fn as_str(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub const fn code_storage(&self) -> Option<&SmolStr>  (the seventeen registered codes written out once; `id` is the other half - the identity leads, the text follows)
  pub const fn is_code(&self) -> bool  (DataTypeKind::Code.contains(self.id()))
  pub fn as_bytes(&self) -> Option<&[u8]>
  pub fn into_json_bytes(&self) -> Result<Vec<u8>>
  pub fn into_json(&self) -> Result<String>
  pub fn as_wkb(&self) -> Option<&[u8]>
  pub fn as_sequence(&self) -> Option<&[Self]>  (the run's slice; None for a column)
  pub fn as_serie(&self) -> Option<&Serie>  (any sequence, borrowed: a run or a column)
  pub fn sequence_rows(&self) -> Option<Cow<'_, [Self]>>  (the door every reader of meaning uses: a run's slice borrowed, a column's rows built)
  pub fn as_mapping(&self) -> Option<&[(Self, Self)]>
  pub fn as_struct(&self) -> Option<&BTreeMap<SmolStr, Self>>
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Cow<'_, Self>>  (borrowed for a run, built for a column)
  pub fn get_key(&self, key: &Self) -> Option<&Self>
  pub fn get_key_str(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&Self>
  pub fn iter(&self) -> Children<'_>  (item Cow<'_, Self>: a column's rows built one at a time)
  pub fn mapping_iter(&self) -> std::slice::Iter<'_, (Self, Self)>
  pub fn record_iter(&self) -> std::collections::btree_map::Iter<'_, SmolStr, Self>
  pub const fn is_null(&self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_container(&self) -> bool
  pub fn is_truthy(&self) -> bool  (coercion, not a reading: absence, zero at any width, empty text/bytes, and a container with nothing set are false; a string the one boolean table reads as false - false/no/off/0/f/n and the prefixes of false and off, trimmed, in any case - is false, wider than Python because values arrive as text, and any other text, `n/a` included, true; a registered code or an enum member is an identity, present unless empty)
  pub const fn is_number(&self) -> bool  (self.family().is_numeric())

### yggdryl::serie  [rust/src/serie.rs]  (Serie, the fourth side of the value model: many values, as a schema-free run or as the Arrow buffers of one Field; the flat root, the identity helpers and the serde shadow live here, the leaves in serie/)
  pub enum Serie  (#[non_exhaustive]; Run(Run), then one variant per storage layout, named as the leaf that holds it without Serie - a variant names the layout, never the datatype, which is the field's (SerieValue::id): Null, Boolean, Int8 .. Float64 (Arc<Int8Serie> ..), DateTimeSecond, DateTimeMillisecond, DateTimeMicrosecond, DateTimeNanosecond (Arc<DateTimeSecondSerie> ..), Date32, Date64, Time32Second, Time32Millisecond, Time64Microsecond, Time64Nanosecond (Arc<Time32SecondSerie> ..), DurationSecond, DurationMillisecond, DurationMicrosecond, DurationNanosecond (Arc<DurationSecondSerie> ..; each holds duration32 and duration64 alike, the width the field's), IntervalYearMonth, IntervalDayTime, IntervalMonthDayNano (Arc<IntervalYearMonthSerie> ..), Binary (a sized binary too), LargeBinary, BinaryView (a large view too), FixedBytes (Arc<BinarySerie> .. Arc<FixedBytesSerie>), Utf8String (every string leaf laid out as UTF-8, ascii and sized_utf8 among them), LargeUtf8String, Utf8ViewString, BinaryString, LargeBinaryString, BinaryViewString, FixedString (Arc<Utf8StringSerie> .. Arc<FixedStringSerie>), the fourteen codes and Version, Url, Urn, Timezone, MimeType, MediaType (Arc<Utf8StringSerie>), Uuid(Arc<FixedBytesSerie>), Serie, SerieView, FixedSizeSerie, LargeSerie, LargeSerieView (Arc<SerieSerie> .. Arc<LargeSerieViewSerie>; the one layout a datatype names, so the variant shares the datatype's name), Struct, Union, Dictionary(Arc<DictionarySerie>), Decimal32 .. Decimal256, Map, SortedMap (Arc<MapSerie>), RunEndEncoded, Variant, Geometry, Geography (Arc<BinarySerie>); 40 bytes, pinned by a const assert - a run's window inline, every column leaf one thin pointer; Clone is a pointer bump per column; Eq/Ord/Hash over the rows alone, never the leaf or the field, so a run and a column of equal rows are one value and hash alike; Display a run as its slice, a column as name[row, ...]; Debug a column as its leaf's Name { field, len, nulls }; Default = an empty run)
  pub fn new(values: impl Into<Arc<[Scalar]>>) -> Self  (a run)
  pub fn empty(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>) -> Result<Self>  (the empty column of field; a union of no member, wherever it lies, refused)
  pub fn with_capacity(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>, rows: usize) -> Result<Self>  (the empty column whose buffers reserve rows; an encoding reserves nothing)
  pub fn from_scalars(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = Scalar>) -> Result<Self>  (every row through Field::scalar, laid out once, landed proven with no second read)
  pub fn lit(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>, value: Scalar, rows: usize) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (rows copies of value as one constant column, LitSerie: the value proven by Field::scalar once - a text field reads an integer as its text, a sequence refused - and laid out as one row, the whole array built only when something exports it)
  pub fn from_default(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>, rows: usize) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (Field::default_value as the constant column lit builds; a required null field has no default)
  pub fn from_arrow_array(field: Option<&Field>, array: ArrayRef, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (None: the array's own field, named item, nullable where it holds a logical null, no buffer copied, each non-contract row read once; Some: one ArrowCastPlan compiled and applied, an exact layout the identity plan sharing the buffers)
  pub fn from_arrow_batch(root: Option<&Field>, batch: &RecordBatch, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (None: a record column named DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME whose children share the batch's columns; Some: cast into a non-null record root)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(root: Option<&Field>, reader: BatchReader, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (ChunkedSerie::from_arrow_reader joined by ChunkedSerie::into_serie: the whole stream is held, the bound is the stream)
  pub fn cast(&self, target: &Field, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (its own field is itself; an equal layout under another name or nullability is the same buffers relabelled; otherwise one plan compiled and applied; a run is refused)
  pub fn into_arrow_scalar(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<arrow_array::Scalar<ArrayRef>>  (exactly one row, its buffers shared)
  pub fn into_arrow_batch(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<RecordBatch>  (a record column's children are the columns, an absent record row refused; any other column is the one column of a row root)
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<BatchReader>  (one batch)
  pub struct StreamChunkedSerie  (one record Serie per batch of an Arrow stream under one ArrowCastPlan compiled from the stream's schema before a batch is pulled; at most one source batch held; fused after the first failure; Iterator<Item = crate::arrow::Result<Serie>>, FusedIterator, Debug)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(root: Option<&Field>, reader: BatchReader, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (None: the stream's own schema as a DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME record)
  pub fn from_serie(serie: Serie) -> crate::arrow::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (one held column as one record column, a non-record column the one child of a DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME record; nothing cast or read and no plan compiled; a run and a record with an absent row refused)
  pub fn from_chunked(chunked: ChunkedSerie) -> crate::arrow::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (a held chunked column as the stream of its chunks, one record column per chunk - a non-record field's chunks each the one child of a DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME record, from_serie's rule per chunk; nothing cast, copied or read and no plan compiled; no chunk is the empty stream of its root; a record chunk with an absent row refused)
  pub fn cast(self, target: &Field, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (every record cast into target - a record root, or a column as the one child of one - by one plan: the reader itself under its own root; held records cast once each; a stream's batches cast by the plan they were opened under and landed under target after it, or by one plan from the stream's own schema over an identity)
  pub fn root_of(field: &Field) -> crate::Result<Field>  (the record root a held column of field crosses into a table under: a record's own field, required, or the DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME record it is the one child of; what from_serie and from_chunked name their field by and what into_arrow_batch, into_arrow_reader and ChunkedSerie::into_arrow_reader project their schema from)
  pub fn field(&self) -> &Field
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(self) -> BatchReader  (transport: batches reconciled to the root and never landed; the inner reader itself over an identity plan; a window's static values dropped)
  pub fn window_by(self, by: impl IntoSelector, sorted: bool) -> crate::arrow::Result<StreamKeySerie>  (one lazy StreamChunkedSerie per window of equal adjacent keys, in the order they arrive, by bound once against the root before any batch is pulled; windows read in order - taking the next drops the open one's unread rows, and a window its walk passed refuses once, naming it; sorted verifies the keys arrive ascending, absent last, and refuses the first window keyed backwards naming the batch and row; the walk holds at most one batch, its key record and one bit per row; refuses before any pull what Serie::window_by refuses, naming the root)
  pub fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (the held records still to yield, or the batch a window's walk stands in; a stream holds no landed batch between pulls and answers zero)
  pub fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool  (held records only, every one of them; never a stream)
  pub fn spill(&mut self, options: &crate::SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<()>  (each held record through Serie::spill; a stream holds none and is untouched)
  pub fn as_spilled(&mut self, options: &crate::SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<&mut Self>  (spill, answering this reader so calls chain)
  pub fn into_spilled(mut self, options: &crate::SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<Self>  (spill on the held records, the reader handed back: a stream owns one source, so it moves rather than copies)
  pub fn join_with(self, other: impl Into<Serie>, by: impl IntoJoinKeys, how: JoinKind, options: &JoinOptions) -> crate::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (Serie::join_with over a stream: the held side - or, of two streams, the right one, drained first - is built before a batch is pulled, then each probe batch answers its own output batches as it is pulled, nothing collected; a build side past the spill bound reads the probe whole into spooled partitions)
  pub fn into_sorted(self, options: SortOptions) -> crate::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (the stream drained into settled chunks, ChunkedSerie::into_sorted's merge, and the merged chunks streamed back; a window's static values kept)
  pub fn into_sort_by(self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> crate::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>  (the same over the `order by` keys of by, refused before any pull where they do not bind)
  pub struct StreamKeySerie  (the windows of a stream: Iterator<Item = crate::arrow::Result<StreamChunkedSerie>>, FusedIterator, Debug; Send + Sync, every window Send; a failure is the item of whichever reader pulled it, once, then the walk and every window end)
  pub fn field(&self) -> &Field  (the root every window yields: the windowed reader's own)
  pub fn field(&self) -> Option<&Field>  (None for a run)
  pub fn field_ref(&self) -> Option<&Arc<Field>>
  pub fn require_field(&self) -> Result<&Field>  (refuses a run by name)
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (constant)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn null_count(&self) -> usize  (constant for a column, a walk for a run)
  pub fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> Result<bool>  (refuses past the end naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>  (one row built; a stored row never refuses, so the one refusal is an index past the end)
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Cow<'_, Scalar>>  (borrowed for a run, built for a column)
  pub fn rows(&self) -> Cow<'_, [Scalar]>  (borrows a run, builds a column's rows)
  pub fn iter(&self) -> Children<'_>  (Children::Sequence for a run, Children::Column for a column; item Cow<'_, Scalar>)
  pub fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>  (a column: a serie of its field named item; a run: agreed out of its rows)
  pub fn as_run(&self) -> Option<&Run>
  pub fn as_slice(&self) -> Option<&[Scalar]>  (the run's values; None for a column)
  pub const fn is_column(&self) -> bool
  pub fn into_run(self) -> Run  (a run answers itself; a column builds its rows once)
  pub fn into_arrow_array(&self) -> Option<ArrayRef>  (None for a run; shares buffers)
  pub fn sort_indices(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Self>  (the row positions in sorted order as a uint32 column named index, stable, in the values' order - Scalar's total order, a nested absence placed as a top-level one: a primitive column over its native slice, every NaN one value; a column whose stored bytes order as its values through Arrow's row format; a record column whose buffers do not child by child, each child on its own rung, so only a value-ordered child builds its rows; a run and any other column - a version, windows-1252 text, a code, a URL, a union, a foreign NaN payload - through the values' own order, each row built once; absent rows gathered to the end the options name; refuses more rows than one uint32 index column addresses)
  pub fn is_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> bool  (one pass over adjacent rows on sort_indices' rung: Arrow's comparator over buffers that order as their values, else the values' order, each row built once)
  pub fn is_unique(&self) -> bool  (one hash set over the row format's bytes or the values, an absent row one value; stops at the first repeat)
  pub fn unique_count(&self) -> usize
  pub fn into_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Self>  (sort_indices then the take; a new serie under the same field, this one untouched)
  pub fn sort_indices_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Self>  (the row positions in the order the `order by` keys of by state, most significant first: the keys bound once as a selector against StreamChunkedSerie::root_of and evaluated once into a key record; one key over no absent row sorts on its own column's rung, several through Arrow's row format where every key's buffers order as its values, else key by key over the values' order; stable; a uint32 column named index; refuses, before any row is read, text that is not a list of keys, no key, a run, an unnest, a term reaching no column or two, two keys publishing one name, and more rows than one uint32 index column addresses)
  pub fn into_sort_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Self>  (sort_indices_by then one take of every column; a new serie under the same field, this one untouched)
  pub fn into_unique(&self) -> Result<Self>  (the first occurrence of every value in first-occurrence order, the field kept)
  pub fn into_reversed(&self) -> Self  (one take for a column, a copy for a run)
  pub fn into_taken(&self, indices: &Self) -> Result<Self>  (the rows an integer column or run of any width names, in that order; refuses an absent, non-integer, negative or out-of-range index naming the serie and the index)
  pub fn into_filtered(&self, mask: &Self) -> Result<Self>  (the rows a boolean column or run of the same length keeps, an absent mask row keeping nothing; refuses another length or a non-boolean row by name)
  pub fn partition_by(&self, keys: &Self) -> Result<Vec<(Scalar, Self)>>  (one (key, rows) per distinct key of a serie of the same length, in first-occurrence order, an absent key one value; sorted keys cut every group as a zero-copy slice, other keys take each group once)
  pub fn memory_size(&self) -> usize  (a column's buffers as its own slice counts them, a run's values as the row estimator charges them)
  pub fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (the bytes memory_size counts that still sit on the heap: a mapped leaf's buffers count nothing, a nested leaf its own unmapped buffers beside its children's answer, a run its values; read off the buffers, allocating nothing)
  pub fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool  (memory_size() > 0 && resident_size() == 0; an empty column and a run never)
  pub fn as_spilled(&mut self, options: &SpillOptions) -> Result<&mut Self>  (spill, answering this serie so calls chain)
  pub fn into_spilled(&self, options: &SpillOptions) -> Result<Self>  (a copy spilled, this one untouched: the buffers the bound leaves resident shared, the rest written once and mapped)
  pub fn spill(&mut self, options: &SpillOptions) -> Result<()>  (writes buffers to one private unlinked file yggdryl-spill-<pid>-<seq> under options.folder(), 64-byte aligned, and lands the same column over the mapping, in place, until resident_size() is under options.byte_size(): a run is left alone, a column under the bound untouched, a flat column or one whose own buffers alone pass the bound spilled whole, any other child by child heaviest first, each under what the bound leaves once everything else stays resident; a write through a leaf brings that leaf back to the heap, a clone or a slice shares the mapping - but a sliced offsets leaf rebuilds its offsets on the heap and so is not spilled; refuses a folder that cannot hold the file by name)
  pub fn as_sorted(&mut self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<&mut Self>  (in place, chaining: a primitive column holding its buffer alone sorts the native slice where it stands and rewrites its validity, a boolean column its two bitmaps, a run its values; any other leaf replaces its buffers by the kernel's one copy; a refusal leaves the serie as it was)
  pub fn as_sort_by(&mut self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<&mut Self>  (in place, chaining: into_sort_by replacing this serie; a refusal leaves it as it was)
  pub fn as_unique(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn as_reversed(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self>  (a primitive column held alone reverses the native slice and its validity bits where they stand, a boolean column its two bitmaps)
  pub fn as_taken(&mut self, indices: &Self) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn as_filtered(&mut self, mask: &Self) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn window(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<WindowSerie<'_>>  (a window reading through this serie; refuses past the end naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn window_mut(&mut self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<WindowSerieMut<'_>>
  pub fn window_by(&self, by: impl IntoSelector, sorted: bool) -> Result<KeySeries<'_>>  (the rows cut into windows of equal keys, by a selector bound once against StreamChunkedSerie::root_of: sorted false, one window per run of equal adjacent keys in row order over this serie; sorted true, each key once in key order, ascending with absent keys last - keys already in order the same windows at the same cost, any others' runs sorted stably and the rows gathered once into a serie the answer owns; the key column computed once, one comparator and one bit per row, each window its key; refuses a run, a key with no projection, an unnest, the binder's refusals and a key cell folding onto windownum or rownum before any row is read, and with sorted a serie past u32::MAX rows once a descent is read)
  pub fn join_with(&self, other: &Self, by: impl IntoJoinKeys, how: JoinKind, options: &JoinOptions) -> Result<Self>  (this record joined with other on by under how, one record column: every key bound once against each side and cast to the datatype the two share, the output root the left columns then the right ones - a `using` key once where coalesced, a colliding right name suffixed - named after the left, metadata cleared; the build side hashed by Arrow's row format where every key's buffers order as its values, else by the values - or, where both roots declare the key order ascending with no key cast, walked by one cursor and never hashed - the probe read batch by batch, the output batches joined once and settled under options.spill(); refuses before any row a run on either side, no key, a key reaching no column, an unnest or an aggregate in a key, and a key pair sharing no datatype, naming both)
  pub fn declared_order(&self) -> Result<Option<Vec<expression::Ordering>>>  (the `order by` keys this record's root declares its rows keep - `SORT:by`, read through Field::as_sort - None for a run, a column that is not a record, and a root declaring none; a proven fact: into_sorted writes every column under its options and into_sort_by its keys, a slice, a filter, into_unique and a take in increasing position keep it, into_reversed flips every key, a write compares the rows it touched against their neighbours and clears it where the order no longer holds - or where a key is computed - and is_sorted, sort_indices, into_sorted and their _by forms answer without a pass where it states what they ask, a prefix included; refuses a root text that is not a key list, which no door of the crate writes)
  pub fn require_arrow_array(&self) -> Result<ArrayRef>  (refuses a run by name)
  pub fn slice(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<Self>  (zero copy: a column's offsets rebased, a run a window over the same shared values, a slice of a slice reaching them with the offsets summed; the whole serie is itself; refuses past the end)
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Self>  (a record column's child by exact name)
  pub fn child_at(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&Self>
  pub fn children(&self) -> &[Self]  (a record or a union column's children; empty elsewhere)
  pub fn items(&self) -> Option<&Self>  (a sequence column's items, a mapping's entries, an encoding's values)
  pub fn get_child_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&Self>  (exactly DataType::get_field_by_path's segment semantics; an index, key, range or predicate segment reaches no column)
  pub fn splice(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>  (the one mutation: a column proves every row once, checks what a write could not do, then writes without failing; refuses a reversed range or one past the end naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn set(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (a primitive or boolean column overwrites one slot in place)
  pub fn push(&mut self, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (amortized in place for a column; a run copies every value it holds)
  pub fn insert(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn pop(&mut self) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>
  pub fn truncate(&mut self, len: usize) -> Result<()>
  pub fn clear(&mut self) -> Result<()>  (keeps the field)
  pub fn extend(&mut self, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn extend_from_serie(&mut self, other: &Self) -> Result<()>  (two columns whose fields have one datatype and whose nullability fits append buffer to buffer, no row read; anything else reads other's rows - one layout under two datatypes, duration32 beside duration64 of one unit, is not one datatype)
  pub fn resize(&mut self, len: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (proves value once and writes clones)
  pub fn set_child(&mut self, child: Self) -> Result<()>  (a record column only: replaces the child of child's name or adds one, extending the field; refuses a run, a length other than len and a non-record column, each by name)
  pub fn set_cell(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (a record column only: descends path through record children by name and writes one slot in place; refuses an absent row on the way)
  pub fn as_lit(&self) -> Option<&LitSerie>  (the constant column this is, None for any other leaf)
  pub fn get_lit_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut LitSerie>  (through Arc::make_mut)
  pub fn as_null(&self) -> Option<&NullSerie>  // and as_boolean, as_struct, as_map (Map and SortedMap), as_union, as_run_end_encoded, as_variant, as_int8 .. as_uint64, as_float16/32/64, as_decimal32/64/128/256, as_date32, as_date64, as_time32_second, as_time32_millisecond, as_time64_microsecond, as_time64_nanosecond, as_datetime_second/millisecond/microsecond/nanosecond, as_duration_second/millisecond/microsecond/nanosecond, as_interval_year_month/day_time/month_day_nano, as_utf8, as_large_utf8, as_utf8_view, as_binary_string, as_large_binary_string, as_binary_view_string, as_fixed_string, as_binary, as_large_binary, as_binary_view, as_fixed_bytes, as_serie, as_large_serie, as_serie_view, as_large_serie_view, as_fixed_size_serie, as_dictionary: one per leaf, a direct match on its variant - as_utf8 also reaches the code, Version, Url, Urn, Timezone, MimeType and MediaType variants, as_fixed_bytes Uuid, as_binary Geometry and Geography; as_serie narrows a column to its SerieSerie leaf, where Scalar::as_serie borrows the whole Serie a value holds; allocation free
  pub fn get_null_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut NullSerie>  // and get_<leaf>_mut for every as_<leaf> above: through Arc::make_mut, the leaf struct copied once when the column is shared; only the typed writers of a leaf can write through it, and they validate nullability
  impl Value for Serie

### yggdryl::chunked_serie  [rust/src/chunked_serie.rs]  (ChunkedSerie: many Serie columns under one Field, held apart - pyarrow's ChunkedArray, and under a non-null record field a Table of one batch per chunk; ChunkedSerie and ChunkedRows re-exported at the root)
  pub struct ChunkedSerie  (the chunks, every one a column whose field is exactly this one, proven at its own door, and the chunk ends kept beside them: a row's chunk is a binary search, the length a read; Clone is a pointer bump per chunk; Eq/Ord/Hash over the rows alone, and PartialEq and PartialOrd with Serie both ways, so it equals and orders as the column of its rows and any other chunking of them; Display name[row, ...] as the column of its rows renders; Debug ChunkedSerie { field, chunks, len, nulls })
  pub fn empty(field: impl Into<Arc<Field>>) -> crate::Result<Self>  (no chunk; refuses what Serie::empty refuses)
  pub fn from_serie(serie: Serie) -> crate::Result<Self>  (one chunk, shared; a run is refused)
  pub fn from_series(field: Option<&Field>, chunks: impl IntoIterator<Item = Serie>, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (None: one datatype in pieces - the first chunk's field, nullable where any chunk's is, a chunk of that datatype under another name or nullability, or naming its serie items or mapping entries otherwise, relabelled, a chunk of another datatype refused naming it; Some: a chunk under the field held as it stands, any other cast in, one plan per run of chunks under one source field; a run, and no chunk with no field, refused; no chunk with a field Self::empty)
  pub fn from_arrow_arrays(field: Option<&Field>, arrays: impl IntoIterator<Item = ArrayRef>, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (the chunked-array door: one array per chunk, one ArrowCastPlan per run of arrays of one layout, the identity sharing buffers; None: one datatype in pieces - the field named item of the first array's datatype, nullable where any array holds a logical null, an array of that datatype naming its items or entries otherwise relabelled, one of another datatype refused naming chunk {index}; Some: every array cast into the field; no array with no field refused, with a field Self::empty)
  pub fn from_chunked_stream(reader: StreamChunkedSerie) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (collected, one chunk per batch, none joined)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(root: Option<&Field>, reader: BatchReader, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (the table door: StreamChunkedSerie::from_arrow_reader collected, one chunk per batch, where Serie::from_arrow_reader joins them)
  pub fn field(&self) -> &Field
  pub fn field_ref(&self) -> &Arc<Field>
  pub fn dtype(&self) -> DataType  (serie(<the field named item>), what Serie::dtype answers for a column of the field; infallible)
  pub fn chunks(&self) -> &[Serie]
  pub fn chunk(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&Serie>  (None past the last)
  pub fn num_chunks(&self) -> usize
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (a read)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn null_count(&self) -> usize  (one read per chunk)
  pub fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> crate::Result<bool>  (refuses past the end naming the field and both counts)
  pub fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> crate::Result<Scalar>  (one row built out of the chunk holding it; the one refusal is an index past the end: row {index} is past the end of {len} rows)
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Scalar>  (None past the end)
  pub fn rows(&self) -> Vec<Scalar>  (every row built once, chunk after chunk)
  pub fn iter(&self) -> ChunkedRows<'_>
  pub fn slice(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> crate::Result<Self>  (zero copy: the chunks the window reaches, found by two binary searches, the two at its edges sliced and every other a pointer bump; a zero-length window holds no chunk; refuses past the end naming both counts)
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<Self>  (a record's child in every chunk - a table's column - a pointer bump per chunk; a chunked serie of no chunk answers from its field)
  pub fn child_at(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Self>  (a record's child, or a union's member, at index)
  pub fn children(&self) -> Vec<Self>  (every child of a record or member of a union; empty elsewhere)
  pub fn items(&self) -> Option<Self>  (a sequence's items, a mapping's entries, an encoding's values, in every chunk)
  pub fn get_child_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<Self>  (exactly Serie::get_child_by_path in every chunk)
  pub fn push_chunk(&mut self, chunk: Serie, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<()>  (a column under the field appended as it stands, any other cast in by one plan compiled per call; a run refused; a refusal leaves it unchanged)
  pub fn into_serie(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<Serie>  (the one join: no chunk the empty column of the field, one chunk itself, shared, several concatenated once and landed with no row read; a dictionary whose gathered vocabulary Arrow would join past its key refused naming its path)
  pub fn cast(&self, target: &Field, options: ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (already under target: a clone; otherwise one ArrowCastPlan compiled and applied to every chunk)
  pub fn into_arrow_arrays(&self) -> Vec<ArrayRef>  (each chunk's buffers, shared)
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<BatchReader>  (one batch per chunk under one schema built once: a record's chunks are its batches, any other field's chunks each the one column of a row root, as Serie::into_arrow_batch crosses one; a record chunk holding an absent row refused)
  pub fn join_with(&self, other: &Self, by: impl IntoJoinKeys, how: JoinKind, options: &JoinOptions) -> Result<Self>  (Serie::join_with with the output batches kept apart as chunks, each settled: one chunk per probe chunk answering rows, then the build side's unmatched rows where the kind keeps them)
  pub fn declared_order(&self) -> crate::Result<Option<Vec<expression::Ordering>>>  (the `order by` keys this chunked record's field declares its rows keep across every chunk, Serie::declared_order over the field; None for a non-record or a field declaring none; a sorted result declares its keys, a reversal flips them, a take out of position clears them, and every chunk edge is verified where chunks arrive under a declaring field)
  pub fn sort_indices(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Serie>  (the one join, then Serie::sort_indices)
  pub fn is_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> bool  (every chunk sorted and every chunk edge in order, an edge compared on the rung the chunks sort on - one comparator over both chunks' buffers, or one row per side - so it answers what the joined column does; no join)
  pub fn is_unique(&self) -> bool  (the one join, then Serie::is_unique)
  pub fn unique_count(&self) -> usize
  pub fn into_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Self>  (the one join then the sort: a chunked serie of one chunk under the field)
  pub fn into_sorted(&self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<Self>  (no join: each chunk sorted on its own by Serie::into_sorted, then merged - one cursor per chunk in a heap, the least key on top, a tie to the earlier chunk, each cursor reading its chunk a block at a time, DEFAULT_RECORD_BATCH_ROW_SIZE rows shared among the cursors - into chunks of at most DEFAULT_RECORD_BATCH_ROW_SIZE rows, each gathered by one interleave and settled; the rows and their order the joined column's Serie::into_sorted; the sort holds the sorted chunks - spilled past the bound - the cursors' blocks, one output batch and the output so far, the rows copied twice and never a third time; one chunk with rows is cut into zero-copy slices)
  pub fn into_sort_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Self>  (into_sorted's merge over the `order by` keys of by, bound once before any chunk is read, each chunk sorted by Serie::into_sort_by, the key cells of each cursor's block computed by the bound keys, each under its own options; refuses its keys with no chunk as with many)
  pub fn into_unique(&self) -> Result<Self>  (the first occurrence of every value across the chunks in first-occurrence order - the joined column's Serie::into_unique - with no join: each chunk's sorted order computed once, the chunks merged in that order as into_sorted merges them, the first row of every run of equal values marked in its chunk's mask, each chunk filtered by its own mask and kept apart, a chunk left empty dropped; one chunk with rows its own Serie::into_unique)
  pub fn into_reversed(&self) -> Self  (the chunks reversed, each reversed, kept apart)
  pub fn into_taken(&self, indices: &Serie) -> Result<Self>  (indices name rows across the whole: the one join then the take, one chunk)
  pub fn into_filtered(&self, mask: &Serie) -> Result<Self>  (a mask as long as the whole, cut to each chunk's window and applied per chunk, the chunks kept apart; refuses another length naming the field)
  pub fn partition_by(&self, keys: &Serie) -> Result<Vec<(Scalar, Self)>>  (keys as long as the whole, cut per chunk; one (key, rows) per distinct key in first-occurrence order across the chunks, each group's chunks what each chunk contributed, kept apart)
  pub fn window_by(&self, by: impl IntoSelector, sorted: bool) -> crate::Result<Vec<(Scalar, Self)>>  (the windows Serie::window_by cuts the joined column into, each zero-copy pieces of the chunks: a run crossing a chunk edge one window, the pending key compared in place at each edge; sorted regroups runs stably by key, never rows, with no join; no window states a record - the key is the item's first half, the place its index - so a key cell named windownum or rownum is taken; Serie::window_by's other refusals, naming the field, before any row is read)
  pub fn memory_size(&self) -> usize  (the chunks summed)
  pub fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (every chunk's Serie::resident_size, summed - kept beside the chunks, so the answer is a read)
  pub fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool  (no byte resident and some bytes, read in that order; a chunked serie of no chunk never)
  pub fn as_spilled(&mut self, options: &SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<&mut Self>  (spill, answering this chunked serie so calls chain)
  pub fn into_spilled(&self, options: &SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<Self>  (a copy spilled, this one untouched: the chunks the bound leaves resident shared, the rest written once and mapped)
  pub fn spill(&mut self, options: &SpillOptions) -> crate::Result<()>  (the heaviest chunks spilled whole first, each through Serie::spill, the sum read again after each, until the resident bytes are under the bound; a chunked serie under it untouched; a refusal leaves the chunks spilled so far mapped)
  pub fn as_sorted(&mut self, options: SortOptions) -> Result<&mut Self>  (in place, chaining: into_sorted's merged chunks replacing the chunks; a refusal leaves the serie as it was)
  pub fn sort_indices_by(&self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<Serie>  (the keys resolved, then the one join, then Serie::sort_indices_by: the positions are one column addressing every row, so they stay the join)
  pub fn as_sort_by(&mut self, by: impl IntoOrderings) -> Result<&mut Self>  (in place, chaining: into_sort_by's merged chunks replacing the chunks; a refusal leaves the serie as it was)
  pub fn as_unique(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn as_reversed(&mut self) -> Result<&mut Self>  (the chunks reversed, each reversed where it stands)
  pub fn as_taken(&mut self, indices: &Serie) -> Result<&mut Self>
  pub fn as_filtered(&mut self, mask: &Serie) -> Result<&mut Self>
  impl PartialEq<Serie> for ChunkedSerie
  impl PartialEq<ChunkedSerie> for Serie
  impl PartialOrd<Serie> for ChunkedSerie
  impl PartialOrd<ChunkedSerie> for Serie
  impl IntoIterator for &ChunkedSerie  (item Scalar)
  pub struct ChunkedRows<'a>  (the rows, each built as the walk reaches it, chunk after chunk; Iterator<Item = Scalar> with an exact size_hint, FusedIterator)

### yggdryl::serie::partition  [rust/src/serie/partition.rs]  (a stream's rows cut by a key into partitions held apart under the process spill bound, each closed as it completes; the one partitioner every split of a stream by partition runs through - the Iceberg table writer's and the partitioned folder writer's - is crate-private here; re-exported at the root)
  pub struct PartitionOptions  (how StreamChunkedSerie::partition_by cuts a stream: the partitions open at once, the threads its batches are cut on, and whether its rows of one key arrive together; Copy, Default every partition open on the host's threads, unclustered; Debug, Eq, Hash)
  pub const fn new() -> Self
  pub const fn with_clustered(mut self, clustered: bool) -> Self  (every row of a key arrives before any row of the next: each partition closed as soon as another key arrives, one open at a time, max_open moot; a key returning after its partition closed a new piece of it - pieces, never rows)
  pub const fn with_max_open(mut self, partitions: usize) -> Self  (at most partitions open, at least one, the lowest keys closed past it)
  pub const fn with_threads(mut self, threads: usize) -> Self  (at least one; one cuts each batch as it is pulled and reads nothing ahead)
  pub const fn max_open(&self) -> Option<usize>
  pub fn threads(&self) -> usize  (as stated, else the host's)
  pub const fn is_clustered(&self) -> bool
  pub struct KeySerie  (one closed partition: its key and its rows, in arrival order, held as the chunks the stream's batches gave it; Clone, Debug)
  pub const fn key(&self) -> &Scalar  (the record of the key cells, as ChunkedSerie::window_by states a key)
  pub const fn rows(&self) -> &ChunkedSerie
  pub fn into_parts(self) -> (Scalar, ChunkedSerie)
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(&self) -> Result<BatchReader>  (one batch per chunk, buffers shared)
  pub struct StreamKeySerie  (Iterator<Item = crate::arrow::Result<KeySerie>>, Send; each partition yielded as it closes - past max_open the lowest keys, clustered once another key arrives - and every one still open when the stream ends in ascending key order; fused after its first error)
  pub fn field(&self) -> &Field  (the record root every partition's rows are held under)
  pub fn partition_by(self, by: impl IntoSelector, options: PartitionOptions) -> Result<StreamKeySerie>  (StreamChunkedSerie's: by bound once against the root before any pull; each batch landed, keyed and cut on options.threads() threads in order - a run of a key one zero-copy slice, a key otherwise one take - and pushed on the pulling thread, the open partitions settled under the process spill bound after each batch, heaviest first; clustered where stated, or where the root declares an order, proven as its batches land, leading with the terms by projects, uncast, in any order and direction)

### yggdryl::serie::primitive  [rust/src/serie/primitive.rs]  (one column of fixed-width rows over every Arrow primitive type, and the 35 named leaves)
  pub trait PrimitiveLeaf: ArrowPrimitiveType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one Arrow primitive type to the leaf its column widens to)
  const NAME: &'static str
  fn into_serie(column: PrimitiveSerie<Self>) -> Serie
  fn from_serie(serie: &Serie) -> Option<&PrimitiveSerie<Self>>
  pub trait NativeLeaf: PrimitiveLeaf  (a leaf whose native domain is its datatype's whole domain, so a typed writer can take a native value: Int8 .. UInt64, Float16/32/64, Date32, DateTime, Duration and Interval; a decimal, Date64, Time32 or Time64 leaf is narrower than its storage and writes only through Scalar)
  pub struct PrimitiveSerie<T: PrimitiveLeaf>  (Clone is pointer bumps; Debug via debug_column; identity over the rows)
  pub fn values(&self) -> &[T::Native]  (the values buffer, lent)
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<T::Native>  (None when absent or past the end)
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &PrimitiveArray<T>
  pub fn push_value(&mut self, value: Option<T::Native>) -> Result<()>  (T: NativeLeaf, as are the three below; refuses None under a required field naming the column and leaves the column unchanged)
  pub fn set_value(&mut self, index: usize, value: Option<T::Native>) -> Result<()>  (one buffer write in place)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, values: Vec<Option<T::Native>>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn extend_values(&mut self, values: Vec<Option<T::Native>>) -> Result<()>
  impl SerieValue for PrimitiveSerie<T>
  pub type Int8Serie = PrimitiveSerie<Int8Type>  // and Int16Serie, Int32Serie, Int64Serie, UInt8Serie, UInt16Serie, UInt32Serie, UInt64Serie, Float16Serie, Float32Serie, Float64Serie, Decimal32Serie, Decimal64Serie, Decimal128Serie, Decimal256Serie, Date32Serie, Date64Serie, Time32SecondSerie, Time32MillisecondSerie, Time64MicrosecondSerie, Time64NanosecondSerie, DateTimeSecondSerie, DateTimeMillisecondSerie, DateTimeMicrosecondSerie, DateTimeNanosecondSerie, DurationSecondSerie, DurationMillisecondSerie, DurationMicrosecondSerie, DurationNanosecondSerie, IntervalYearMonthSerie, IntervalDayTimeSerie, IntervalMonthDayNanoSerie: one alias per Arrow primitive type, each tied to its root variant

### yggdryl::serie::boolean  [rust/src/serie/boolean.rs]  (one column of booleans: a values bitmap and a validity bitmap)
  pub struct BooleanSerie
  pub fn values(&self) -> &BooleanBuffer
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<bool>
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &BooleanArray
  pub fn push_value(&mut self, value: Option<bool>) -> Result<()>  (refuses None under a required field naming the column)
  pub fn set_value(&mut self, index: usize, value: Option<bool>) -> Result<()>  (one bit write in place)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, values: Vec<Option<bool>>) -> Result<()>
  impl SerieValue for BooleanSerie

### yggdryl::serie::lit  [rust/src/serie/lit.rs]  (the constant column: one field, one value, a length, and the Arrow array it lays out as, built on the first export and shared after)
  pub struct LitSerie  (Clone is pointer bumps; Debug via debug_column; identity, serde and the digest are the rows it is - equal to and written as the laid-out column)
  pub const fn value(&self) -> &Scalar  (the value every row holds, canonical under the field)
  pub const fn row(&self) -> &Serie  (the value laid out once as a one-row column of the field)
  pub fn is_built(&self) -> bool  (whether the column has been laid out whole and is held)
  pub fn laid_out(&self) -> &Serie  (the column laid out as its field's own leaf, the one row taken `rows` times: built on the first call and shared by every later one; what a typed narrowing - as_int64, as_utf8 - reads a constant through)
  pub fn array(&self) -> ArrayRef  (laid_out's buffers)
  impl SerieValue for LitSerie  (scalar clones the value; slice moves the count, a slice of a built column sharing its buffers; splice of the value moves the count, of another value a conflict - Serie::splice lays the column out first; memory_size the laid-out estimate, one row's bytes times the rows; resident_size the one row and the built array while held; is_spilled never; spill forgets the built array and writes nothing)

### yggdryl::serie::null  [rust/src/serie/null.rs]  (one column of nulls: a length, and no buffer at all)
  pub struct NullSerie
  pub fn array(&self) -> NullArray  (built on demand)
  pub const fn push_value(&mut self)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, count: usize) -> Result<()>  (range refusal only)
  impl SerieValue for NullSerie

### yggdryl::serie::bytes  [rust/src/serie/bytes.rs]  (every column whose rows are a run of bytes: offsets into one payload, views into several, or one fixed width; the marker says whether the field reads them as text or as bytes, and no byte leaf has a typed writer)
  pub trait ByteKind: Send + Sync + Clone + Copy + fmt::Debug + 'static  (which family a run of bytes belongs to when its layout belongs to both)
  pub struct Chars  (the marker for bytes a field reads as text)
  pub struct Octets  (the marker for bytes a field reads as bytes)
  pub trait ByteLeaf<K: ByteKind>: ByteArrayType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one offsets layout under one marker to its root variant: NAME, into_serie, from_serie)
  pub trait ViewLeaf<K: ByteKind>: ByteViewType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (the same for a view layout)
  pub trait FixedLeaf: Sized  (the same for the fixed-width layout)
  pub struct ByteSerie<T: ByteArrayType, K: ByteKind>  (offsets into one payload buffer; Utf8, LargeUtf8, Binary, LargeBinary under Chars or Octets)
  pub fn offsets(&self) -> &OffsetBuffer<T::Offset>
  pub fn payload(&self) -> &Buffer
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &GenericByteArray<T>
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T::Native>  (None when absent or past the end)
  impl SerieValue for ByteSerie<T, K>
  pub struct ByteViewSerie<T: ByteViewType, K: ByteKind>  (views into several payload buffers; a write repacks every run)
  pub fn views(&self) -> &[u128]
  pub fn payloads(&self) -> &[Buffer]
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &GenericByteViewArray<T>
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T::Native>
  impl SerieValue for ByteViewSerie<T, K>
  pub struct FixedSerie<K: ByteKind>  (one payload buffer and no offsets; a same-count overwrite is one memcpy at index * width)
  pub fn width(&self) -> i32
  pub fn payload(&self) -> &Buffer
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &FixedSizeBinaryArray
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&[u8]>
  impl SerieValue for FixedSerie<K>
  pub type BinarySerie = ByteSerie<BinaryType, Octets>
  pub type LargeBinarySerie = ByteSerie<LargeBinaryType, Octets>
  pub type BinaryViewSerie = ByteViewSerie<BinaryViewType, Octets>
  pub type FixedBytesSerie = FixedSerie<Octets>  (a UUID, or any identity stored at one width)

### yggdryl::serie::string  [rust/src/serie/string.rs]  (the string family's leaves over the byte layouts: the field says which of the eighteen string leaves or seventeen codes the bytes are)
  pub type Utf8StringSerie = ByteSerie<Utf8Type, Chars>
  pub type LargeUtf8StringSerie = ByteSerie<LargeUtf8Type, Chars>
  pub type Utf8ViewStringSerie = ByteViewSerie<StringViewType, Chars>
  pub type BinaryStringSerie = ByteSerie<BinaryType, Chars>  (a windows-1252 or other non-UTF-8 charset, stored as bytes)
  pub type LargeBinaryStringSerie = ByteSerie<LargeBinaryType, Chars>
  pub type BinaryViewStringSerie = ByteViewSerie<BinaryViewType, Chars>
  pub type FixedStringSerie = FixedSerie<Chars>

### yggdryl::serie::structure  [rust/src/serie/structure.rs]  (one column of records: one child column per child field beside the record's validity; no child is mutably reachable, so alignment cannot break)
  pub struct StructSerie
  pub fn children(&self) -> &[Serie]
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Serie>  (by exact name, as Field::index_of resolves it)
  pub fn child_at(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&Serie>
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn set_child(&mut self, child: Serie) -> Result<()>  (replaces the child of child's name or adds one, extending the field; refuses a run and a length other than len)
  pub fn set_cell(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (one slot written in place, path deep; refuses an absent row on the way: "row {index} of {name} is absent; set the whole row")
  pub fn without_child(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Self>  (a new column sharing every other child)
  impl SerieValue for StructSerie

### yggdryl::serie::sequence  [rust/src/serie/sequence.rs]  (the five serie layouts: an offsets cut, a viewed cut kept compact, or width items per row, each over one item column; every cut is rebased)
  pub trait OffsetLeaf: OffsetSizeTrait + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one offset width to the offsets leaf and the viewed leaf it widens through)
  fn into_serie(column: OffsetSerie<Self>) -> Serie
  fn from_serie(serie: &Serie) -> Option<&OffsetSerie<Self>>
  fn into_view_serie(column: OffsetViewSerie<Self>) -> Serie
  fn from_view_serie(serie: &Serie) -> Option<&OffsetViewSerie<Self>>
  pub struct OffsetSerie<O: OffsetSizeTrait>
  pub type SerieSerie = OffsetSerie<i32>
  pub type LargeSerieSerie = OffsetSerie<i64>
  pub const fn items(&self) -> &Serie
  pub const fn offsets(&self) -> &OffsetBuffer<O>
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>  (the item range row index occupies; None when absent or past the end)
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>  (the item column sliced to that row, zero copy)
  impl SerieValue for OffsetSerie<O>
  pub struct OffsetViewSerie<O: OffsetSizeTrait>  (views compact: row i starts where row i - 1 ended; the door rebases a foreign layout onto exactly the items it reaches)
  pub type SerieViewSerie = OffsetViewSerie<i32>
  pub type LargeSerieViewSerie = OffsetViewSerie<i64>
  pub const fn items(&self) -> &Serie
  pub const fn offsets(&self) -> &ScalarBuffer<O>
  pub const fn sizes(&self) -> &ScalarBuffer<O>
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>
  impl SerieValue for OffsetViewSerie<O>
  pub struct FixedSizeSerieSerie  (width items per row and no cut; a null row holds width placeholder items and a cleared bit)
  pub const fn width(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn items(&self) -> &Serie
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>
  impl SerieValue for FixedSizeSerieSerie
