### yggdryl::value  [rust/src/value/mod.rs]  (what a datatype, a field and a value each owe the root that holds them, and the leaf contracts the leaves of one family share - a family itself is no type, only the DataTypeId range its DataTypeKind owns; the module is private and every name here is re-exported at the crate root as yggdryl::<Name>)
pub trait Value: Sized + Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + 'static  (one concrete scalar representation; no associated consts: a leaf's identity is its dtype; no Family type - a leaf widens through into_scalar)
  fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>
  fn into_scalar(self) -> Scalar
  fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Option<&Self>
  fn into_variant(&self) -> Result<Variant>  (encode through Scalar using the Parquet Variant mapping)
  fn from_variant(value: &Variant) -> Result<Self>  (decode and require this exact leaf; Variant itself shares its buffers)
pub trait IntegerValue: Value  (implemented by Int8, Int16, Int32, Int64, UInt8, UInt16, UInt32, UInt64, Int128 and UInt128)
  const SIGNED: bool
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn as_i128(&self) -> Option<i128>
  fn as_u128(&self) -> Option<u128>
  fn from_i128(value: i128) -> Result<Self>
pub trait FloatingValue: Value  (implemented by Float16, Float32 and Float64)
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn as_f64(&self) -> f64
pub trait DecimalValue: Value  (implemented by Decimal32, Decimal64, Decimal128, Decimal256, and by Decimal and BigDecimal at their one scale, a rescale to any other refused)
  fn coefficient(&self) -> i256
  fn scale(&self) -> i8
  fn rescale(self, scale: i8) -> Result<Self>
pub trait TemporalValue: Value  (implemented by Date32, Date64, Time32, Time64, DateTime64, Duration32, Duration64 and Interval)
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn count(&self) -> i64
  fn unit(&self) -> TimeUnit
  fn timezone(&self) -> Timezone
  fn with_unit(self, unit: TimeUnit) -> Result<Self>
  fn with_timezone(self, timezone: Timezone) -> Result<Self>
pub trait GeospatialValue: Value  (implemented by Geometry and Geography)
  fn as_bytes(&self) -> &[u8]
  fn storage(&self) -> &Arc<[u8]>
pub trait CodeValue: Value  (implemented by the seventeen registered codes)
  const WIDTH: usize
  fn as_str(&self) -> &str
  fn storage(&self) -> &SmolStr
  const MAX_RANK: u8  (provided as 1: the rank of a real value - 2 for Isin and Cfi)
  fn rank(&self) -> u8  (provided as MAX_RANK: how real this value is, from zero to MAX_RANK - `new` admits the shape, and what the shape leaves open is this reading: an Isin closing under a listed prefix 2, closing or listed 1, neither 0; a Cusip, Sedol, Figi, Lei or Dti 1 where it closes; a Country 1 where ISO 3166 lists it; a Bic 1 where its country is listed or SWIFT's `XK`; a Ccy `XXX`, a Mic `XXXX` and an empty Unit 0; a Cfi unclassified 0, classified 1, detailed 2; every other code 1)
  fn is_real(&self) -> bool  (provided: `rank() == MAX_RANK`)
  fn merge_with(self, other: &Self) -> Self  (provided: the better statement of two codes of one kind - the other where it outranks this one, else this one, so a real value replaces a placeholder, a masked number or a typo whatever the order and two of one rank keep the one that leads; a Cfi folds further, filling every `X` from the other where the two describe one instrument, and keeping this code across another category or group or a contradicted attribute)
pub trait EnumValue: Value + Copy + Default  (implemented by the Enum-family leaves State, MarketDataKind, Side, MarketDataType, TimeInForce and PluginSide through `enum_leaf!`: a closed set of members, each stored as its unsigned code at the width of its `Repr`)
  const ALL: &'static [Self]
  const KIND: &'static str  (the datatype's name and the refusal word)
  const EXTENSION_NAME: &'static str
  type Repr: EnumRepr  (the width the codes are held and stored at: u8 or u16)
  fn code(self) -> Self::Repr
  fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  fn description(self) -> &'static str
  fn from_code(code: Self::Repr) -> Option<Self>
  fn read(spelling: &str) -> Result<Self>
  fn read_code(code: i64) -> Result<Self>
  fn from_scalar_value(value: &Scalar) -> Option<Self>  (provided: a member, the code an integer holds or a spelling text holds; `None` where it names none)
pub trait NestedValue: Value  (implemented by Run, Serie, Map and Struct)
  fn len(&self) -> usize
  fn is_empty(&self) -> bool  (provided: len() == 0)
  fn children(&self) -> Children<'_>
pub trait FieldSidecar: Clone + fmt::Debug + Default + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (the per-column facts only one datatype has; `()` for every datatype but the dictionary-encoded one)
  fn dictionary_id(&self) -> Option<i64>  (provided: None)
  fn dictionary_is_ordered(&self) -> Option<bool>  (provided: None)
  fn set_dictionary_options(&mut self, id: i64, is_ordered: bool) -> Result<bool>  (provided: refused, "dictionary options require a dictionary datatype")
  impl FieldSidecar for ()
pub struct DictionaryOptions  (Arrow's IPC dictionary identifier and ordering flag, one column's encoding rather than its datatype; Copy, Default, Eq/Ord/Hash; FieldSidecar answering both and setting both, reporting whether anything changed)
pub trait DataTypeValue: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (one datatype: a family's payload, or the root that redirects to it; DataType itself implements it with FAMILY = "datatype" and Sidecar = DictionaryOptions)
  const FAMILY: &'static str  (the family's parameter-free name, as a binding and a refusal spell it)
  type Sidecar: FieldSidecar
  fn id(&self) -> DataTypeId
  fn kind(&self) -> DataTypeKind  (provided: self.id().kind(), the family whose range the id is in)
  fn validate(&self) -> Result<()>
  fn into_dtype(self) -> DataType
  fn from_dtype(dtype: &DataType) -> Option<Self>  (owned: a payload is Copy or one shared pointer)
  fn cast_scalar(&self, value: &crate::Scalar) -> Result<crate::Scalar>  (provided: the datatype is the target, and every family answers through the root's one recursive walk; Arrow casts are Serie's)
  fn encode_value_stream_bytes(&self, value: &crate::Scalar) -> Result<crate::ValueStream>  (provided: the value cast to this datatype and encoded one chunk per leaf; encode_value_bytes, decode_value_bytes and decode_value_stream_bytes beside it, each through the datatype the leaf widens to)
pub trait FieldValue<D: DataTypeValue>: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Sized  (one field over one family's datatype; implemented by every FieldOf<D> and by Field as FieldValue<DataType>)
  fn name(&self) -> &str
  fn dtype(&self) -> DataType
  fn typed_dtype(&self) -> D
  fn is_nullable(&self) -> bool
  fn metadata(&self) -> &Metadata
  fn validate(&self) -> Result<()>
  fn into_field(self) -> Field
  fn from_field(field: &Field) -> Option<&Self>
  fn cast_scalar(&self, value: &crate::Scalar) -> Result<crate::Scalar>  (provided: the datatype's door under the field, whose nullability is the one thing a datatype does not state; Arrow casts are Serie's)
pub enum Children<'a>  (Sequence(slice::Iter<'a, Scalar>), Mapping(slice::Iter<'a, (Scalar, Scalar)>), Struct(btree_map::Values<'a, SmolStr, Scalar>), Column(ColumnRows<'a>); Iterator<Item = Cow<'a, Scalar>> over sequence values, mapping keys, struct values or a column's rows - borrowed where the value is already there, built where it is not; DoubleEndedIterator, ExactSizeIterator, FusedIterator)
pub struct ColumnRows<'a>  (the rows of one column, built one at a time as the walk reaches them; a stored row is one its field accepts, so none refuses)
pub trait SerieValue: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (what every column leaf of Serie owes: its field, the datatype id and family that field answers, its length, one row built on demand, a zero-copy slice, the one mutation splice - prove once, check once, write without failing - its buffers as an Arrow array, and the widening to and narrowing from Serie; Serie itself does not implement it, the run having no field)
  fn field(&self) -> &Field
  fn field_ref(&self) -> &Arc<Field>
  fn id(&self) -> DataTypeId  (provided: self.field().id() - the field's, because one storage layout holds several datatypes; a dictionary or run-end column answers its encoding)
  fn kind(&self) -> DataTypeKind  (provided: self.id().kind())
  fn len(&self) -> usize
  fn is_empty(&self) -> bool  (provided)
  fn null_count(&self) -> usize
  fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> Result<bool>
  fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  fn slice(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<Self>
  fn splice(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>
  fn into_arrow_array(&self) -> ArrayRef  (borrows: the buffers are already shared)
  fn memory_size(&self) -> usize  (provided: array_memory_size of into_arrow_array; every leaf overrides it to read its own buffers)
  fn resident_size(&self) -> usize  (memory_size less what lies in a spill file's mapping: a flat leaf all or nothing, a nested leaf its own unmapped buffers beside its children's; read off the buffers, allocating nothing)
  fn is_spilled(&self) -> bool  (provided: memory_size() > 0 && resident_size() == 0)
  fn spill(&mut self, options: &crate::SpillOptions) -> Result<()>  (provided: Serie::spill over this leaf, which comes back as itself; a leaf that does not is a conflict naming the column)
  fn into_serie(self) -> Serie
  fn from_serie(value: &Serie) -> Option<&Self>
  fn set(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (provided over splice, as are the eight below, with Serie's semantics)
  fn push(&mut self, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>
  fn insert(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>
  fn remove(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  fn pop(&mut self) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>
  fn truncate(&mut self, len: usize) -> Result<()>
  fn clear(&mut self) -> Result<()>
  fn extend(&mut self, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>
  fn resize(&mut self, len: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>
pub struct UnionType  (the datatype of a DataType::Union field: `fields: UnionFields`, `mode: UnionMode`; Clone, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display = the datatype's; DataTypeValue with FAMILY = "union", kind Nested, Sidecar = ())
  pub const fn new(fields: crate::UnionFields, mode: crate::UnionMode) -> Self
  pub const fn fields(&self) -> &crate::UnionFields
  pub const fn mode(&self) -> &crate::UnionMode
pub struct RunEndType  (the datatype of a DataType::RunEndEncoded field: `encoding: Arc<RunEndEncodedType>`; the same derives; DataTypeValue with FAMILY = "run_end_encoded", kind Nested, Sidecar = ())
  pub const fn new(encoding: Arc<RunEndEncodedType>) -> Self
  pub const fn encoding(&self) -> &Arc<RunEndEncodedType>
pub struct GeometryType  (the datatype of a DataType::Geometry field: `parameters: Arc<GeospatialParameters>`; the same derives; DataTypeValue with FAMILY = "geometry", kind Geospatial, Sidecar = ())
  pub const fn new(parameters: Arc<GeospatialParameters>) -> Self
  pub const fn parameters(&self) -> &Arc<GeospatialParameters>
pub struct GeographyType  (the same over a DataType::Geography field, FAMILY = "geography")
  pub const fn new(parameters: Arc<GeospatialParameters>) -> Self
  pub const fn parameters(&self) -> &Arc<GeospatialParameters>

### yggdryl::temporal  [rust/src/temporal.rs]  (what the five temporal families share: the ISO 8601 spellings and the Scalar readers that answer across the families, which family a leaf is in being DataTypeId::temporal_family; no datatype, no field and no leaf value lives here, and TemporalValue, the contract every leaf answers, is under yggdryl::value)
impl Scalar
  pub const fn temporal_unit(&self) -> Option<TimeUnit>  (an interval answers its layout unit)

  pub fn temporal_dtype(&self) -> Option<DataType>

  pub fn closing_digits(body: &str) -> Option<u8>  (the two digits, 2..=98, closing eighteen upper-case letters or digits)
  pub const fn lei() -> Self
  pub type LeiField = FieldOf<LeiType>;

### yggdryl::idkey  [rust/src/idkey.rs]  (the key an identifier is held under: the source that gave it and the type of name it is, spelled `src:type`, a key from the base source spelled as its type alone)
pub struct IdKey { src, kind }  (an `IdSource` and an `IdType`; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq, Ord by the bytes of its spelling - `cusip` < `derived:cusip` < `isin` < `oms:instrumentid` < `ullink:isin` - Display `src:type` (`isin` for a base key), FromStr, `PartialEq<str>` and `PartialEq<&str>` comparing the spelling without building it, `From<&IdKey> for SmolStr` a static string for a key of two member words; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub const fn new(src: IdSource, kind: IdType) -> Self
  pub const fn base(kind: IdType) -> Self  (the type's base key: its answer in an `Identifiers` map)
  pub fn src(&self) -> &IdSource
  pub fn kind(&self) -> &IdType
  pub fn is_base(&self) -> bool
  pub fn with_kind(&self, kind: IdType) -> Self  (the same source under another type)
  impl FromStr for IdKey  (reads exactly: `src:type` that source and that type, each folded, a bare word that type from the base source - `ISIN`, `ISIN_Number`, `base:isin`, `BASE:ISIN` and `fix:isin` are all `isin`, `marketorderid` the type `marketorderid`; refuses an empty half, a second `:` and a word no fold reads, `expected an identifier key src:type or type`; nothing is inferred - `Identifier::from_key` infers; Err = Error)

### yggdryl::identifier  [rust/src/identifier.rs]  (identifiers: a value under a key - an `IdKey`, the source that gave it and the type of name it is - and the sorted map from the key an element states them in: `securityids`, `identifiers`, `partyids`, each laid out as a sorted `map<utf8, utf8>`, the base key of a type its answer)
pub const IDENTIFIER_WORD_WIDTH: usize = 64  (the most bytes a type or a source folds to: room for the longest FIX code-set name a party role or source has)
pub const IDENTIFIER_VALUE_WIDTH: usize = 64
pub struct IdWord(SmolStr)  (a word no `IdType` or `IdSource` member names, folded: lower-case ASCII letters, digits and `.`, 1 to 64 bytes; only a fold builds one, so a word a member names is never one; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str  (the folded word)
pub struct Identifier { key, value }  (an `IdKey` and a `SmolStr`; the value trimmed text stating something, held as its type stores it; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord (the key as spelled, then the value), Display `key=value` - `isin=US0378331005`, `ullink:isin=US0378331005`)
  pub fn new(key: IdKey, value: &str) -> Result<Self>  (the one constructor: refuses a value that states nothing, is null-like (`null`, `none`, `n/a`) or its type refuses - the shape of an ISIN, CUSIP, SEDOL or FIGI, whose check digit is a rank (`IdType::rank`) rather than a refusal, the canonical pair, a WKN's or a Valor number's shape, the bound `IdType::max_value_width`; holds it as the type stores it, upper-cased where the type folds case; under the `bic` source a value must also be a BIC by shape and under `legalentityidentifier` an LEI, whatever its type - a role, the account, an `Other` word - held upper-cased, else refused located on the key: `invalid record value at bic:executingtrader: a value under the bic source is a BIC: expected eight or eleven characters, got "T-1"`)
  pub fn with_kind(&self, kind: IdType) -> Result<Self>  (this identifier's value under another type of the same source - a parent's own type, a type's parent - held as that type stores it)
  pub fn from_key(key: &str, value: &str) -> Option<Self>  (infers the identifier a name no key spells names: an explicit `src:type`; else a whole security name - `ISINCode`, `security_cusip` - as that type's base key; else the name folded and the longest identifier name it ends with - a spelling the crate reads a type by, an alias included, that ends with `id`, `account`, `isin`, `cusip`, `sedol` or `figi`, or a security type's ending with `code`, `symbol`, `number` or `ticker` - `OMS_RICCODE` names its `ric`, `ULLINK.ISINCODE` its `isin`, `OMS_BloombergTicker` its `bloomberg` - a parentage word `parent`/`orig`/`origin`/`original` right before it kept inside the type - under the source the rest of the folded name names, its `.` trimmed at both ends and kept inside, the base source where nothing is left or where it folds to a source the crate reserves - `base`, `fix`, `derived` - which names no namespace (`Derived_ISIN` and `FIX.ISIN` are `isin`; an explicit `src:type` keeps its source, `derived:isin` included): `firm.x.ParentOrderID` is `firm.x:parentorderid`, `OMS_InstrumentID` `oms:instrumentid`, `marketorderid` `market:orderid`; a security type is refused where `leg`, `underlying`, `contra`, `related` or `benchmark` opens the name or ends what it spells before the type, after any namespace; none where the name names no identifier, the value states nothing or its type refuses it)
  pub fn key(&self) -> &IdKey
  pub fn src(&self) -> &IdSource
  pub fn kind(&self) -> &IdType
  pub fn value(&self) -> &str
pub struct Identifiers(Vec<Identifier>)  (a sorted map from a key to its value, held in the key as spelled: a lookup a binary search, an insertion one shifted slice; one vector, 24 bytes, an empty set holding no backing; the base rule: a named source fills its type's base key where it is empty or ranks below, a statement takes back the type's derivation (`derived:T`) and its echo unless the derivation outranks it - a named one then standing under its own key as evidence - a derivation lands only where nothing of its type is held or the base key ranks below it and its echo follows it, the base key moves only through its own key - so wherever a type is held its base key is; a higher rank replaces a lower one under any key through `insert`, `merge`, `carry` and a read map's close whatever the order, two of one rank folding by that order; under `bic` or `legalentityidentifier`, the lower of the type's and code's rank applies to that named identifier; a base key ranks from same-valued nonbase statements - the highest such rank, or the type's rank if none; a same-valued statement from a source without a registered code gives the type's rank - so a base answer filled by a BIC or LEI typo is replaced by a listed-country BIC or closing LEI whichever arrives first; Clone, Debug, Default, Eq, Hash, PartialEq, Display `[a=b, ...]`, `FromIterator<Identifier>` through `insert`, `IntoIterator` by reference)
  pub const fn new() -> Self
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn iter(&self) -> std::slice::Iter<'_, Identifier>
  pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[Identifier]
  pub fn get(&self, kind: &IdType) -> Option<&str>  (the base key's value: the type's answer, whichever source stated it)
  pub fn get_from(&self, key: &IdKey) -> Option<&str>  (the value under exactly that key)
  pub fn contains_kind(&self, kind: &IdType) -> bool  (whether the base key is held, which it is wherever any key of the type is)
  pub fn of_kind<'map>(&'map self, kind: &'map IdType) -> impl Iterator<Item = &'map Identifier>  (every key of the type, the base key included, in key order)
  pub fn is_derived(&self, kind: &IdType) -> bool  (the base key holds only a derivation: the value of `derived:kind`, which no named source states)
  pub fn insert(&mut self, id: Identifier) -> bool  (fills an absent key, or replaces a value ranking below `id`, under the base rule, two values of one rank keeping the first; whether `id`'s own key landed)
  pub fn set(&mut self, id: Identifier) -> bool  (replaces the value under `id`'s key under the base rule, the explicit statement - a base id replaces the answer and leaves the named sources, a named one its type's derivation outranks replacing its own key alone, a derivation refused where a statement of its type is held; whether anything moved)
  pub fn remove(&mut self, key: &IdKey) -> Option<Identifier>  (a named source's key that identifier alone; `derived:T` the derivation, its echo following; a base key every key of its type, answering the base one)
  pub fn merge(&mut self, other: &Self, later: bool) -> bool  (a union by key: where both hold a key with two values, the one ranking higher, and between two of one rank `other`'s when `later`, else this map's - a later placeholder never replaces a real value; a derivation of `other`'s lands only where this map holds nothing of its type or a base key ranking below it; whether anything moved)
  pub fn clear(&mut self)
  pub fn carry(&mut self, previous: &Self, carried: impl Fn(&Identifier) -> bool) -> bool  (each identifier `previous` - the same element's map one step earlier - holds under a key this map lacks or holds only as the echo of a derivation, where `carried` admits it, through `insert`; whether any was)
  pub fn follow_parents<'list>(&mut self, previous: &Self, parents_of: impl Fn(&IdType) -> Cow<'list, [IdType]>, parent_of: impl Fn(&IdType) -> Option<(IdType, usize)>) -> bool  (for each type with parents this map states - never a parent type - under a source `previous` also states it, fills each parent it does not state: a type that kept its value takes each parent the previous statement held; one that changed takes the previous value as its first parent, each middle parent from the previous one a step nearer, and the last of two or more the chain's first value; base-source fills first; whether anything moved)
  pub fn fill_parents(&mut self, parent_of: impl Fn(&IdType) -> Option<(IdType, usize)>) -> bool  (a type a stated parent names and the map does not state, under the parent's source, takes the value of the nearest stated parent - `parentorderid` before `origorderid` - base-source parents first; whether anything moved)
  pub fn dtype() -> DataType  (`map<entries: struct<key: utf8 not null, value: utf8 not null>, keys_sorted = true>`: the key its `IdKey`'s spelling, the value the identifier's)
  pub fn into_scalar(&self) -> Scalar  (a `Scalar::SortedMap` of the key text and the value, in key order; a key of two member words a static string)
  pub fn from_scalar(scalar: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (a `Map` or a `SortedMap` in any order, or a sequence of such maps - their union - each key read exactly through `IdKey::from_str`, then closed by the base rule - a type with no base key taking its highest-ranked named source's value, the first in key order among equals, unless its derivation outranks every named one; refuses, located `$['k']` or `$[i]['k']`, a key that reads as none, a value that states nothing or its type refuses, two spellings of one key with two values, and a key or value that is not text)

### yggdryl::idtype  [rust/src/idtype.rs]  (the type of name an identifier is: one vocabulary for security, own and party identifiers)
pub enum IdType  (eighty-nine members the crate names, each a static folded word, and `Other(IdWord)` for any other: the thirty-three FIX `SecurityIDSource(22)` types - `Cusip` 1, `Sedol` 2, `Quik`, `Isin` 4, `Ric` 5, `IsoCcy`, `IsoCtry`, `ExchSymb`, `Cta`, `Bloomberg` A, `Wkn`, `Dutch`, `Valor`, `Sicovam`, `Belgian`, `Common`, `ClearingHouse` (also its full name `clearinghouseclearingorganization`; `clearingorganization` alone is the party role), `FpmlSpec`, `Opra`, `FpmlUrl`, `Loc`, `MktAssigned`, `RedEntity`, `RedPair`, `Cftc`, `IsdaCommodity`, `Figi` S, `Lei`, `Synthetic`, `Fim`, `Index`, `Umtf`, `Dti` Y - the crate's own `Forex` (a currency pair, no FIX code), `Cfi`, `Fisn` (an ISO 18774 short name, FIX's `FinancialInstrumentShortName(2737)`, no FIX code; also `fisncode`, `financialinstrumentshortname`) and `InstrumentId`, `Elf` (an ISO 20275 entity legal form, neither a security nor a party; also `elfcode`, `entitylegalform`, `entitylegalformcode`), the operation identifiers `OrderId`, `ClOrdId`, `OrigClOrdId` (`clordid`'s one parent, also spelled `parentclordid`), `SecondaryOrderId`, `SecondaryClOrdId`, `SecondaryExecId`, `SecondaryQuoteId`, `SecondaryTradeId`, `SecondaryFirmTradeId`, `SecondaryAllocId`, `SecondaryIndividualAllocId`, `ExecId`, `QuoteId`, `QuoteReqId`, `MdReqId`, `TrdMatchId`, `TradeId`, `TradeReportId`, `MdEntryId`, `MdEntryRefId`, the regulatory trade ids `RegTradeId`, `PrevRegTradeId`, `BlockRegTradeId`, `RelatedRegTradeId`, `ClearedRegTradeId`, `Tvtic`, `ReportTrackingNumber`, and `Account`, `Party`, `UserId` and the `PartyRole(452)` roles `ExecutingFirm`, `ClientId`, `ClearingFirm`, `InvestorId`, `EnteringFirm`, `OrderOriginationTrader`, `ExecutingTrader`, `OrderOriginationFirm`, `ExecutingSystem`, `ContraFirm`, `ClearingOrganization` (21), `Exchange`, `CustomerAccount`, `EnteringTrader`, `ContraTrader`, `PositionAccount`, `OrderEntryOperatorId`, `ExecutionVenue`, `DeskId`, `InvestmentDecisionMaker`, `Algorithm`; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq, Ord by spelling, `PartialEq<str>` and `PartialEq<&str>` so `id.kind() == "isin"` holds, AsRef<str>, Display, FromStr, `From<IdType> for SmolStr`; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub const KNOWN: [Self; 89]  (every member the crate names, in declaration order)
  pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str  (the folded spelling: `isin`, `clordid`, `executingtrader`)
  pub const fn is_known(&self) -> bool  (a member, not an `Other`)
  impl FromStr for IdType  (folds the text - trimmed, lower-cased, the `_`, `-`, space and `#` a spelling breaks it with dropped, ASCII letters, digits and `.` only, 1 to 64 bytes - and reads the member it or an alias names: `isinnumber`, `isincode` and `isinid` are `Isin`, `riccode`, `reuterscode` and `reuterssymbol` `Ric`, `bbg`, `bloombergsymbol`, `bloombergcode` and `bloombergticker` `Bloomberg`, `figicode` and `openfigi` `Figi`, `cusipnumber` `Cusip`, `sedolcode` `Sedol`, `ccypair` `Forex`, `xswxvalor` and `valorennummer` `Valor`, `sixsymbol` and `valorsymbol` `ExchSymb`; any other word is an `Other`; Err = Error)
  pub fn from_fix_security_source(code: char) -> Option<Self>  (the type one FIX `SecurityIDSource(22)` code names: `4` is `Isin`)
  pub fn fix_security_source(&self) -> Option<char>  (the code of a type FIX names; none for `Forex`, `Cfi` and every operation or party type)
  pub fn from_security_source(text: &str) -> Result<Self>  (a one-character FIX code, case-sensitive, else a code-set name with its punctuation, spacing and parenthesized remarks passed over, else the value kept as the word the `IdType` fold makes of it - `100` stays `100`, `Z` is `z`; refuses `ticker`, a member naming an operation's or a party's identifier, and a value no word holds)
  pub fn check_security(&self) -> Result<()>  (refuses `ticker`, the name a person knows an instrument by, which lives on `set_ticker`; `Error::InvalidRecord`)
  pub fn from_field_name(name: &str) -> Option<Self>  (the security type a field is named for: a leading `#` dropped, the name folded; the whole name as a type FIX names or `forex`, else `[security] + alias + [code | id | number]`; `ticker`, `symbol`, `symbolticker` and `leg*`, `underlying*`, `contra*`, `related*`, `benchmark*` name none)
  pub fn parents(&self) -> Cow<'static, [Self]>  (the types holding this type's parents, nearest first: `clordid`'s is `origclordid` alone, FIX's `OrigClOrdID(41)`; any other type the crate names, or a word ending in `id`, has `parent{type}` then `orig{type}` - `orderid`'s are `parentorderid` and `origorderid`; every other type, a parent type included, has none, so parentage never nests; borrowed for a type the crate names; `FixRegistry::parents_of` reads a dictionary's `FIX:parents` first)
  pub fn parent_of(&self) -> Option<(Self, usize)>  (the type this type is a parent of and its place among that type's `parents`: `origclordid` is `clordid`'s first, `parentorderid` `orderid`'s first and `origorderid` its second, `origtradeid` `tradeid`'s second; `origin...` and `original...` are words of their own and no parent; allocation-free)

  pub const fn is_party(&self) -> bool  (`Account`, `Party`, `UserId` or a `PartyRole(452)` role the crate names: what an operation's `partyids` hold)

  pub const fn value_dtype(&self) -> DataType  (the datatype a column of this type's values declares: `isin`, `cusip`, `sedol`, `figi`, `ric`, `bbg`, `ccy`, `country`, `cfi`, `forex`, `lei`, `dti`, `fisn`, `elf`, else `utf8`)
  pub fn max_value_width(&self) -> usize  (ISIN 12, CUSIP 9, SEDOL 7, FOREX 7, FIGI 12, WKN 6, CFI 6, VALOR 9, ISOCCY 3, ISOCTRY 2, BLOOMBERG `BBG_WIDTH` 32, RIC `RIC_WIDTH` 32, LEI `LEI_WIDTH` 20, DTI `DTI_WIDTH` 9, FISN `FISN_WIDTH` 35, ELF `ELF_WIDTH` 4, `IDENTIFIER_VALUE_WIDTH` 64 for every other type, FIX-named or not)
  pub fn rank(&self, value: &str) -> u8  (how real `value` is as a value of this type, from zero to `max_rank`: a registered code's `CodeValue::rank` - an ISIN, a CUSIP, a SEDOL, a FIGI, an LEI, a DTI, a CFI, an ISO currency or country code - read at the case the type stores it in, one for every other type, zero for a value the type refuses; what `Identifiers` decides a restated key by; allocation-free)
  pub fn max_rank(&self) -> u8  (the rank of a real value: `CodeValue::MAX_RANK` for an ISIN or a CFI, two; one for every other type)
  pub fn is_real(&self, value: &str) -> bool  (`rank(value) == max_rank()`)

  pub fn SymbolCode::instrument(text: &str) -> Option<SymbolCode>  (an instrument key shaped twelve, four and three ASCII letters and digits, each part read by its own type and none where it refuses it; what an `*INSTRUMENTID` security identifier's tail is read by)

### yggdryl::isin_registry  [rust/src/isin_registry.rs]  (the instrument registry: one row per ISIN and market of every security fact it is known by - the instrument's facts on every listing row, a listing's on its own - learned from and filled into market elements by the ordered lifecycle, filled from by every parse door off the snapshot it fixes as it opens, bound to a store it is loaded from and committed back to as one snapshot; a folded row derives, once its statement has landed, what its own facts imply where the statement left it empty - the national number its ISIN embeds, the currency of its market's country - and the seed, `config/isin/instruments.json` embedded through the crate's copy `isin_registry/seed.json` by the crate-private `isin_registry/seed.rs`, is the common instruments every process default starts from)
pub struct IsinEntry  (one listing of one instrument: `isin`, `updunix` (ns UTC of the statement that last moved a fact of the instrument, a stamp and never a gate), `firstunix` (ns UTC of the earliest event the registry learned the instrument from, moved back by an earlier learn; a stamp), `lastunix` (ns UTC of the latest event the registry learned the instrument from, moved by every learn; a stamp), the detailed `cficode`, the `countrycode` of issue stated beside the ISIN, the `forexcode` pair, the `underlyingisin` it is written on (a real ISIN other than its own), the `eusipacode` (its EUSIPA product category, an `Eusipa`), the `miccode` the row is the listing of (never `XXXX`; none for the unlisted row an ISIN holds alone while no market is known), the `ticker` (1 to 64 bytes, trimmed), the `fisn` (its ISO 18774 short name, a `Fisn`), the listing's `currency` (never `XXX`), the `origccy` the instrument was issued in (held only where stated, never derived), and one code per `SecurityIDSource(22)` type but the ISIN, at most `IsinRegistry::MAX_EQUIVALENTS`; the stamps, the CFI code, the country, the pair, the underlying, the product category, the short name, the origin currency and every code `IdType::is_listing` does not name are the instrument's - every listing row of an ISIN holds the same - and the market, the ticker, the currency and the listing codes the row's own; a row the registry folds fills, once the statement has landed, where the statement left it empty the code its ISIN embeds (`securityid::embedded`: `cusip`, `sedol`, `wkn`, `valor`; a type with no room passed over) and the currency of the country its market is in (`Mic::country`, `Country::currency`; a row of no market, or of a market of no single country, none) - a default never displaces a statement, a later statement replaces it, and an entry built and never folded derives nothing; Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub fn new(isin: Isin) -> Self
  pub fn dtype() -> DataType
  pub fn field() -> Field  (the non-null struct `isinregistry`: `isin`, `updunix` `datetime64(ns,"UTC")`, `firstunix` `datetime64(ns,"UTC")`, `lastunix` `datetime64(ns,"UTC")`, `cficode` `cfi`, `countrycode` `country`, `forexcode` `forex`, `underlyingisin` `isin`, `eusipacode` `int32`, `miccode` `mic`, `ticker` `utf8`, `fisn` `fisn`, `currency` `ccy`, `origccy` `ccy`, then one column per `SecurityIDSource(22)` type but the ISIN in code-set order, each `IdType::value_dtype`; 46 columns; the root declares `PARTITION:by` `["truncate(isin, 2)"]` - the ISIN's country prefix, Iceberg's native truncation of the key, storing no column, so a table created from the field (`PartitionSpec::from_schema`) holds one spec field `isin_truncate` `truncate[2]` and every listing of one ISIN in one partition, while a leaf stays flat and a plain folder, partitioning by marked columns alone, is written under the row declaring nothing - and `SORT:by` `["isin","miccode"]`, the snapshot's order)
  pub fn isin(&self) -> &Isin
  pub fn updunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (moved with a fact of the instrument, never by `lastunix` alone)
  pub fn firstunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the earliest `currunix` of an event the registry learned the instrument from; earlier-wins, an instrument fact; a stamp)
  pub fn lastunix(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the latest `currunix` of an event the registry learned the instrument from; later-wins, an instrument fact)
  pub fn cficode(&self) -> Option<&Cfi>
  pub fn countrycode(&self) -> Option<&Country>  (the country stated beside the ISIN; none in a folded row where it is the ISIN's own prefix)
  pub fn country(&self) -> Option<Country>  (the stated one, else the ISIN's prefix where ISO 3166 lists it; an agency prefix - `XS`, `EU`, `XT` - none)
  pub fn forexcode(&self) -> Option<&Forex>
  pub fn underlyingisin(&self) -> Option<&Isin>  (the instrument it is written on - FIX's underlying; real, never the row's own ISIN; an instrument fact every listing holds)
  pub fn eusipacode(&self) -> Option<Eusipa>  (the EUSIPA product category of the structured product, held by its shape whatever either map lists; an instrument fact every listing holds)
  pub fn miccode(&self) -> Option<&Mic>  (the market the row is the listing of; none for the unlisted row)
  pub fn ticker(&self) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn fisn(&self) -> Option<&Fisn>  (the ISO 18774 short name the instrument states - FIX's `FinancialInstrumentShortName(2737)`; an instrument fact every listing holds)
  pub fn currency(&self) -> Option<&Ccy>  (the stated one, else on a folded row the legal tender of its market's country; none for a row of no market)
  pub fn origccy(&self) -> Option<&Ccy>  (the currency the instrument was issued in, held only where stated - a golden file, the seed, a merge, a learn; an instrument fact every listing holds, never derived)
  pub fn get(&self, kind: &IdType) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&IdType, &str)>  (the codes in code-set order)
  pub fn with_updunix(self, unix: Option<i64>) -> Self
  pub fn with_firstunix(self, unix: Option<i64>) -> Self
  pub fn with_lastunix(self, unix: Option<i64>) -> Self

  pub fn with_fisn(self, name: Option<Fisn>) -> Self

  pub fn with_origccy(self, code: Option<Ccy>) -> Self  (`XXX` is stored as none)
  pub fn set_code(&mut self, kind: IdType, value: &str) -> Result<()>  (the type's value rule; refused for `isin`, `forex` (a column of its own), a type that is no FIX security source, and past `MAX_EQUIVALENTS`)
  pub fn try_with_code(self, kind: IdType, value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn into_scalar(&self) -> Scalar  (the named struct of the row's cells, a fact it does not state a null)
  pub fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>  (the named struct - a name it lacks a null - or the ordered row, through `field()`'s value door; refused as that door refuses, and past `MAX_EQUIVALENTS`)
pub enum MatchTier { Isin, Code(IdType), Symbology, Economic { similarity } }  (the tier `resolve` matched by; Clone, Debug, PartialEq)
pub enum Resolution<'a> { Matched { entry, tier, derived, listing }, Unmatched(Unmatched) }  (what `resolve` answers: the row, its tier, whether the ISIN was derived, whether the row's listing facts are the element's)
pub enum Unmatched { NoKey, UnknownIsin { stated }, NoCandidate, Ambiguous { tier, isins }, CfiConflict { stated, held, isin }, CurrencyConflict { stated, held, isin }, BelowThreshold { best, isin } }  (why `resolve` matched no row; Ambiguous lists its ISINs in ISIN order)
pub struct IsinRegistry  (one listing row per real ISIN - closing under a listed prefix (`CodeValue::is_real`) - and market, keyed by the ISIN's text, its listings in MIC order (one inline, so an instrument of one market costs its row alone) or the unlisted row alone while no market is known, beside the exact inverse index of the rows' tickers by ISIN and market, a ticker gated by the market its listing was stated on, and of every `LOOKUP_CODES` code, one slot per ISIN; the table is held shared behind two counted pointers - the crate-private `IsinTable` a parse door fixes once as it opens - so a write while a door holds it copies the table once; a value with no Clone: shared behind the caller's own lock (`FixCodec::with_isin_registry`, `from_env`); Debug, Default; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub const DEFAULT_MAX_INSTRUMENTS: usize = 16_384  (ISINs; each listing row at 8 KiB at most, 128 MiB for one listing each)
  pub const MAX_EQUIVALENTS: usize = 12
  pub const LOOKUP_CODES: [IdType; 20]  (the codes a lookup reads, in cascade order: cusip, sedol, wkn, valor, figi, bloomberg, ric, quik, exchsymb, cta, dutch, sicovam, belgian, common, opra, mktassigned, redpair, fim, umtf, dti - each indexed with one slot per ISIN; a currency, a country, an index, a synthetic code, an issuer's or a shared reference's none)
  pub const DEFAULT_ECONOMIC_THRESHOLD: f64 = 0.85
  pub fn new() -> Self  (empty, bound to no store)
  pub fn seeded() -> Self  (the seed: the common instruments `config/isin/instruments.json` states - stocks, funds and indexes by ISIN, ticker, market but an index's, currency, country, ISO 10962:2021 CFI and, where known, short name - embedded at build time, read once per process from the crate's copy `rust/src/isin_registry/seed.json` (`scripts/check_isin_seed.py --sync` writes it) through `extend_from_arrow_reader`'s column rule and folded by `merge`, so its derived facts are any row's; clean, bound to no store, sharing the one table until a write moves it; what `from_env` lays its store over)
  pub fn with_max_instruments(self, max: usize) -> Self
  pub fn max_instruments(&self) -> usize
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (the instruments: its ISINs)
  pub fn rows(&self) -> usize  (the listing rows: what `iter` walks and a commit writes)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn is_dirty(&self) -> bool  (whether the table moved since it was loaded or committed: a learn, a merge, a removal or a clear that moved something sets it - a learn moving `lastunix` alone included; a load or a `commit` clears it)
  pub fn get(&self, isin: &str) -> Option<&IsinEntry>  (the first listing in MIC order - the unlisted row where that is all - borrowed, allocation-free)
  pub fn listings(&self, isin: &str) -> &[IsinEntry]  (every listing row in MIC order; empty where unknown)
  pub fn get_listing(&self, isin: &str, market: &Mic) -> Option<&IsinEntry>  (the listing row on `market`)
  pub fn get_by_ticker(&self, ticker: &str, market: Option<&Mic>) -> Option<&IsinEntry>  (the ticker trimmed, through the ticker index: the one listing row stating the ticker on `market`, else - none there - the one stating it on no market; `market` unstated - none and `XXXX` - the one stating it on any; two rows answering is ambiguous and answers none)
  pub fn get_by_code(&self, kind: &IdType, code: &str, market: Option<&Mic>) -> Option<&IsinEntry>  (one of `LOOKUP_CODES`, read as its type stores it: the one ISIN holding it, then its row on `market`, else the one row holding the code, else its single row, else its first; two ISINs, a type no lookup reads or a value its type refuses answer none)
  pub fn resolve<E: Market + ?Sized>(&self, element: &E) -> Resolution<'_>  (the one waterfall a fill reads: a real stated ISIN decides alone and one the registry lacks ends the cascade; else each `LOOKUP_CODES` code in order, then the ticker on its market, the first naming two ISINs ending it; else, scored, the short name in the element's stated currency, at least `economic_threshold`, another origin currency or CFI category dropped)
  pub fn economic_threshold(&self) -> f64  (`DEFAULT_ECONOMIC_THRESHOLD` unless set)
  pub fn set_economic_threshold(&mut self, threshold: f64) -> Result<()>  (NaN and anything outside (0, 1] refused at `$.economic_threshold`)
  pub fn try_with_economic_threshold(self, threshold: f64) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn is_economic_match(&self) -> bool  (whether a fill takes an economic match; false unless set, and a parse never does)
  pub fn set_economic_match(&mut self, enabled: bool)
  pub fn with_economic_match(self, enabled: bool) -> Self
  pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &IsinEntry>  (every listing row, ISIN then MIC order)
  pub fn merge(&mut self, entry: IsinEntry) -> Result<bool>  (the update rule, no clock gate: a stated valid value fills a column the row lacks and replaces one it holds that differs; a code that is no real value of its type (`IdType::is_real`) is dropped with one warning per column; a CFI code compatible with the held one refines it and a contradicting one replaces it; a stated country that is the ISIN's own prefix takes a differing held one back; a stated underlying fills or replaces as an instrument fact, one that is no real ISIN dropped with one warning; a stated product category fills or replaces as an instrument fact, a number of no category's shape read as none with one warning; a stated short name fills or replaces as an instrument fact; the instrument facts fold into every listing row of the ISIN, the listing facts - ticker, currency, listing codes - into the row of the market the entry names, created where the ISIN has none there (the unlisted row taken over where that is all it holds), and where it names no market into the ISIN's single row, or none - one warning per column - of several; a new code type past `MAX_EQUIVALENTS` is passed over and moves nothing; `updunix` the later on every row, only where a fact moved; `lastunix` the later on every row, whatever moved; a row created or moved then derives its defaults where the statement left it empty (`IsinEntry`); a statement that moves nothing allocates nothing; refuses an ISIN that is not real - a `ZZ` number, a masked one, a typo - and a new ISIN past `max_instruments`)
  pub fn remove(&mut self, isin: &str) -> Vec<IsinEntry>  (every listing row of the ISIN, in MIC order; empty where unknown)
  pub fn remove_listing(&mut self, isin: &str, market: &Mic) -> Option<IsinEntry>  (the listing row on `market`; the instrument goes with its last listing)
  pub fn clear(&mut self)
  pub fn learn<E: Market + Event + ?Sized>(&mut self, event: &E) -> bool  (keyed by the stated ISIN alone - a real one, closing under a listed prefix, never a derivation - its market naming the listing row, at its `currunix`, which every learn states as `lastunix`, so an event stating the ISIN alone is learned - a new instrument's row made; reads the detailed CFI, the market but `XXXX`, the ticker, the currency but `XXX` unless the event holds a `forex` identifier (the dealt currency, not the listing's), the pair it states as a `forex` identifier, the short name it states as a `fisn` identifier, and each equivalent type's base key it states with a real value (`IdType::is_real`) - never one it only derived, a masked number or a typo, and never an underlying or a product category, which the FIX lifecycle learns beside a message (`FixMsg::stated_underlying_isin`, `FixMsg::stated_eusipa`, crate-private); a new ISIN past the bound skipped with one warning per registry)
  pub fn fill<E: Market + Element + ?Sized>(&self, element: &mut E) -> bool  (from the listing row its real ISIN - stated or derived - names, a miss ending the fill - the row of its market, else the single row, else the first for the instrument's facts alone - else its ticker's on its market (`get_by_ticker`), the ISIN derived first - over none, or over a number ranking below it, a masked one or a typo: each equivalent of a type it holds nothing of through `derive_securityid`, the listing codes only where its market - none and `XXXX` unstated - is the row's or either is unstated, the pair, the short name, the ticker on the same market, its CFI code where it states none or the row's refines it, the currency only where both markets are stated and equal, the ticker is the row's and it states none; finalized where anything moved; never a FIX field or the wire)
  pub fn enrich<E: Market + Event + ?Sized>(&mut self, event: &mut E) -> bool  (learn, then fill)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(reader: BatchReader) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (unbound and clean after the load)
  pub fn extend_from_arrow_reader(&mut self, reader: BatchReader) -> crate::arrow::Result<usize>  (each column resolved once from the schema - the registry's own name, any spelling of an identifier type (`RIC`, `BloombergSymbol`, `ISINCode`), a field name a type is spelled by, the market spellings `cfi`/`mic`/`symbol`/`tickersymbol`/`country`/`countryofissue`/`ccy`/`currencycode`/`forex`/`ccypair`, `underlyingisin` or any key naming an underlying's ISIN (`UnderlyingISIN`), `eusipa`/`eusipacode`/`eusipacategory`/`sspa`/`sspacode`/`sspacategory` for the product category (Euronext's `EUSIPA_Code`; numbers or text of one), `fisn`/`fisncode`/`shortname`/`financialinstrumentshortname` for the short name, `lastunix`/`lastseen`/`lastseenunix` for the instant last met - an unknown one ignored, two naming one fact refused naming both, no `isin` refused unless the stream has no column at all, the empty stream a missing store reads as; one cast plan, a code column's cells adopted as the landing proved them, a `utf8` one's through its type's rule, each row folded through `merge` as a statement - rows of one ISIN on several markets loading as several listings -, a refusal located `$[row].column`, a load past `max_instruments` refused; how many rows it read)
  pub fn extend_from_handle(&mut self, handle: &dyn IOBase) -> Result<usize>  (the handle's own record stream - an Arrow IPC file, Parquet, a folder of either, an object store - through `extend_from_arrow_reader`, binding nothing)
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<BatchReader>  (a snapshot stream of every listing row under `IsinEntry::field()` in ISIN then MIC order, one bounded batch at a time, which a learn while it streams does not move; written by `IOBase::write_arrow_reader`, a merge by `isin` upserting)

### yggdryl::isin_registry::env  [rust/src/isin_registry/env.rs]  (the process default: the seeded registry with the store the environment names laid over it, resolved once on first use and shared behind one lock, what `FixCodec::from_env` attaches and the caller commits)

### yggdryl::eusipa  [rust/src/eusipa.rs]  (the product category of a structured product: EUSIPA's four-digit code, which the SSPA's Swiss map numbers the same way; a root value type, not a DataType variant)
pub struct Eusipa(u16)  (four digits, 1000 to 2999 - the level, the group, the member - held by its shape whatever either map lists; Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd, Display the four digits, FromStr through `from_text`, serde the number, `TryFrom<u16>`, `From<Eusipa> for u16`; re-exported at the crate root)
  pub fn new(code: u16) -> Result<Self>  (refuses a number outside 1000..=2999: `invalid record value at $: expected a four-digit EUSIPA product category opening with 1, an investment product, or 2, a leverage product, got 3100`)
  pub fn from_text(text: &str) -> Result<Self>  (four ASCII digits once trimmed - no sign, no leading zero, no fraction - then `new`: `invalid record value at $: expected a four-digit EUSIPA product category, got "23x0"`)

  pub const fn group(&self) -> u8  (the first two digits)
  pub const fn level(&self) -> u8  (the first digit: 1 investment, 2 leverage)
  pub fn name(&self) -> Option<&'static str>  (the English name in EUSIPA's European Derivative Map of February 2024, 33 members; `1260` Express Certificates)
  pub fn sspa_name(&self) -> Option<&'static str>  (the English name in the SSPA Swiss Derivative Map, 2023 v.23/2 and 2026 v.26/1 one list, 24 members; `1260` Conditional Coupon Barrier Reverse Convertible)
  pub fn is_listed(&self) -> bool  (either map lists the code)

### yggdryl::limit  [rust/src/limit.rs]  (one price limit of a book side: its price, the quantity resting there, the entries resting there and whether the level trades; a root value type, not a DataType variant)
pub struct Limit { pub price: Option<Decimal>, pub quantity: Decimal, pub uuids: Vec<Uuid>, pub tradable: bool }  (price `None` on the one limit folding every unpriced entry; quantity the exact sum of the entries' quantities, an entry stating none adding nothing; uuids the entries' `curruuid`s in live order; tradable the entries' `tradable` facts folded with `any`, an entry counting unless it states `tradable = false` - true when one live entry at the level does not say it cannot trade, false only when every one says so, an entry stating nothing trading; Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq - order-sensitive on `uuids`)
  pub fn dtype() -> DataType  (`struct<price: decimal?, quantity: decimal, uuids: serie<uuid>, tradable: boolean>`, the uuid item `uuid` required)
